        IF esMod == 2 THEN TEXTOUT_P("Cohort: rows = exposure, columns = case or not.", G1, 6, 194, 1, esGRN, 308); END;
        IF esMod == 5 THEN TEXTOUT_P("Stratum " + STRING(esStr, 2, 0) + ": rows = exposure, cols = case/control.", G1, 6, 194, 1, esGRN, 308); END;
        IF esMod == 6 THEN TEXTOUT_P("Stratum " + STRING(esStr, 2, 0) + ": rows = exposure, cols = case or not.", G1, 6, 194, 1, esGRN, 308); END;
        IF esMod == 7 THEN TEXTOUT_P("Stratum " + STRING(esStr, 2, 0) + ": cases and person-time (PT) per row.", G1, 6, 194, 1, esGRN, 308); END;
        DEFAULT TEXTOUT_P("Columns = the truth (disease), rows = the test.", G1, 6, 194, 1, esGRN, 308);
    END;
    // no footer text: the soft-key strip (DRAWMENU) owns y 218-239
    BLIT_P(G0, 0, 0, G1);
END;

// The touch grid (port of ChiTable's csEntry).  Tap a cell, or move
// the red frame with the arrows and press Enter, to type that one
// cell's count.  The soft keys along the bottom:
//   Edit    type the selected cell's count       (Enter)
//   Values  all four counts in the module's form  (+); accepting the
//           form runs the analysis at once, as v1.4's form did
//   Clear   every cell back to 0
//   Cancel  back to the menu                      (Esc)
//   Run     check the table and go on to the results
// The labels never produce a key code (hp-prime-kit; ChiTable), so
// a tap in the strip, y >= 218, is read as slot MIN(6, IP(x/53.4)+1)
// and the keys stay as shortcuts.  Returns 1 if the table is ready,
// 0 if the user cancelled.  The counts are left in esCel.
es_Grid(gdTitle, gdRows, gdCols)
BEGIN
    LOCAL gdK, gdM, gdM1, gdGo, gdR, gdC;
    LOCAL gdV, gdOK, gdAct, gdLbl, gdHlp, gdRet;
    gdGo := 1; gdRet := 0;
    IF esSel < 1 OR esSel > 4 THEN esSel := 1; END;
    REPEAT gdK := GETKEY; UNTIL gdK == -1;      // drain the menu's key
    es_Drain();                                 // and its finger
    WHILE gdGo == 1 DO
        es_GridDraw(gdTitle, gdRows, gdCols);
        // the soft keys, painted on the SCREEN after the blit (DRAWMENU
        // draws on G0, not on G1), every frame
        DRAWMENU({"Edit", "Values", "Clear", "", "Cancel", "Run"});
        REPEAT
            gdK := GETKEY;
            gdM := MOUSE;
            gdM1 := gdM(1);
        UNTIL gdK > -1 OR SIZE(gdM1) > 0;
        // gdAct: 1 edit the selected cell, 2 the Values form, 3 clear,
        // 5 cancel, 6 run; 0 nothing
        gdAct := 0;
        gdR := IP((esSel - 1) / 2) + 1;         // the selected cell's row
        gdC := esSel - (gdR - 1) * 2;           // and column
        IF SIZE(gdM1) > 0 THEN
            es_Drain();                         // wait for the finger to lift
            IF gdM1(2) >= 218 THEN
                // the soft-key strip: six 53-px slots, left to right
                gdAct := MIN(6, IP(gdM1(1) / 53.4) + 1);
                IF gdAct == 4 THEN gdAct := 0; END;     // the blank key
            ELSE
                IF gdM1(1) >= esGX0 AND gdM1(2) >= esGY0 THEN
                    gdC := IP((gdM1(1) - esGX0) / esGCW) + 1;
                    gdR := IP((gdM1(2) - esGY0) / esGRH) + 1;
                    IF gdC >= 1 AND gdC <= 2 AND gdR >= 1 AND gdR <= 2 THEN
                        esSel := (gdR - 1) * 2 + gdC;
                        gdAct := 1;                     // a tap on a cell = Edit it
                    END;
                END;
            END;
        ELSE
            // the keys remain as shortcuts for the soft keys
            IF gdK == 7 THEN gdC := MAX(1, gdC - 1); END;
            IF gdK == 8 THEN gdC := MIN(2, gdC + 1); END;
            IF gdK == 2 THEN gdR := MAX(1, gdR - 1); END;
            IF gdK == 12 THEN gdR := MIN(2, gdR + 1); END;
            esSel := (gdR - 1) * 2 + gdC;
            IF gdK == 30 THEN gdAct := 1; END;        // Enter = Edit
            IF gdK == 50 THEN gdAct := 2; END;        // +     = Values
            IF gdK == 4 THEN gdAct := 5; END;         // Esc   = Cancel
        END;
        // ---- Edit: one cell, in a one-field form ----
        IF gdAct == 1 THEN
            gdR := IP((esSel - 1) / 2) + 1;
            gdC := esSel - (gdR - 1) * 2;
            gdV := esCel(esSel);
            gdLbl := es_CellName(esSel) + ":";
            gdHlp := gdRows(gdR) + " and " + gdCols(gdC) + ": a whole number, 0 or more";
            IF esMod == 7 AND gdC == 2 THEN gdHlp := gdRows(gdR) + " person-time: any number, 0 or more"; END;
            gdOK := INPUT({gdV}, "Count for one cell", {gdLbl}, {gdHlp});
            IF gdOK == 1 THEN
                IF gdV < 0 THEN
                    MSGBOX("A count cannot be negative.");
                ELSE
                    IF esMod == 7 AND gdC == 2 THEN
                        esCel(esSel) := gdV;          // v1.6: person-time, as typed
                    ELSE
                        IF FP(gdV) <> 0 THEN
                            MSGBOX("Counts are whole numbers: " + STRING(ROUND(gdV, 2), 2, 2) + " was rounded to the nearest one.");
                        END;
                        esCel(esSel) := ROUND(gdV, 0);
                    END;
                END;
            END;
        END;
        // ---- Values: the module's four-field form; OK runs at once ----
        IF gdAct == 2 THEN
            IF es_AskAll() == 1 THEN gdAct := 6; END;
        END;
        // ---- Clear: back to an empty table ----
        IF gdAct == 3 THEN
            esCel := {0, 0, 0, 0};
        END;
        // ---- Cancel: keep the counts, leave without computing ----
        IF gdAct == 5 THEN
            gdGo := 0; gdRet := 0;
        END;
        // ---- Run: an empty table cannot be analysed; say so and stay ----
        IF gdAct == 6 THEN
            IF esCel(1) + esCel(2) + esCel(3) + esCel(4) > 0 THEN
                gdGo := 0; gdRet := 1;
            ELSE
                MSGBOX("The table is empty: tap a cell and type its count first.");
            END;
        END;
        // the tap that closed a dialog can arrive again on the screen
        // underneath, so drain both before the next frame
        REPEAT gdK := GETKEY; UNTIL gdK == -1;
        es_Drain();
    END;
    RETURN gdRet;
END;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MODULE 1: 2×2 TABLE ANALYSIS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// One line of the small font inside the worked-arithmetic box, in
// colour alCol; returns the y of the next line (13 px down).
es_ALine(alS, alY, alCol)
BEGIN
    TEXTOUT_P(alS, G1, 14, alY, 1, alCol, 294);
    RETURN alY + 13;
END;

// Two pieces on one line if they fit (about 44 small-font characters
// across the box), otherwise the second piece on its own line under
// the first, indented.  Big counts make the formulas long.
es_Line2(l2A, l2B, l2Y, l2Col)
BEGIN
    LOCAL l2S;
    IF DIM(l2A) + DIM(l2B) <= 44 THEN
        l2S := l2A + l2B;                 // both pieces are text already
        TEXTOUT_P(l2S, G1, 14, l2Y, 1, l2Col, 294);
        RETURN l2Y + 13;
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P(l2A, G1, 14, l2Y, 1, l2Col, 294);
    l2S := "   " + l2B;
    TEXTOUT_P(l2S, G1, 14, l2Y + 13, 1, l2Col, 294);
    RETURN l2Y + 26;
END;

// The worked arithmetic behind one row of page 3 (v1.5): apId 1 =
// odds ratio, 2 = risk ratio, 3 = risk difference, with the
// student's own cells put into the formula, then the CI built step
// by step.  Drawn as an orange box on top of the page already in G1,
// then one blit.  It uses 1.96, as es_CalcORCI, es_CalcRRCI and
// es_CalcRDCI do, so the box ends on the very CI the page shows.
es_Arith(apId, apA, apB, apC, apD)
BEGIN
    LOCAL apY, apS, apSE, apLo, apHi, apV;
    LOCAL apP1, apP2, apN1, apN2, apH, apZ;
    LOCAL apA2, apB2, apC2, apD2;
    // v1.7b: every decimal on this box is rounded before STRING (see
    // es_FmtPct): a risk of 1/2000 or an OR of 0.004 printed in
    // scientific form on the Prime's rule.
    apZ := 1.96;
    apN1 := apA + apB;                  // row totals: exposed, unexposed
    apN2 := apC + apD;
    // the box: a two-pixel orange frame on light orange
    RECT_P(G1, 6, 34, 313, 214, esORNG, esLORN);
    RECT_P(G1, 7, 35, 312, 213, esORNG, esLORN);
    apY := 40;
    CASE
        IF apId == 1 THEN
            // ---- the odds ratio: cross-product ratio, Woolf CI ----
            TEXTOUT_P("Odds ratio, step by step", G1, 14, apY, 2, esORNG, 292);
            apY := apY + 20;
            apS := "OR = (a×d)/(b×c) = (" + es_Cnt(apA) + "×" + es_Cnt(apD) + ")/(" + es_Cnt(apB) + "×" + es_Cnt(apC) + ")";
            IF apB * apC > 0 THEN
                apV := (apA * apD) / (apB * apC);
                apY := es_Line2(apS, " = " + STRING(ROUND(apV, 2), 2, 2), apY, #000000h);
            ELSE
                apY := es_ALine(apS, apY, #000000h);
                apY := es_ALine("b×c = 0, so the odds ratio is undefined.", apY, esRED);
            END;
            apY := apY + 4;
            apY := es_ALine("95% CI, worked on the log scale (Woolf):", apY, esBLUE);
            // a zero cell makes ln(OR) or the SE infinite: add 0.5 to
            // every cell first (Haldane-Anscombe), as es_CalcORCI does
            apH := 0;
            IF apA == 0 OR apB == 0 OR apC == 0 OR apD == 0 THEN apH := 0.5; END;
            IF apH > 0 THEN
                apY := es_ALine("A zero cell: 0.5 is added to every cell.", apY, esRED);
            END;
            apA2 := apA + apH; apB2 := apB + apH; apC2 := apC + apH; apD2 := apD + apH;
            apV := (apA2 * apD2) / (apB2 * apC2);
            apY := es_ALine("ln OR = ln(" + STRING(ROUND(apV, 3), 2, 3) + ") = " + STRING(ROUND(LN(apV), 3), 2, 3), apY, #000000h);
            apY := es_ALine("SE = sqrt(1/a + 1/b + 1/c + 1/d)", apY, #000000h);
            apSE := (1/apA2 + 1/apB2 + 1/apC2 + 1/apD2)^0.5;
            apY := es_Line2("   = sqrt(1/" + es_Cnt(apA2) + " + 1/" + es_Cnt(apB2) + " + 1/" + es_Cnt(apC2) + " + 1/" + es_Cnt(apD2) + ")", " = " + STRING(ROUND(apSE, 3), 2, 3), apY, #000000h);
            apLo := LN(apV) - apZ * apSE;
            apHi := LN(apV) + apZ * apSE;
            apY := es_ALine("ln OR - and + 1.96 × SE: " + STRING(ROUND(apLo, 3), 2, 3) + " to " + STRING(ROUND(apHi, 3), 2, 3), apY, #000000h);
            apY := es_ALine("CI = exp(" + STRING(ROUND(apLo, 3), 2, 3) + ") to exp(" + STRING(ROUND(apHi, 3), 2, 3) + ")", apY, #000000h);
            apY := es_ALine("   = " + STRING(ROUND(EXP(apLo), 2), 2, 2) + " to " + STRING(ROUND(EXP(apHi), 2), 2, 2), apY, esGRN);
        END;
        IF apId == 2 THEN
            // ---- the risk ratio: risk in the exposed over risk in the
            //      unexposed, CI on the log scale ----
            TEXTOUT_P("Risk ratio, step by step", G1, 14, apY, 2, esORNG, 292);
            apY := apY + 20;
            apY := es_ALine("RR = [a/(a+b)] / [c/(c+d)]", apY, #000000h);
            IF apN1 > 0 AND apN2 > 0 THEN
                apP1 := apA / apN1;
                apP2 := apC / apN2;
                apY := es_ALine("   = (" + es_Cnt(apA) + "/" + es_Cnt(apN1) + ") / (" + es_Cnt(apC) + "/" + es_Cnt(apN2) + ")", apY, #000000h);
                IF apP2 > 0 THEN
                    apV := apP1 / apP2;
                    apY := es_Line2("   = " + STRING(ROUND(apP1, 3), 2, 3) + " / " + STRING(ROUND(apP2, 3), 2, 3), " = " + STRING(ROUND(apV, 3), 2, 3), apY, #000000h);
                    apY := apY + 4;
                    IF apP1 > 0 THEN
                        apY := es_ALine("95% CI, worked on the log scale:", apY, esBLUE);
                        apY := es_ALine("ln RR = ln(" + STRING(ROUND(apV, 3), 2, 3) + ") = " + STRING(ROUND(LN(apV), 3), 2, 3), apY, #000000h);
                        apY := es_ALine("SE = sqrt((1-p1)/a + (1-p2)/c)", apY, #000000h);
                        apSE := ((1 - apP1)/apA + (1 - apP2)/apC)^0.5;
                        apY := es_Line2("   = sqrt(" + STRING(ROUND(1 - apP1, 3), 2, 3) + "/" + es_Cnt(apA) + " + " + STRING(ROUND(1 - apP2, 3), 2, 3) + "/" + es_Cnt(apC) + ")", " = " + STRING(ROUND(apSE, 3), 2, 3), apY, #000000h);
                        apLo := LN(apV) - apZ * apSE;
                        apHi := LN(apV) + apZ * apSE;
                        apY := es_ALine("ln RR - and + 1.96 × SE: " + STRING(ROUND(apLo, 3), 2, 3) + " to " + STRING(ROUND(apHi, 3), 2, 3), apY, #000000h);
                        apY := es_ALine("CI = exp(" + STRING(ROUND(apLo, 3), 2, 3) + ") to exp(" + STRING(ROUND(apHi, 3), 2, 3) + ")", apY, #000000h);
                        apY := es_ALine("   = " + STRING(ROUND(EXP(apLo), 2), 2, 2) + " to " + STRING(ROUND(EXP(apHi), 2), 2, 2), apY, esGRN);
                    ELSE
                        apY := es_ALine("a = 0, so RR = 0 and ln(0) does not exist:", apY, esRED);
                        apY := es_ALine("there is no log-scale CI.", apY, esRED);
                    END;
                ELSE
                    apY := es_ALine("c = 0: no events among the unexposed, so", apY, esRED);
                    apY := es_ALine("RR = p1 / 0 is undefined (and has no CI).", apY, esRED);
                END;
            ELSE
                apY := es_ALine("A row total is 0: a risk needs people in", apY, esRED);
                apY := es_ALine("both rows, so RR is undefined.", apY, esRED);
            END;
        END;
        DEFAULT
            // ---- the risk difference: Wald CI on the risk scale ----
            TEXTOUT_P("Risk difference, step by step", G1, 14, apY, 2, esORNG, 292);
            apY := apY + 20;
            apY := es_ALine("RD = a/(a+b) - c/(c+d)", apY, #000000h);
            IF apN1 > 0 AND apN2 > 0 THEN
                apP1 := apA / apN1;
                apP2 := apC / apN2;
                apV := apP1 - apP2;
                apY := es_Line2("   = " + es_Cnt(apA) + "/" + es_Cnt(apN1) + " - " + es_Cnt(apC) + "/" + es_Cnt(apN2), " = " + STRING(ROUND(apP1, 3), 2, 3) + " - " + STRING(ROUND(apP2, 3), 2, 3), apY, #000000h);
                apY := es_ALine("   = " + STRING(ROUND(apV, 3), 2, 3) + "   (" + STRING(ROUND(100 * apV, 1), 2, 1) + " %)", apY, #000000h);
                apY := apY + 4;
                apY := es_ALine("95% CI (Wald, on the risk scale):", apY, esBLUE);
                apY := es_ALine("SE = sqrt(p1(1-p1)/n1 + p2(1-p2)/n2)", apY, #000000h);
                apSE := (apP1 * (1 - apP1) / apN1 + apP2 * (1 - apP2) / apN2)^0.5;
                apY := es_Line2("   = sqrt(" + STRING(ROUND(apP1, 3), 2, 3) + "×" + STRING(ROUND(1 - apP1, 3), 2, 3) + "/" + es_Cnt(apN1), " + " + STRING(ROUND(apP2, 3), 2, 3) + "×" + STRING(ROUND(1 - apP2, 3), 2, 3) + "/" + es_Cnt(apN2) + ")", apY, #000000h);
                apY := es_ALine("   = " + STRING(ROUND(apSE, 3), 2, 3), apY, #000000h);
                apLo := apV - apZ * apSE;
                apHi := apV + apZ * apSE;
                apY := es_ALine("RD - and + 1.96 × SE: " + STRING(ROUND(apLo, 3), 2, 3) + " to " + STRING(ROUND(apHi, 3), 2, 3), apY, #000000h);
                // es_CalcRDCI keeps the interval inside -1 .. 1; say so
                // when that cut happens
                apS := "";
                IF apLo < -1 OR apHi > 1 THEN apS := " (cut at 100 %)"; END;
                IF apLo < -1 THEN apLo := -1; END;
                IF apHi > 1 THEN apHi := 1; END;
                apY := es_ALine("CI as %: " + STRING(ROUND(100 * apLo, 1), 2, 1) + " % to " + STRING(ROUND(100 * apHi, 1), 2, 1) + " %" + apS, apY, esGRN);
            ELSE
                apY := es_ALine("A row total is 0: a risk needs people in", apY, esRED);
                apY := es_ALine("both rows, so RD is undefined.", apY, esRED);
            END;
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P("any key or tap = close", G1, 14, 200, 1, esFOOT, 294);
    BLIT_P(G0, 0, 0, G1);
END;

// PAGE 4 of module 1 (v1.5): a mosaic of the 2×2, ported from
// ChiTable's csMosaic.  One bar per row (exposure group), its WIDTH
// the row's share of N; the two tiles in it are the outcome columns,
// their HEIGHTS the row percentages (Outcome+ on top).  If exposure
// and outcome were independent every bar would look like the grey
// "all" bar on the right.  Tile colour is the adjusted residual
// (Haberman): blue = more than expected, red = fewer, |adj| >= 2,
// darker beyond |4| (the shading Friendly and R's mosaicplot use).
// A tile is a hit box: a tap on it (or Up/Down) puts its O, E and
// row % in the panel underneath (esTile).
es_Mosaic(mcA, mcB, mcC, mcD)
BEGIN
    LOCAL moO, moRT, moCT, moN, moI, moR;
    LOCAL moC, moX, moY, moW, moH, moV;
    LOCAL moAW, moAH, moCol, moFill, moXR, moS;
    LOCAL moPL, moPR, moPT, moPB, moGap, moRN;
    LOCAL moCN;
    moO := {mcA, mcB, mcC, mcD};
    moRT := {mcA + mcB, mcC + mcD};      // row totals (exposed, unexposed)
    moCT := {mcA + mcC, mcB + mcD};      // column totals (outcome +, -)
    moN := mcA + mcB + mcC + mcD;
    moRN := {"Exposed+", "Exposed-"};
    moCN := {"Outcome+", "Outcome-"};
    // expected counts E = row total × column total / N, and the
    // adjusted residual (O - E) / sqrt(E (1 - R/N)(1 - C/N))
    FOR moI FROM 1 TO 4 DO
        moR := IP((moI - 1) / 2) + 1;
        moC := moI - (moR - 1) * 2;
        esE(moI) := moRT(moR) * moCT(moC) / moN;
        moV := esE(moI) * (1 - moRT(moR) / moN) * (1 - moCT(moC) / moN);
        esAdj(moI) := 0;                 // an empty row or column: no residual
        IF moV > 0 THEN esAdj(moI) := (moO(moI) - esE(moI)) / moV^0.5; END;
    END;
    // geometry: the bars from x 12 to 262, the "all" bar 274..306,
    // tiles from y 30 to 146, 4 px gaps
    moPL := 12; moPR := 262; moPT := 30; moPB := 146; moGap := 4;
    moAW := (moPR - moPL) - moGap;
    moAH := (moPB - moPT) - moGap;
    es_DrawHeader("MOSAIC PLOT", "6/7");
    moX := moPL;
    FOR moR FROM 1 TO 2 DO
        moW := IP(moAW * moRT(moR) / moN + 0.5);
        moY := moPT;
        FOR moC FROM 1 TO 2 DO
            moI := (moR - 1) * 2 + moC;
            moH := 0;
            IF moRT(moR) > 0 THEN moH := IP(moAH * moO(moI) / moRT(moR) + 0.5); END;
            // colour by the adjusted residual
            moCol := esGRAY; moFill := esLGRY;
            IF esAdj(moI) >= 2 THEN moCol := esBLUE; moFill := esLBLU; END;
            IF esAdj(moI) >= 4 THEN moFill := esMBLU; END;
            IF esAdj(moI) <= -2 THEN moCol := esRED; moFill := esLRED; END;
            IF esAdj(moI) <= -4 THEN moFill := esMRED; END;
            IF moI == esTile THEN moCol := esRED; END;
            IF moH > 0 AND moW > 0 THEN
                RECT_P(G1, moX, moY, moX + moW, moY + moH, moCol, moFill);
                IF moI == esTile THEN
                    RECT_P(G1, moX + 1, moY + 1, moX + moW - 1, moY + moH - 1, moCol, moFill);
                END;
                es_AddHit(moI, moX, moY, moX + moW, moY + moH);
                IF moW >= 40 AND moH >= 12 THEN
                    TEXTOUT_P(es_CellName(moI) + " = " + STRING(moO(moI), 2, 0), G1, moX + 3, moY + 2, 1, #000000h, moW - 4);
                END;
            END;
            moY := moY + moH + moGap;
        END;
        IF moW > 0 THEN
            // the row's name and size under its bar; just the name if
            // the bar is too narrow for both
            moS := moRN(moR) + " (" + STRING(moRT(moR), 2, 0) + ")";
            IF DIM(moS) * 7 > moW THEN moS := moRN(moR); END;
            TEXTOUT_P(moS, G1, moX + 2, moPB + 3, 1, #000000h, moW);
        END;
        moX := moX + moW + moGap;
    END;
    // ---- the reference bar: the column shares of all N ----
    moXR := 274;
    moY := moPT;
    FOR moC FROM 1 TO 2 DO
        moH := IP(moAH * moCT(moC) / moN + 0.5);
        IF moH > 0 THEN
            RECT_P(G1, moXR, moY, moXR + 32, moY + moH, esGRAY, esLGRY);
            IF moH >= 12 THEN TEXTOUT_P(STRING(100 * moCT(moC) / moN, 2, 0) + "%", G1, moXR + 3, moY + 2, 1, esGRAY, 28); END;
        END;
        moY := moY + moH + moGap;
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P("all", G1, moXR + 4, moPB + 3, 1, esGRAY, 30);
    TEXTOUT_P("top = Outcome+, height = row %, width = row N", G1, 6, 160, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    // ---- the panel: the tapped tile's numbers ----
    RECT_P(G1, 4, 174, 315, 215, esORNG, esLORN);
    IF esTile >= 1 AND esTile <= 4 THEN
        moR := IP((esTile - 1) / 2) + 1;
        moC := esTile - (moR - 1) * 2;
        moS := es_CellName(esTile) + ": " + moRN(moR) + ", " + moCN(moC) + ": O = " + STRING(moO(esTile), 2, 0) + ", E = " + STRING(ROUND(esE(esTile), 2), 2, 2);
        TEXTOUT_P(moS, G1, 10, 177, 1, #000000h, 302);
        IF moRT(moR) > 0 THEN
            moS := "row % = " + STRING(ROUND(100 * moO(esTile) / moRT(moR), 1), 2, 1) + " %   (all rows: " + STRING(ROUND(100 * moCT(moC) / moN, 1), 2, 1) + " %)";
        ELSE
            moS := "row % undefined: this row is empty";
        END;
        TEXTOUT_P(moS, G1, 10, 190, 1, esMAG, 302);
        moS := "adj. residual = " + es_Sgn(esAdj(esTile), 2);
        IF esAdj(esTile) >= 2 THEN moS := moS + ": MORE than expected"; END;
        IF esAdj(esTile) <= -2 THEN moS := moS + ": FEWER than expected"; END;
        IF ABS(esAdj(esTile)) < 2 THEN moS := moS + ": about as expected"; END;
        TEXTOUT_P(moS, G1, 10, 203, 1, esBLUE, 302);
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P("Tap a tile (or Up/Down): its O, E and row %.", G1, 10, 177, 1, #000000h, 302);
        TEXTOUT_P("blue = more than expected, red = fewer,", G1, 10, 190, 1, esGRAY, 302);
        TEXTOUT_P("when |adjusted residual| >= 2; grey = neither", G1, 10, 203, 1, esGRAY, 302);
    END;
    es_Foot("tap a tile = numbers, " + CHAR(8592) + "/" + CHAR(8594) + " = page, Esc = menu");
END;

// PAGE 1 of module 1 (v1.6): THE THREE READINGS.  The same four
// counts answer three different questions, depending on how the
// rows were sampled (ConTable2's idea):
//   Cohort      rows = exposure groups followed forward: risks are
//               real, so RR, OR, RD and the attributable fractions;
//   Diagnostic  rows = test result, columns = the truth: sens, spec,
//               PPV, NPV, the likelihood ratios, Youden, error rate;
//   Paired      rows = rater A, columns = rater B (or before/after on
//               the same people): kappa, McNemar, Yule's Q and Y.
// Three tabs across the top; a tap on a tab (or Up/Down) changes the
// reading (esRd).  The tabs are hit boxes 1, 2, 3.  The cohort ratios
// are the ones module 1 already stored in EP_RR, EP_OR, EP_RD.
es_ReadPage(rpA, rpB, rpC, rpD)
BEGIN
    LOCAL rpN, rpI, rpX, rpY, rpT, rpS;
    LOCAL rpRL, rpCL, rpSf, rpR1, rpR2, rpC1;
    LOCAL rpC2, rpV, rpW, rpSe, rpSp, rpPo;
    LOCAL rpPe, rpK, rpMc, rpQ, rpYu;
    rpN := rpA + rpB + rpC + rpD;
    rpR1 := rpA + rpB; rpR2 := rpC + rpD;
    rpC1 := rpA + rpC; rpC2 := rpB + rpD;
    es_DrawHeader("THREE READINGS", "1/7");
    // ---- the three tabs: the chosen one filled blue ----
    rpT := {"Cohort", "Diagnostic", "Paired"};
    FOR rpI FROM 1 TO 3 DO
        rpX := 2 + (rpI - 1) * 106;
        IF rpI == esRd THEN
            RECT_P(G1, rpX, 27, rpX + 104, 43, esBLUE, esBLUE);
            TEXTOUT_P(rpT(rpI), G1, rpX + 10, 30, 2, #FFFFFFh, 92);
        ELSE
            RECT_P(G1, rpX, 27, rpX + 104, 43, esGRAY, esLGRY);
            TEXTOUT_P(rpT(rpI), G1, rpX + 10, 30, 2, esBLUE, 92);
        END;
        es_AddHit(rpI, rpX, 25, rpX + 104, 45);
    END;
    // ---- the same four counts, labelled for this reading ----
    CASE
        IF esRd == 2 THEN
            rpRL := {"Test+", "Test-"}; rpCL := {"Disease+", "Disease-"};
            rpSf := {" TP", " FP", " FN", " TN"};
        END;
        IF esRd == 3 THEN
            rpRL := {"A: yes", "A: no"}; rpCL := {"B: yes", "B: no"};
            rpSf := {" agree", " differ", " differ", " agree"};
        END;
        DEFAULT
            rpRL := {"Exposed+", "Exposed-"}; rpCL := {"Outcome+", "Outcome-"};
            rpSf := {"", "", "", ""};
    END;
    RECT_P(G1, 4, 46, 315, 85, esGRID, esLBLU);
    TEXTOUT_P(rpCL(1), G1, 96, 48, 1, esBLUE, 90);
    TEXTOUT_P(rpCL(2), G1, 196, 48, 1, esBLUE, 84);
    TEXTOUT_P("total", G1, 284, 48, 1, esGRAY, 30);
    rpV := {rpA, rpB, rpC, rpD};
    rpS := {"a", "b", "c", "d"};
    FOR rpI FROM 1 TO 2 DO
        rpY := 48 + 12 * rpI;
        TEXTOUT_P(rpRL(rpI), G1, 10, rpY, 1, esBLUE, 82);
        TEXTOUT_P(rpS(2 * rpI - 1) + " = " + STRING(rpV(2 * rpI - 1), 2, 0) + rpSf(2 * rpI - 1), G1, 96, rpY, 1, #000000h, 96);
        TEXTOUT_P(rpS(2 * rpI) + " = " + STRING(rpV(2 * rpI), 2, 0) + rpSf(2 * rpI), G1, 196, rpY, 1, #000000h, 84);
        TEXTOUT_P(STRING(rpV(2 * rpI - 1) + rpV(2 * rpI), 2, 0), G1, 284, rpY, 1, esGRAY, 32);
    END;
    rpY := 90;
    // ---- COHORT: risks, ratios, attributable fractions ----
    IF esRd == 1 THEN
        IF rpR1 > 0 THEN rpS := es_FmtPct(rpA / rpR1); ELSE rpS := "undef (row empty)"; END;
        rpY := es_ALine("Risk if exposed, a/(a+b) = " + rpS, rpY, #000000h);
        IF rpR2 > 0 THEN rpS := es_FmtPct(rpC / rpR2); ELSE rpS := "undef (row empty)"; END;
        rpY := es_ALine("Risk if unexposed, c/(c+d) = " + rpS, rpY, #000000h);
        // v1.7c: es_EqTxt -- "RR = Re/Ru < 0.001", never "= < 0.001"
        rpY := es_ALine(es_EqTxt("RR = Re/Ru", es_FmtNum(EP_RR, 3)) + "   " + es_FmtCI(EP_RR_CI, 0), rpY, esGRN);
        rpY := es_ALine(es_EqTxt("OR = ad/bc", es_FmtNum(EP_OR, 3)) + "   " + es_FmtCI(EP_OR_CI, 0), rpY, esGRN);
        // v1.7: the conditional MLE beside it, in the exact colour
        // (orange); page 7 shows it and says why the two differ
        rpS := "  cond. MLE OR = " + es_CORTxt();
        IF DIM(rpS) <= 37 THEN rpS := rpS + ": page 7"; END;
        rpY := es_ALine(rpS, rpY, esORNG);
        rpY := es_ALine("RD = Re - Ru = " + es_FmtPct(EP_RD) + "   " + es_FmtCI(EP_RD_CI, 1), rpY, esGRN);
        // the attributable fractions, as module 2 defines them
        rpS := "undef";
        IF rpR1 > 0 AND rpR2 > 0 AND rpA > 0 THEN
            rpS := es_FmtPct((rpA / rpR1 - rpC / rpR2) / (rpA / rpR1));
        END;
        rpY := es_ALine("AF exposed = (Re - Ru)/Re = " + rpS, rpY, #000000h);
        rpS := "undef";
        IF rpR2 > 0 AND rpC1 > 0 THEN
            rpS := es_FmtPct((rpC1 / rpN - rpC / rpR2) / (rpC1 / rpN));
        END;
        rpY := es_ALine("AF population = (Rt - Ru)/Rt = " + rpS, rpY, #000000h);
        // v1.7: the two v1.6 notes share one line, to make room for
        // the conditional MLE above
        rpY := es_ALine("Rows = exposure: risks real; worked: page 5.", rpY, esGRAY);
    END;
    // ---- DIAGNOSTIC: columns are the truth ----
    IF esRd == 2 THEN
        rpSe := -1; rpSp := -1;
        rpS := "undef (no Disease+)";
        IF rpC1 > 0 THEN
            rpSe := rpA / rpC1;
            rpS := es_FmtPct(rpSe) + "  " + es_FmtCI(es_WilsonCI(rpA, rpC1), 1);
        END;
        rpY := es_ALine("Sensitivity a/(a+c) = " + rpS, rpY, esGRN);
        rpS := "undef (no Disease-)";
        IF rpC2 > 0 THEN
            rpSp := rpD / rpC2;
            rpS := es_FmtPct(rpSp) + "  " + es_FmtCI(es_WilsonCI(rpD, rpC2), 1);
        END;
        rpY := es_ALine("Specificity d/(b+d) = " + rpS, rpY, esGRN);
        // v1.7: the exact (Clopper-Pearson) CIs of the same two, in
        // orange, under Wilson's (esX1 is worked out once per table)
        IF esBinOK == 1 THEN
            rpS := "undef"; rpW := "undef";
            IF rpC1 > 0 THEN rpS := es_FmtCI({esX1(1), esX1(2)}, 1); END;
            IF rpC2 > 0 THEN rpW := es_FmtCI({esX1(3), esX1(4)}, 1); END;
            rpY := es_ALine("Exact: sens " + rpS + ", spec " + rpW, rpY, esORNG);
        ELSE
            rpY := es_ALine("Exact CIs unavailable (BINOMIAL check failed)", rpY, esGRAY);
        END;
        rpS := "undef"; rpW := "undef";
        IF rpR1 > 0 THEN rpS := es_FmtPct(rpA / rpR1); END;
        IF rpR2 > 0 THEN rpW := es_FmtPct(rpD / rpR2); END;
        rpY := es_ALine("PPV a/(a+b) = " + rpS + ",  NPV d/(c+d) = " + rpW, rpY, #000000h);
        rpS := "undef"; rpW := "undef";
        IF rpSe >= 0 AND rpSp >= 0 THEN
            IF rpSp < 1 THEN rpS := es_FmtNum(rpSe / (1 - rpSp), 2); END;
            IF rpSp > 0 THEN rpW := es_FmtNum((1 - rpSe) / rpSp, 3); END;
        END;
        // v1.7c: es_EqTxt on both ratios ("LR- < 0.001" for a near-perfect test)
        rpY := es_ALine(es_EqTxt("LR+ = sens/(1-spec)", rpS) + es_EqTxt(",  LR-", rpW), rpY, #000000h);
        rpS := "undef";
        IF rpSe >= 0 AND rpSp >= 0 THEN rpS := STRING(ROUND(rpSe + rpSp - 1, 3), 2, 3); END;
        rpY := es_ALine("Youden J = sens + spec - 1 = " + rpS, rpY, #000000h);
        rpY := es_ALine("Accuracy = " + es_FmtPct((rpA + rpD) / rpN) + ", error rate (b+c)/N = " + es_FmtPct((rpB + rpC) / rpN), rpY, #000000h);
        rpY := es_ALine("Prevalence (a+c)/N = " + es_FmtPct(rpC1 / rpN) + ": PPV, NPV hold here", rpY, #000000h);
        rpY := es_ALine("Columns = the truth; module 3 has more pages.", rpY, esGRAY);
    END;
    // ---- PAIRED: agreement, and change between the two ratings ----
    IF esRd == 3 THEN
        rpPo := (rpA + rpD) / rpN;
        rpPe := (rpR1 / rpN) * (rpC1 / rpN) + (rpR2 / rpN) * (rpC2 / rpN);
        rpK := 1;
        IF rpPe < 1 THEN rpK := (rpPo - rpPe) / (1 - rpPe); END;
        rpY := es_ALine("Agreement po = (a+d)/N = " + es_FmtPct(rpPo), rpY, #000000h);
        rpY := es_ALine("Agreement by chance pe = " + es_FmtPct(rpPe), rpY, #000000h);
        rpY := es_ALine("Kappa = (po-pe)/(1-pe) = " + STRING(ROUND(rpK, 3), 2, 3) + " (" + es_KapTxt(rpK) + ")", rpY, esGRN);
        rpMc := es_McNemar(rpB, rpC);
        IF rpMc(1) >= 0 THEN
            rpY := es_ALine("McNemar χ² (cont. corr.) = " + STRING(ROUND(rpMc(1), 3), 2, 3) + ", " + es_PTxt(rpMc(2), 4), rpY, esGRN);
        ELSE
            rpY := es_ALine("McNemar: undefined (b + c = 0)", rpY, esGRAY);
        END;
        rpY := es_ALine("  only the pairs that differ: b = " + STRING(rpB, 2, 0) + ", c = " + STRING(rpC, 2, 0), rpY, esGRAY);
        rpS := "undef";
        IF rpA * rpD + rpB * rpC > 0 THEN rpS := STRING(ROUND((rpA * rpD - rpB * rpC) / (rpA * rpD + rpB * rpC), 3), 2, 3); END;
        rpY := es_ALine("Yule's Q = (ad-bc)/(ad+bc) = " + rpS, rpY, #000000h);
        rpQ := (rpA * rpD)^0.5; rpYu := (rpB * rpC)^0.5;
        rpS := "undef";
        IF rpQ + rpYu > 0 THEN rpS := STRING(ROUND((rpQ - rpYu) / (rpQ + rpYu), 3), 2, 3); END;
        rpY := es_ALine("Yule's Y (on sqrt(ad), sqrt(bc)) = " + rpS, rpY, #000000h);
        rpY := es_ALine("Disagreement (error) rate (b+c)/N = " + es_FmtPct((rpB + rpC) / rpN), rpY, #000000h);
        rpY := es_ALine("Same pairs rated twice: McNemar, not χ².", rpY, esGRAY);
    END;
    es_Foot("tap a tab = reading, " + CHAR(8592) + "/" + CHAR(8594) + " = page, Esc = menu");
END;

// v1.6: the hypergeometric distribution behind Fisher's exact test,
// worked out once per visit to page 4 and kept in esQP / esQK.  With
// the row and column totals fixed, the table is decided by a alone,
// and P(a') = C(a+c, a') C(b+d, a+b-a') / C(N, a+b), computed in log
// space with es_lngamma exactly as es_FisherExact does, so the
// magenta bars add up to the very p on page 3.  Bars with P below
// 1E-7 of the tallest are too small to draw; they are left out (and
// counted in esQCut), but the observed table is always kept.
//   esQP  P(a') per bar     esQK  1 = counted in the two-sided p
//   esQLo the a' of bar 1   esQN  number of bars
//   esQP0 P(observed)       esQPv the two-sided p
//   esQOK 0 not worked out, 1 ready, 2 N > 2000 (not computed)
es_HyperFill(hfA, hfB, hfC, hfD)
BEGIN
    LOCAL hfR1, hfR2, hfC1, hfC2, hfN, hfI;
    LOCAL hfMin, hfMax, hfCst, hfL0, hfMx, hfS;
    LOCAL hfLo, hfHi, hfLL, hfNA, hfOb, hfAp;
    hfR1 := hfA + hfB; hfR2 := hfC + hfD;
    hfC1 := hfA + hfC; hfC2 := hfB + hfD;
    hfN := hfR1 + hfR2;
    esQOK := 2;
    IF hfN > 2000 THEN RETURN 0; END;
    hfCst := es_lngamma(hfR1+1) + es_lngamma(hfR2+1) + es_lngamma(hfC1+1) + es_lngamma(hfC2+1) - es_lngamma(hfN+1);
    hfL0 := hfCst - es_lngamma(hfA+1) - es_lngamma(hfB+1) - es_lngamma(hfC+1) - es_lngamma(hfD+1);
    hfMin := MAX(0, hfR1 - hfC2);
    hfMax := MIN(hfR1, hfC1);
    hfNA := hfMax - hfMin + 1;
    // every a' from hfMin to hfMax: its log probability, the largest,
    // and the sum of those no more likely than the observed table
    hfLL := MAKELIST(0, K, 1, hfNA, 1);
    hfMx := hfL0; hfS := 0;
    FOR hfI FROM 1 TO hfNA DO
        hfAp := hfMin + hfI - 1;
        hfLL(hfI) := hfCst - es_lngamma(hfAp+1) - es_lngamma(hfR1-hfAp+1) - es_lngamma(hfC1-hfAp+1) - es_lngamma(hfC2-hfR1+hfAp+1);
        IF hfLL(hfI) > hfMx THEN hfMx := hfLL(hfI); END;
        IF hfLL(hfI) <= hfL0 + 0.00000001 THEN hfS := hfS + EXP(hfLL(hfI)); END;
    END;
    // trim the far tails (ln 1E-7 = -16.1), never past the observed bar
    hfOb := hfA - hfMin + 1;
    hfLo := 1;
    WHILE hfLo < hfOb AND hfLL(hfLo) < hfMx - 16.1 DO hfLo := hfLo + 1; END;
    hfHi := hfNA;
    WHILE hfHi > hfOb AND hfLL(hfHi) < hfMx - 16.1 DO hfHi := hfHi - 1; END;
    esQN := hfHi - hfLo + 1;
    esQLo := hfMin + hfLo - 1;
    esQCut := hfNA - esQN;
    esQP := MAKELIST(0, K, 1, esQN, 1);
    esQK := MAKELIST(0, K, 1, esQN, 1);
    FOR hfI FROM 1 TO esQN DO
        esQP(hfI) := EXP(hfLL(hfLo + hfI - 1));
        IF hfLL(hfLo + hfI - 1) <= hfL0 + 0.00000001 THEN esQK(hfI) := 1; END;
    END;
    esQP0 := EXP(hfL0);
    esQPv := MIN(1, hfS);
    esQSel := hfA - esQLo + 1;           // the panel opens on your own table
    esQOK := 1;
    RETURN 1;
END;

// PAGE 4 of module 1 (v1.6): FISHER'S TEST AS A COUNTING ARGUMENT.
// One bar for every table with your row and column totals, its
// height P(a'): your table in red, every table no more likely than
// yours in magenta (their sum IS the two-sided p), the more likely
// ones in pale grey.  A thin strip under the axis repeats the colours
// so that bars too short to see still show whether they count.  A
// tap on the plot (hit box 20), or Up/Down, picks a bar; the orange
// corner panel shows that table, its P and whether it counts.  The
// panel sits in the corner away from the peak.  Plot box: x from esPL
// (34) to esPR (306), the same constants the tap handler uses; y
// from 46 down to the axis at 150.
es_HyperPage(hgA, hgB, hgC, hgD)
BEGIN
    LOCAL hgI, hgX1, hgX2, hgH, hgCol, hgMx;
    LOCAL hgTop, hgStp, hgDec, hgV, hgY, hgPt;
    LOCAL hgOb, hgMd, hgPx, hgS, hgAp, hgTk;
    LOCAL hgXc, hgLb;
    IF esQOK == 0 THEN es_HyperFill(hgA, hgB, hgC, hgD); END;
    es_DrawHeader("FISHER: EVERY TABLE", "4/7");
    IF esQOK <> 1 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("N > 2000: Fisher's exact test is not", G1, 12, 44, 1, #000000h, 296);
        TEXTOUT_P("computed here (too many tables to add);", G1, 12, 58, 1, #000000h, 296);
        TEXTOUT_P("use the chi-square on page 3.", G1, 12, 72, 1, #000000h, 296);
        es_DrawFooter(1);
        RETURN 0;
    END;
    // ---- the y scale: the tallest bar at about 93 % of the box ----
    hgMx := 0; hgMd := 1;
    FOR hgI FROM 1 TO esQN DO
        IF esQP(hgI) > hgMx THEN hgMx := esQP(hgI); hgMd := hgI; END;
    END;
    hgTop := hgMx * 1.08;
    hgStp := es_Nice(hgMx / 4);
    hgDec := MAX(0, -FLOOR(LOG(hgStp)));
    hgV := 0;
    WHILE hgV <= hgTop DO
        hgY := 150 - IP(hgV / hgTop * 104 + 0.5);
        LINE_P(G1, esPL, hgY, esPR, hgY, esGRID);
        TEXTOUT_P(STRING(hgV, 2, hgDec), G1, 0, hgY - 5, 1, esGRAY, 33);
        hgV := hgV + hgStp;
    END;
    // ---- the bars ----
    hgPt := (esPR - esPL) / esQN;            // pixels per bar (can be < 1)
    hgOb := hgA - esQLo + 1;                 // your table's bar
    FOR hgI FROM 1 TO esQN DO
        hgX1 := esPL + IP((hgI - 1) * hgPt);
        hgX2 := esPL + IP(hgI * hgPt) - 1;
        IF hgPt >= 4 THEN hgX1 := hgX1 + 1; END;      // a gap between wide bars
        IF hgX2 < hgX1 THEN hgX2 := hgX1; END;
        hgH := IP(esQP(hgI) / hgTop * 104 + 0.5);
        hgCol := #C8C8C8h;                             // more likely than yours
        IF esQK(hgI) == 1 THEN hgCol := esMAG; END;    // counted in the p
        IF hgI == hgOb THEN hgCol := esRED; END;       // your table
        IF hgH > 0 THEN RECT_P(G1, hgX1, 150 - hgH, hgX2, 150, hgCol, hgCol); END;
        RECT_P(G1, hgX1, 152, hgX2, 154, hgCol, hgCol);
        IF hgI == esQSel THEN
            // a small black arrow over the picked bar
            hgXc := IP((hgX1 + hgX2) / 2);
            LINE_P(G1, hgXc, 141 - hgH, hgXc, 147 - hgH, #000000h);
            LINE_P(G1, hgXc - 2, 145 - hgH, hgXc, 147 - hgH, #000000h);
            LINE_P(G1, hgXc + 2, 145 - hgH, hgXc, 147 - hgH, #000000h);
        END;
    END;
    LINE_P(G1, esPL, 46, esPL, 150, #000000h);
    LINE_P(G1, esPL, 150, esPR, 150, #000000h);
    // ---- x ticks: values of a' ----
    hgTk := MAX(1, es_Nice(esQN / 6));         // whole values of a' only
    hgLb := CEILING(esQLo / hgTk) * hgTk;
    WHILE hgLb <= esQLo + esQN - 1 DO
        hgXc := esPL + IP((hgLb - esQLo + 0.5) * hgPt);
        LINE_P(G1, hgXc, 155, hgXc, 158, #000000h);
        TEXTOUT_P(STRING(hgLb, 2, 0), G1, hgXc - 6, 158, 1, esGRAY, 30);
        hgLb := hgLb + hgTk;
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P("a =", G1, 4, 158, 1, esGRAY, 26);
    // ---- the legend ----
    TEXTOUT_P("red = your table (a = " + STRING(hgA, 2, 0) + "), " + es_EqTxt("P", es_FmtNum(esQP0, 6)), G1, 6, 170, 1, esRED, 308);
    TEXTOUT_P("magenta = P <= yours; they sum to " + es_EqTxt("p", es_FmtNum(esQPv, 6)), G1, 6, 182, 1, esMAG, 308);
    TEXTOUT_P("grey = tables more likely than yours", G1, 6, 194, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    IF esQCut > 0 THEN
        hgS := STRING(esQCut, 2, 0) + " more tables have P near 0 (not drawn)";
    ELSE
        hgS := "all " + STRING(esQN, 2, 0) + " tables with these row/column totals";
    END;
    IF esQN > 60 THEN hgS := STRING(esQN, 2, 0) + " bars: too narrow to label; a tap picks one"; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(hgS, G1, 6, 206, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    // ---- the corner panel: the picked bar's table ----
    IF esQSel >= 1 AND esQSel <= esQN THEN
        hgAp := esQLo + esQSel - 1;
        hgPx := esPR - 112;
        IF esPL + (hgMd - 0.5) * hgPt > (esPL + esPR) / 2 THEN hgPx := esPL + 4; END;
        RECT_P(G1, hgPx, 48, hgPx + 108, 116, esORNG, esLORN);
        hgS := "a = " + STRING(hgAp, 2, 0);
        IF esQSel == hgOb THEN hgS := hgS + " (yours)"; END;
        TEXTOUT_P(hgS, G1, hgPx + 5, 51, 1, IFTE(esQSel == hgOb, esRED, #000000h), 100);
        TEXTOUT_P(STRING(hgAp, 2, 0) + "   " + STRING(hgA + hgB - hgAp, 2, 0) + "  | " + STRING(hgA + hgB, 2, 0), G1, hgPx + 5, 64, 1, #000000h, 100);
        TEXTOUT_P(STRING(hgA + hgC - hgAp, 2, 0) + "   " + STRING(hgD - hgA + hgAp, 2, 0) + "  | " + STRING(hgC + hgD, 2, 0), G1, hgPx + 5, 76, 1, #000000h, 100);
        TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt("P", es_FmtNum(esQP(esQSel), 6)), G1, hgPx + 5, 89, 1, #000000h, 100);
        IF esQK(esQSel) == 1 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P("counted in p", G1, hgPx + 5, 102, 1, esMAG, 100);
        ELSE
            TEXTOUT_P("not counted", G1, hgPx + 5, 102, 1, esGRAY, 100);
        END;
    END;
    es_AddHit(20, esPL, 44, esPR, 162);
    es_Foot("tap a bar = its table, " + CHAR(8592) + "/" + CHAR(8594) + " = page, Esc = menu");
    RETURN 1;
END;

// v1.7: the picture on page 7, worked out on the first visit for each
// table from esNL (es_CondOR filled it).  For every possible a':
//   esOP  P(a') at the conditional MLE (all 0 when the MLE is 0 or
//         infinite: then all the probability sits on your a)
//   esOQ  P(a') when OR = 1, the Fisher bars of page 4
// and only the stretch of a' where either is at least 1/10000 of the
// tallest bar is drawn (your a always included):
//   esOLo the first index drawn, esON how many, esOMx the tallest,
//   esOE0 the mean of a' when OR = 1 (= (a+b)(a+c)/N)
//   esOOK 0 not worked out, 1 ready, 2 nothing to draw (state 3 or 4)
es_CORFill(cfA)
BEGIN
  LOCAL cfS, cfI, cfT, cfG, cfM1, cfM0;
  LOCAL cfZ1, cfZ0, cfLo, cfHi, cfMx, cfOb;
  esOOK := 2;
  IF esCO(4) >= 3 THEN RETURN 0; END;
  cfS := esNHi - esNLo + 1;
  cfT := 0;
  IF esCO(4) == 0 THEN cfT := LN(esCO(1)); END;
  // the largest log weight of each distribution, so EXP never overflows
  cfM1 := esNL(1) + esNLo * cfT; cfM0 := esNL(1);
  FOR cfI FROM 2 TO cfS DO
    cfG := esNL(cfI) + (esNLo + cfI - 1) * cfT;
    IF cfG > cfM1 THEN cfM1 := cfG; END;
    IF esNL(cfI) > cfM0 THEN cfM0 := esNL(cfI); END;
  END;
  esOP := MAKELIST(0, K, 1, cfS, 1);
  esOQ := MAKELIST(0, K, 1, cfS, 1);
  cfZ1 := 0; cfZ0 := 0;
  FOR cfI FROM 1 TO cfS DO
    IF esCO(4) == 0 THEN
      cfG := esNL(cfI) + (esNLo + cfI - 1) * cfT - cfM1;
      IF cfG > -46 THEN esOP(cfI) := EXP(cfG); END;
    END;
    cfG := esNL(cfI) - cfM0;
    IF cfG > -46 THEN esOQ(cfI) := EXP(cfG); END;
    cfZ1 := cfZ1 + esOP(cfI); cfZ0 := cfZ0 + esOQ(cfI);
  END;
  esOE0 := 0; cfMx := 0;
  FOR cfI FROM 1 TO cfS DO
    IF cfZ1 > 0 THEN esOP(cfI) := esOP(cfI) / cfZ1; END;
    esOQ(cfI) := esOQ(cfI) / cfZ0;
    esOE0 := esOE0 + (esNLo + cfI - 1) * esOQ(cfI);
    IF esOP(cfI) > cfMx THEN cfMx := esOP(cfI); END;
    IF esOQ(cfI) > cfMx THEN cfMx := esOQ(cfI); END;
  END;
  // trim the far tails of both, never past your a
  cfOb := cfA - esNLo + 1;
  cfLo := 1;
  WHILE cfLo < cfOb AND MAX(esOP(cfLo), esOQ(cfLo)) < cfMx / 10000 DO cfLo := cfLo + 1; END;
  cfHi := cfS;
  WHILE cfHi > cfOb AND MAX(esOP(cfHi), esOQ(cfHi)) < cfMx / 10000 DO cfHi := cfHi - 1; END;
  esOLo := cfLo; esON := cfHi - cfLo + 1; esOMx := cfMx;
  esOOK := 1;
  RETURN 1;
END;

// PAGE 7 of module 1 (v1.7): THE EXACT ODDS RATIO.  The sample OR
// ad/bc (with its Woolf CI) beside the conditional MLE (with its exact
// CI), and a picture of what "conditional MLE" means: the orange bars
// are the distribution of a' when the true OR is the MLE -- their mean
// is exactly your a (the red line); the grey line is the same
// distribution when OR = 1 (page 4's Fisher bars).  The plot box is
// x esPL..esPR (34..306), y 62 to the axis at 148.
es_CORPage(cpA, cpB, cpC, cpD)
BEGIN
  LOCAL cpI, cpX1, cpX2, cpH, cpY, cpTop;
  LOCAL cpPt, cpS, cpX, cpTk, cpLb, cpOb;
  LOCAL cpPx, cpPy, cpLx, cpLy, cpXc;
  IF esOOK == 0 THEN es_CORFill(cpA); END;
  es_DrawHeader("EXACT ODDS RATIO", "7/7");
  cpS := "undefined";
  IF EP_OR <> 999 THEN cpS := es_SigTxt(EP_OR); END;
  // v1.7c: es_EqTxt -- "Sample OR = ad/bc < 0.000001", never "= <"
  TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt("Sample OR = ad/bc", cpS) + ", Woolf CI " + es_FmtCI(EP_OR_CI, 0), G1, 6, 27, 1, #000000h, 308);
  TEXTOUT_P("Conditional MLE OR = " + es_CORTxt(), G1, 6, 39, 1, esORNG, 308);
  TEXTOUT_P("(exact CI; R's fisher.test and SciPy report it)", G1, 6, 51, 1, esGRAY, 308);
  IF esOOK <> 1 THEN
    IF esCO(4) == 4 THEN
      TEXTOUT_P("N > 2000: not computed here, the same cap", G1, 12, 70, 1, #000000h, 296);
      TEXTOUT_P("as Fisher's test on page 3 (too many tables).", G1, 12, 84, 1, #000000h, 296);
    ELSE
      TEXTOUT_P("A row or column total is 0: with every table", G1, 12, 70, 1, #000000h, 296);
      TEXTOUT_P("the same, no odds ratio can be estimated.", G1, 12, 84, 1, #000000h, 296);
    END;
    es_DrawFooter(1);
    RETURN 0;
  END;
  // ---- the y scale, and a light grid at a quarter and a half ----
  cpTop := esOMx * 1.08;
  FOR cpI FROM 1 TO 3 DO
    cpY := 148 - IP(cpI * 21 + 0.5);
    LINE_P(G1, esPL, cpY, esPR, cpY, esGRID);
  END;
  // ---- the orange bars: P(a') at the MLE ----
  cpPt := (esPR - esPL) / esON;            // pixels per bar (can be < 1)
  cpOb := cpA - esNLo - esOLo + 2;         // your a, as a drawn bar (1..esON)
  FOR cpI FROM 1 TO esON DO
    cpX1 := esPL + IP((cpI - 1) * cpPt);
    cpX2 := esPL + IP(cpI * cpPt) - 1;
    IF cpPt >= 4 THEN cpX1 := cpX1 + 1; END;
    IF cpX2 < cpX1 THEN cpX2 := cpX1; END;
    cpH := IP(esOP(esOLo + cpI - 1) / cpTop * 84 + 0.5);
    IF cpH > 0 THEN RECT_P(G1, cpX1, 148 - cpH, cpX2, 148, esORNG, #FFA060h); END;
  END;
  // ---- the grey line: P(a') when OR = 1, through the bar centres ----
  cpLx := -1; cpLy := -1;
  FOR cpI FROM 1 TO esON DO
    cpPx := esPL + IP((cpI - 0.5) * cpPt);
    cpPy := 148 - IP(esOQ(esOLo + cpI - 1) / cpTop * 84 + 0.5);
    IF cpLx >= 0 THEN LINE_P(G1, cpLx, cpLy, cpPx, cpPy, esGRAY); END;
    IF cpPt >= 6 THEN RECT_P(G1, cpPx - 1, cpPy - 1, cpPx + 1, cpPy + 1, esGRAY, esGRAY); END;
    cpLx := cpPx; cpLy := cpPy;
  END;
  // ---- the mean when OR = 1, dotted grey; your a, red ----
  cpXc := esPL + IP((esOE0 - esNLo - esOLo + 1.5) * cpPt);
  IF cpXc >= esPL AND cpXc <= esPR THEN
    FOR cpY FROM 62 TO 146 STEP 3 DO PIXON_P(G1, cpXc, cpY, esGRAY); END;
  END;
  cpXc := esPL + IP((cpOb - 0.5) * cpPt);
  LINE_P(G1, cpXc, 60, cpXc, 148, esRED);
  cpS := "a = " + STRING(cpA, 2, 0);
  IF cpXc > esPR - 50 THEN
    TEXTOUT_P(cpS, G1, cpXc - 48, 60, 1, esRED, 46);
  ELSE
    TEXTOUT_P(cpS, G1, cpXc + 3, 60, 1, esRED, 46);
  END;
  LINE_P(G1, esPL, 60, esPL, 148, #000000h);
  LINE_P(G1, esPL, 148, esPR, 148, #000000h);
  // ---- x ticks: values of a' (the Fisher page's scheme) ----
  cpTk := MAX(1, es_Nice(esON / 6));
  cpLb := CEILING((esNLo + esOLo - 1) / cpTk) * cpTk;
  WHILE cpLb <= esNLo + esOLo + esON - 2 DO
    cpX := esPL + IP((cpLb - esNLo - esOLo + 1.5) * cpPt);
    LINE_P(G1, cpX, 149, cpX, 152, #000000h);
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(cpLb, 2, 0), G1, cpX - 6, 152, 1, esGRAY, 30);
    cpLb := cpLb + cpTk;
  END;
  TEXTOUT_P("a =", G1, 4, 152, 1, esGRAY, 26);
  // ---- the reading ----
  IF esCO(4) == 0 THEN
    TEXTOUT_P("orange = P(a) at the MLE: its mean is your a", G1, 6, 164, 1, esORNG, 308);
  ELSE
    IF esCO(4) == 1 THEN cpS := "MLE 0: your a is the smallest it can be"; ELSE cpS := "MLE infinite: your a is the largest it can be"; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(cpS, G1, 6, 164, 1, esORNG, 308);
  END;
  TEXTOUT_P("grey = P(a) if OR = 1 (page 4); its mean is " + STRING(ROUND(esOE0, 2), 2, 2), G1, 6, 176, 1, esGRAY, 308);
  TEXTOUT_P("Why two? This one fixes all four totals, as", G1, 6, 188, 1, #000000h, 308);
  TEXTOUT_P("Fisher's test does: nearer 1; big N: the same.", G1, 6, 200, 1, #000000h, 308);
  es_DrawFooter(1);
  RETURN 1;
END;

es_Module_Table2x2()
BEGIN
    LOCAL a, b, c, d;
    LOCAL r1, r2, c1, c2, N, p1;
    LOCAL ea, eb, ec, ed;
    LOCAL chisq, chisq_yates, chisq_p, chisq_yates_p;
    LOCAL fisher_p, phi, pearsonR;
    LOCAL oddsR, RR, RD;
    LOCAL page, action;
    LOCAL inputOK, fpt, mcn, xcp;

    // v1.5: the touch grid replaces v1.4's INPUT form (that form is
    // still one tap away: the Values soft key, or +).  The grid opens
    // on this module's last table.
    esMod := 1;
    esCel := esM1;
    IF esDir == 1 THEN
        inputOK := 1;          // v1.6: EpiStats2x2(a,b,c,d) from Home: no grid
    ELSE
        inputOK := es_Grid("2×2 TABLE: COUNTS", {"Exposed+", "Exposed-"}, {"Outcome+", "Outcome-"});
    END;
    esM1 := esCel;
    IF inputOK == 0 THEN RETURN; END;
    a := esCel(1); b := esCel(2); c := esCel(3); d := esCel(4);

    // the grid never lets a negative or an empty table through; the
    // v1.4 checks stay as a guard
    IF a < 0 OR b < 0 OR c < 0 OR d < 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: All values must be ≥ 0");
        RETURN;
    END;

    N := a + b + c + d;
    IF N == 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: Table cannot be empty");
        RETURN;
    END;

    EP_table := {a, b, c, d};
    EP_N := N;

    r1 := a + b;
    r2 := c + d;
    c1 := a + c;
    c2 := b + d;
    p1 := r1 * r2 * c1 * c2;

    ea := r1 * c1 / N;
    eb := r1 * c2 / N;
    ec := r2 * c1 / N;
    ed := r2 * c2 / N;

    IF ea > 0 AND eb > 0 AND ec > 0 AND ed > 0 THEN
        chisq := (a-ea)^2/ea + (b-eb)^2/eb + (c-ec)^2/ec + (d-ed)^2/ed;
        // v1.7c: the upper tail with 1 df, 2 NORMALD_CDF(-sqrt(x)) -- the same number as
        // 1 - CHISQUARE_CDF, which is exactly 0 for a chi-square above about 52
        chisq_p := 2 * NORMALD_CDF(0, 1, -(chisq^0.5));
    ELSE
        chisq := 0;
        chisq_p := 1;
    END;
    EP_chisq := chisq;
    EP_chisq_p := chisq_p;

    IF p1 > 0 THEN
        // MAX(0,...) is REQUIRED. When |ad-bc| < N/2 the parenthesis goes negative
    // and squaring flips it positive, so the "conservative" Yates statistic
    // comes out LARGER than uncorrected Pearson. On the null table (5,5,5,5)
    // the unclamped form gives X2 = 0.2, p = 0.655 where Pearson gives 0 and
    // p = 1.000. Clamping matches scipy's chi2_contingency(correction=True).
    chisq_yates := N * MAX(0, ABS(a*d - b*c) - N/2)^2 / p1;
        // v1.7c: the upper tail with 1 df, as for Pearson's (never exactly 0)
        chisq_yates_p := 2 * NORMALD_CDF(0, 1, -(chisq_yates^0.5));
    ELSE
        chisq_yates := 0;
        chisq_yates_p := 1;
    END;
    EP_chisq_yates := chisq_yates;

    fisher_p := es_FisherExact(a, b, c, d);
    EP_fisher_p := fisher_p;
    fpt := es_FisherPt(a, b, c, d);      // v1.6: this table's own probability
    mcn := es_McNemar(b, c);             // v1.6: McNemar (the Paired reading)
    EP_MCN_P := mcn(2);

    IF p1 > 0 THEN
        phi := (a*d - b*c) / SQRT(p1);
    ELSE
        phi := 0;
    END;
    EP_phi := phi;
    EP_cramersV := ABS(phi);

    // For a 2x2 table, Pearson's r between the two binary variables IS phi --
    // signed. SQRT(chisq/N) returns |phi|, discarding the sign, so a negative
    // association displayed a POSITIVE "Pearson's r" directly beneath a negative
    // "Phi", and identical to Cramer's V (which is |phi| by definition). The
    // adjacent label "Phi^2 (= r^2)" already asserts r = +/-phi.
    // v1.4: this used to sit inside IF N > 0, but N == 0 already
    // returned at the top of the module, so the test was dead.
    pearsonR := phi;

    IF b*c > 0 THEN
        oddsR := (a*d)/(b*c);
    ELSE
        oddsR := 999;
    END;
    EP_OR := oddsR;
    EP_OR_CI := es_CalcORCI(a, b, c, d);

    IF r1 > 0 AND r2 > 0 THEN
        EP_risk_exp := a/r1;
        EP_risk_unexp := c/r2;
        IF EP_risk_unexp > 0 THEN
            RR := EP_risk_exp / EP_risk_unexp;
        ELSE
            RR := 999;
        END;
        RD := EP_risk_exp - EP_risk_unexp;
        EP_RR_CI := es_CalcRRCI(a, r1, c, r2);
        EP_RD_CI := es_CalcRDCI(a, r1, c, r2);
    ELSE
        RR := 999;
        // v1.7d (E4): with a row empty there is no risk in that row, so
        // there is no risk difference either.  v1.7c set RD := 0 and the
        // "CI" {-1, 1}, and the pages printed "RD = 0.0% (-100.0-100.0%)".
        // RD now carries the 999 sentinel, as RR does: es_FmtPct prints
        // it "undef", and EP_RD_CI {0, 0} is blanked by es_FmtCI.
        RD := 999;
        EP_risk_exp := 0;        // v1.4: were left from the previous run
        EP_risk_unexp := 0;
        EP_RR_CI := {0, 999};
        EP_RD_CI := {0, 0};
    END;
    EP_RR := RR;
    EP_RD := RD;

    // v1.7: the conditional MLE odds ratio and its exact CI (page 1's
    // cohort tab and page 7), once per table
    esCO := es_CondOR(a, b, c, d);
    esOOK := 0;                         // page 7's picture: on its first visit
    EP_OR_CMLE := 999; EP_OR_CMLE_CI := {0, 999};
    IF esCO(4) == 0 THEN EP_OR_CMLE := esCO(1); EP_OR_CMLE_CI := {esCO(2), esCO(3)}; END;
    IF esCO(4) == 1 THEN EP_OR_CMLE := 0; EP_OR_CMLE_CI := {0, esCO(3)}; END;
    IF esCO(4) == 2 THEN EP_OR_CMLE_CI := {esCO(2), 999}; END;
    // v1.7: the exact CIs of sensitivity a/(a+c) and specificity
    // d/(b+d) for the Diagnostic tab, once per table
    esX1 := {-1, -1, -1, -1};
    IF esBinOK == 1 THEN
        IF c1 > 0 THEN xcp := es_CPInt(a, c1, 0.025, 0); esX1(1) := xcp(1); esX1(2) := xcp(2); END;
        IF c2 > 0 THEN xcp := es_CPInt(d, c2, 0.025, 0); esX1(3) := xcp(1); esX1(4) := xcp(2); END;
    END;

    // v1.6: six pages.  1 the three readings (tabs), 2 the table and
    // its expected counts, 3 the tests (Fisher's point probability
    // added), 4 the Fisher bars, 5 the association measures with the
    // worked arithmetic (v1.5's page 3), 6 the mosaic (v1.5's page 4).
    // v1.7: and 7, the exact (conditional) odds ratio.
    // Taps turn pages (es_TurnPage); on page 1 a tap on a tab changes
    // the reading (esRd), on page 4 a tap on the plot picks a bar
    // (esQSel), on page 5 a tap on a row opens its arithmetic (esPick),
    // on page 6 a tap on a tile shows its numbers (esTile).  Up/Down do
    // the same from the keys.
    page := 1; esPick := 0; esLast := 0; esTile := 0;
    esRd := 1; esQOK := 0; esQSel := 0;     // a new table: work the bars out again
    REPEAT
        CASE
            IF page == 1 THEN
                es_ReadPage(a, b, c, d);
            END;
            IF page == 2 THEN
                es_Table2x2_Page1(a, b, c, d, r1, r2, c1, c2, N, ea, eb, ec, ed);
            END;
            IF page == 3 THEN
                es_Table2x2_Page2(chisq, chisq_p, chisq_yates, chisq_yates_p, fisher_p, N, fpt);
            END;
            IF page == 4 THEN
                es_HyperPage(a, b, c, d);
            END;
            IF page == 5 THEN
                es_Table2x2_Page3(oddsR, RR, RD, phi, pearsonR);
            END;
            IF page == 6 THEN
                es_Mosaic(a, b, c, d);
            END;
            // v1.7: the exact (conditional) odds ratio
            IF page == 7 THEN
                es_CORPage(a, b, c, d);
            END;
        END;
        // the worked-arithmetic box goes on top of page 5 (G1 still
        // holds the page, so the box is one more frame and one blit)
        IF page == 5 AND esPick > 0 THEN es_Arith(esPick, a, b, c, d); END;
        action := es_WaitKey();
        IF esPick > 0 THEN
            esPick := 0;                    // any key or tap closes the box
        ELSE
            IF action == 0 THEN BREAK; END;
            CASE
                IF action == 3 AND page == 1 THEN
                    esRd := esHit;          // a tab: 1 cohort, 2 diagnostic, 3 paired
                END;
                IF page == 1 AND (action == 4 OR action == 5) THEN
                    IF action == 5 THEN esRd := esRd + 1; ELSE esRd := esRd - 1; END;
                    IF esRd > 3 THEN esRd := 1; END;
                    IF esRd < 1 THEN esRd := 3; END;
                END;
                IF action == 3 AND page == 4 THEN
                    // the bar under the finger: the same esPL..esPR the
                    // bars are drawn with
                    IF esHit == 20 AND esQN > 0 THEN
                        esQSel := MIN(esQN, MAX(1, IP((esTX - esPL) * esQN / (esPR - esPL)) + 1));
                    END;
                END;
                IF page == 4 AND (action == 4 OR action == 5) THEN
                    IF esQN > 0 THEN
                        IF action == 5 THEN esQSel := esQSel + 1; ELSE esQSel := esQSel - 1; END;
                        esQSel := MIN(esQN, MAX(1, esQSel));
                    END;
                END;
                IF action == 3 AND page == 5 THEN
                    esPick := esHit; esLast := esHit;
                END;
                IF page == 5 AND (action == 4 OR action == 5) THEN
                    // the keyboard way in: Down opens OR, RR, RD in turn
                    IF action == 5 THEN esLast := esLast + 1; ELSE esLast := esLast - 1; END;
                    IF esLast > 3 THEN esLast := 1; END;
                    IF esLast < 1 THEN esLast := 3; END;
                    esPick := esLast;
                END;
                IF action == 3 AND page == 6 THEN
                    esTile := esHit;
                END;
                IF page == 6 AND (action == 4 OR action == 5) THEN
                    // and Down / Up step through the tiles a, b, c, d
                    IF action == 5 THEN esTile := esTile + 1; ELSE esTile := esTile - 1; END;
                    IF esTile > 4 THEN esTile := 1; END;
                    IF esTile < 1 THEN esTile := 4; END;
                END;
                DEFAULT
                    page := es_TurnPage(page, action, 7);   // v1.7: seven pages
            END;
        END;
    UNTIL 0;
END;


es_Table2x2_Page1(a, b, c, d, r1, r2, c1, c2, N, ea, eb, ec, ed)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y, x1, x2, x3;
    es_DrawHeader("2×2 TABLE ANALYSIS", "2/7");
    x1 := 100; x2 := 170; x3 := 240;
    y := 30;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Observed Values ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("Outcome+", G1, x1, y, 1, RGB(0,60,200));
    TEXTOUT_P("Outcome-", G1, x2, y, 1, RGB(0,60,200));
    TEXTOUT_P("Total", G1, x3, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Exposed+", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(a,1,0), G1, x1+15, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(b,1,0), G1, x2+15, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(r1,1,0), G1, x3+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Exposed-", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(c,1,0), G1, x1+15, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(d,1,0), G1, x2+15, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(r2,1,0), G1, x3+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Total", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(c1,1,0), G1, x1+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(c2,1,0), G1, x2+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(N,1,0), G1, x3+10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 22;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Expected Values ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("Outcome+", G1, x1, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P("Outcome-", G1, x2, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Exposed+", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(ea, 2), G1, x1+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(eb, 2), G1, x2+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Exposed-", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(ec, 2), G1, x1+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(ed, 2), G1, x2+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 20;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Cell Percentages ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("a/N: " + es_FmtPct(a/N), G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P("b/N: " + es_FmtPct(b/N), G1, 90, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P("c/N: " + es_FmtPct(c/N), G1, 170, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P("d/N: " + es_FmtPct(d/N), G1, 250, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

es_Table2x2_Page2(chisq, chisq_p, chisq_yates, chisq_yates_p, fisher_p, N, fpt)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y, sig;
    es_DrawHeader("STATISTICAL TESTS", "3/7");
    // v1.4: re-spaced.  The N line used to land at y 210 (font 2 ends
    // at 222, on the footer).  Rows are 14 px apart; the last row,
    // N, is at y 196 and ends at 208.
    y := 30;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Chi-Square Tests (df=1) ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("Pearson χ²:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(chisq, 4), G1, 140, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    IF chisq_p < 0.05 THEN sig := " *"; ELSE sig := ""; END;
    TEXTOUT_P("p-value:", G1, 30, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(chisq_p, 5) + sig, G1, 140, y, 1,
              IFTE(chisq_p < 0.05, RGB(255,0,0), RGB(0,0,0)));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("Yates' χ² (continuity corrected):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(chisq_yates, 4), G1, 140, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    IF chisq_yates_p < 0.05 THEN sig := " *"; ELSE sig := ""; END;
    TEXTOUT_P("p-value:", G1, 30, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(chisq_yates_p, 5) + sig, G1, 140, y, 1,
              IFTE(chisq_yates_p < 0.05, RGB(255,0,0), RGB(0,0,0)));
    // v1.6: two pixels tighter from here down, to make room for the
    // point probability (FEP's and ConTable2's second Fisher line)
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Fisher's Exact Test ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    IF fisher_p >= 0 THEN
        IF fisher_p < 0.05 THEN sig := " *"; ELSE sig := ""; END;
        TEXTOUT_P("Two-sided exact p:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
        TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(fisher_p, 6) + sig, G1, 180, y, 2,
                  IFTE(fisher_p < 0.05, RGB(255,0,0), RGB(0,0,0)));
        y := y + 15;
        TEXTOUT_P("Point prob. (this table): " + es_FmtNum(fpt, 6) + "  (page 4)", G1, 30, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90), 284);
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P("N > 2000: Use χ² approximation", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
        y := y + 15;
    END;
    y := y + 15;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Interpretation ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("* Significant at α = 0.05", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(255,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("N = " + STRING(N,1,0), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    IF N < 20 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("(Small N: prefer Fisher's exact)", G1, 80, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    END;
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

es_Table2x2_Page3(oddsR, RR, RD, phi, pearsonR)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y;
    es_DrawHeader("ASSOCIATION MEASURES", "5/7");
    // v1.4: re-spaced.  Cramer's V used to sit at y 212 and the
    // Quick Reference block at y 232-256: under the footer and
    // below the screen, so nobody ever saw it.  Rows are now 14 px
    // apart (12 px font-2 rows, 2 px of air); Cramer's V is at y 190
    // and the Quick Reference is one small-font line at y 205, which
    // ends at 215 -- everything is above the footer at 222.  The full
    // text of the old reference lines is in Help > Risk & Odds Ratios.
    // v1.5: the OR, RR and RD rows (each with its CI line) are
    // tappable: es_AddHit records each row's box as it is drawn, and
    // a tap there opens the worked arithmetic (es_Arith).  The small
    // orange "(tap)" marks them.  The boxes stop at x 258, so a tap
    // at the right-hand edge, or anywhere below the three rows, still
    // turns the page.
    y := 30;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Effect Measures ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    es_AddHit(1, 4, y - 2, 258, y + 25);
    TEXTOUT_P("Odds Ratio (OR):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(oddsR, 3), G1, 170, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    TEXTOUT_P("(tap)", G1, 222, y + 2, 1, esORNG, 36);
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("95% CI: " + es_FmtCI(EP_OR_CI, 0), G1, 30, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    es_AddHit(2, 4, y - 2, 258, y + 25);
    TEXTOUT_P("Risk Ratio (RR):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(RR, 3), G1, 170, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    TEXTOUT_P("(tap)", G1, 222, y + 2, 1, esORNG, 36);
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("95% CI: " + es_FmtCI(EP_RR_CI, 0), G1, 30, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    es_AddHit(3, 4, y - 2, 258, y + 25);
    TEXTOUT_P("Risk Difference (RD):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(RD), G1, 170, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P("(tap)", G1, 222, y + 2, 1, esORNG, 36);
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("95% CI: " + es_FmtCI(EP_RD_CI, 1), G1, 30, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Correlation Measures ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("Phi (φ):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(phi, 4), G1, 170, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Phi² (= r²):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(phi^2, 4), G1, 170, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Pearson's r:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(pearsonR, 4), G1, 170, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Cramer's V:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(ABS(phi), 4), G1, 170, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 15;
    TEXTOUT_P("OR or RR = 1 none, > 1 positive, < 1 protective", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    es_Foot("tap a ratio = working, " + CHAR(8592) + "/" + CHAR(8594) + " = page, Esc = menu");
END;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MODULE 2: RISK ANALYSIS (Cohort Study Design)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

es_Module_RiskAnalysis()
BEGIN
    LOCAL a, b, c, d;
    LOCAL r1, r2, c1, c2, N;
    LOCAL Rexp, Runexp, Rtot;
    LOCAL oddsR, RR, RD, NNT;
    LOCAL AFe, AFp, ERR;
    LOCAL page, action;
    LOCAL inputOK;

    // v1.5: the touch grid replaces v1.4's INPUT form (Values or +
    // still opens it).  A cohort table has the same layout as module
    // 1: rows = exposed or not, columns = became a case or not.
    esMod := 2;
    esCel := esM2;
    inputOK := es_Grid("COHORT: COUNTS", {"Exposed+", "Exposed-"}, {"Outcome+", "Outcome-"});
    esM2 := esCel;
    IF inputOK == 0 THEN RETURN; END;
    a := esCel(1); b := esCel(2); c := esCel(3); d := esCel(4);

    // the grid never lets a negative or an empty table through; the
    // v1.4 checks stay as a guard
    IF a < 0 OR b < 0 OR c < 0 OR d < 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: Values must be ≥ 0");
        RETURN;
    END;

    N := a + b + c + d;
    IF N == 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: Table cannot be empty");
        RETURN;
    END;

    // v1.7d (E2): a risk is the cases in a row over the PEOPLE in that
    // row.  With a row empty (a + b = 0 or c + d = 0) that row has no
    // risk at all; v1.7c read it as 0 %, so the pages went on to "RD =
    // -50.0%" and "NNT (benefit) = 2.0".  Refused here, before any EP_*
    // result is set, as Incidence Rates refuses zero person-time.
    // (Module 1 still takes such a table: chi-square and Fisher hold.)
    IF a + b == 0 OR c + d == 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("A risk needs people in both rows: one row of the table is empty.");
        RETURN;
    END;

    EP_table := {a, b, c, d};
    EP_N := N;

    r1 := a + b;
    r2 := c + d;
    c1 := a + c;
    c2 := b + d;

    IF r1 > 0 THEN Rexp := a / r1; ELSE Rexp := 0; END;
    IF r2 > 0 THEN Runexp := c / r2; ELSE Runexp := 0; END;
    IF N > 0 THEN Rtot := c1 / N; ELSE Rtot := 0; END;

    EP_risk_exp := Rexp;
    EP_risk_unexp := Runexp;

    IF Runexp > 0 THEN
        RR := Rexp / Runexp;
        EP_RR := RR;
        EP_RR_CI := es_CalcRRCI(a, r1, c, r2);
    ELSE
        RR := 999;
        EP_RR := 999;
        EP_RR_CI := {0, 999};
    END;

    IF b > 0 AND c > 0 THEN
        oddsR := (a * d) / (b * c);
        EP_OR := oddsR;
        EP_OR_CI := es_CalcORCI(a, b, c, d);
    ELSE
        oddsR := 999;
        EP_OR := 999;
        EP_OR_CI := {0, 999};
    END;

    RD := Rexp - Runexp;
    EP_RD := RD;
    EP_RD_CI := es_CalcRDCI(a, r1, c, r2);

    // v1.7: the exact (Clopper-Pearson) CI of each risk, once per
    // table; page 4 draws it beside Wilson's
    EP_risk_exp_CP := {0, 0}; EP_risk_unexp_CP := {0, 0};
    IF esBinOK == 1 THEN
        IF r1 > 0 THEN EP_risk_exp_CP := es_CPInt(a, r1, 0.025, 0); END;
        IF r2 > 0 THEN EP_risk_unexp_CP := es_CPInt(c, r2, 0.025, 0); END;
    END;

    IF RD <> 0 THEN
        NNT := 1 / ABS(RD);
        EP_NNT := NNT;
        // v1.7d (E7): when |RD| is below 0.000001 the NNT is above
        // 1,000,000, no meaningful number of people to treat (and from
        // 1E12 on, STRING printed it in scientific form, "1.00E12").
        // Pages 3 and 5 say so, reading RD itself; NNT keeps its value
        // for them, and EP_NNT takes the 999 "undefined" sentinel that
        // Help > Variables names, as it already did for RD = 0.
        IF ABS(RD) < 0.000001 THEN EP_NNT := 999; END;
    ELSE
        NNT := 999;
        EP_NNT := 999;
    END;

    IF Rexp > 0 THEN
        AFe := (Rexp - Runexp) / Rexp;
        EP_AFe := AFe;
    ELSE
        AFe := 0;
        EP_AFe := 0;
    END;

    IF Rtot > 0 THEN
        AFp := (Rtot - Runexp) / Rtot;
        EP_AFp := AFp;
    ELSE
        AFp := 0;
        EP_AFp := 0;
    END;

    // v1.4: when RR is the 999 "undefined" sentinel, ERR stays 999 so
    // the display prints "undef" (it used to print 998 and 99800%).
    IF RR == 999 THEN ERR := 999; ELSE ERR := RR - 1; END;

    page := 1;
    REPEAT
        CASE
            IF page == 1 THEN
                es_Risk_Page1(Rexp, Runexp, Rtot, r1, r2, a, c);
            END;
            IF page == 2 THEN
                es_Risk_Page2(RR, oddsR, RD, ERR);
            END;
            IF page == 3 THEN
                es_Risk_Page3(NNT, AFe, AFp, RD);
            END;
            // v1.6: the risk bars and the NNT icon array
            IF page == 4 THEN
                es_RiskBars(a, b, c, d, RR, oddsR);
            END;
            IF page == 5 THEN
                es_NNTIcons(RD, NNT);
            END;
        END;
        action := es_WaitKey();
        IF action == 0 THEN BREAK; END;
        page := es_TurnPage(page, action, 5);   // v1.5: taps turn pages too
    UNTIL 0;
END;

es_Risk_Page1(Rexp, Runexp, Rtot, r1, r2, a, c)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y;
    es_DrawHeader("RISK ESTIMATES", "1/5");
    y := 32;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Absolute Risks ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 20;
    TEXTOUT_P("Risk in Exposed (Re):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(Rexp), G1, 200, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("(" + STRING(a,1,0) + "/" + STRING(r1,1,0) + ")", G1, 200, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 20;
    TEXTOUT_P("Risk in Unexposed (Ru):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(Runexp), G1, 200, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("(" + STRING(c,1,0) + "/" + STRING(r2,1,0) + ")", G1, 200, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 20;
    TEXTOUT_P("Overall Risk (Rt):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(Rtot), G1, 200, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 26;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Interpretation ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    // v1.4: the "x higher / x lower" ratio divides by the smaller
    // risk, which is 0 when one group has no events (c = 0 or a = 0).
    // That was a runtime division by zero in front of the class.  A
    // ratio against a zero risk is undefined, and saying so is the
    // teaching point.
    IF Rexp > Runexp THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("Exposure INCREASES risk", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(255,0,0));
        y := y + 14;
        IF Runexp > 0 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P("Risk is " + es_FmtNum(Rexp/Runexp, 2) + "x higher in exposed", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
        ELSE
            TEXTOUT_P("Ratio undefined: no events in unexposed group", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
        END;
    ELSE
        IF Rexp < Runexp THEN
            TEXTOUT_P("Exposure DECREASES risk", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
            y := y + 14;
            IF Rexp > 0 THEN
                TEXTOUT_P("Risk is " + es_FmtNum(Runexp/Rexp, 2) + "x lower in exposed", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
            ELSE
                TEXTOUT_P("Ratio undefined: no events in exposed group", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
            END;
        ELSE
            TEXTOUT_P("No difference in risk", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
        END;
    END;
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

es_Risk_Page2(RR, oddsR, RD, ERR)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y;
    es_DrawHeader("COMPARATIVE MEASURES", "2/5");
    // v1.4: re-spaced.  The ERR line used to land at y 212 (font 2
    // ends at 224, on the footer); it is now at y 190.
    y := 30;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Risk Ratio (Relative Risk) ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    // v1.7c: es_EqLb -- no "=" in the label when the value is "< 0.001"
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqLb("RR = Re / Ru", es_FmtNum(RR, 3)), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(RR, 3), G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("95% CI: " + es_FmtCI(EP_RR_CI, 0), G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Odds Ratio ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    // v1.7c: es_EqLb, as above
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqLb("OR = (a×d)/(b×c)", es_FmtNum(oddsR, 3)), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(oddsR, 3), G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("95% CI: " + es_FmtCI(EP_OR_CI, 0), G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Risk Difference ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("RD = Re - Ru =", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(RD), G1, 150, y, 2, IFTE(RD > 0, RGB(255,0,0), RGB(0,140,60)));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("95% CI: " + es_FmtCI(EP_RD_CI, 1), G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Excess Relative Risk ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    // v1.7c: es_EqLb, as above
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqLb("ERR = RR - 1", es_FmtNum(ERR, 3)), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    // v1.4: ERR carries the 999 sentinel when RR is undefined
    IF ERR == 999 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("undef (RR undefined)", G1, 140, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(ERR, 3) + " (" + es_FmtPct(ERR) + ")", G1, 140, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    END;
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

es_Risk_Page3(NNT, AFe, AFp, RD)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y;
    es_DrawHeader("CLINICAL IMPACT", "3/5");
    y := 32;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Number Needed to Treat ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    // v1.7d (E7): |RD| below 0.000001 (but not 0) gives an NNT above
    // 1,000,000, which tells a reader nothing, and from 1E12 on the
    // interpretation's STRING(CEILING(NNT), 1, 0) and es_FmtNum were in
    // scientific form ("For every 1.00000100011E12 exposed").  The line
    // reads "> 1,000,000", with no "=" before it, in its side's colour,
    // and says why.  Every other RD draws what v1.7c drew.
    IF RD <> 0 AND ABS(RD) < 0.000001 THEN
        IF RD > 0 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P("NNT (harm) = 1/RD", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
            TEXTOUT_P("> 1,000,000", G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(255,0,0));
        ELSE
            TEXTOUT_P("NNT (benefit) = 1/|RD|", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
            TEXTOUT_P("> 1,000,000", G1, 210, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
        END;
        y := y + 16;
        TEXTOUT_P("RD near 0 (|RD| < 0.000001): no meaningful NNT;", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
        y := y + 12;
        TEXTOUT_P("the two risks are practically equal", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    ELSE
        IF RD > 0 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P("NNT (harm) = 1/RD =", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
            TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(NNT, 1), G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(255,0,0));
            y := y + 16;
            TEXTOUT_P("Interpretation: For every " + STRING(CEILING(NNT),1,0) + " exposed,", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
            y := y + 12;
            TEXTOUT_P("1 additional case occurs due to exposure", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
        ELSE
            IF RD < 0 THEN
                TEXTOUT_P("NNT (benefit) = 1/|RD| =", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
                TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(NNT, 1), G1, 210, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
                y := y + 16;
                TEXTOUT_P("Interpretation: Treat " + STRING(CEILING(NNT),1,0) + " to prevent", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
                y := y + 12;
                TEXTOUT_P("1 case", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
            ELSE
                TEXTOUT_P("NNT: undefined (RD = 0)", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
            END;
        END;
    END;
    y := y + 26;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Attributable Fractions ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("AF (exposed) = (Re-Ru)/Re:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(AFe), G1, 230, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("% of cases in exposed due to exposure", G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 20;
    TEXTOUT_P("AF (population) = (Rt-Ru)/Rt:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(AFp), G1, 230, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("% of all cases due to exposure", G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 20;
    IF RD < 0 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("(Negative RD suggests protective effect)", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(0,140,60));
    END;
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

// PAGE 4 of module 2 (v1.6): THE TWO RISKS AS BARS.  Each bar is a
// group's risk, the whisker its 95 % Wilson CI (es_WilsonCI, the
// interval module 3 uses for proportions); the magenta bracket on
// the right joins the two bar tops, and its length is the risk
// difference.  Under the plot: how far the odds ratio overstates the
// risk ratio, from the data: OR / RR = (1 - Ru) / (1 - Re), so the
// two agree only when the outcome is rare in both groups.
es_RiskBars(rbA, rbB, rbC, rbD, rbRR, rbOR)
BEGIN
    LOCAL rbN1, rbN0, rbRe, rbRu, rbCe, rbCu;
    LOCAL rbTop, rbStp, rbV, rbY, rbYe, rbYu;
    LOCAL rbI, rbX, rbS, rbPc, rbYl, rbYh;
    LOCAL rbP, rbNm, rbK, rbL1, rbL2, rbRf;
    LOCAL rbXe, rbXu, rbXl, rbXh;
    rbN1 := rbA + rbB; rbN0 := rbC + rbD;
    es_DrawHeader("RISK BARS", "4/5");
    IF rbN1 == 0 OR rbN0 == 0 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("Both rows need people: a risk is the cases", G1, 12, 44, 1, #000000h, 296);
        TEXTOUT_P("in a row divided by the people in it.", G1, 12, 58, 1, #000000h, 296);
        es_DrawFooter(1);
        RETURN 0;
    END;
    rbRe := rbA / rbN1; rbRu := rbC / rbN0;
    rbCe := es_WilsonCI(rbA, rbN1); rbCu := es_WilsonCI(rbC, rbN0);
    // v1.7: the exact CIs, worked out at module entry
    rbXe := EP_risk_exp_CP; rbXu := EP_risk_unexp_CP;
    // ---- the y scale: 0 at y 150, rbTop at y 40 ----
    rbTop := MAX(MAX(rbCe(2), rbCu(2)), 0.05);
    IF esBinOK == 1 THEN rbTop := MAX(rbTop, MAX(rbXe(2), rbXu(2))); END;
    rbStp := es_Nice(rbTop / 4);
    rbTop := CEILING(rbTop / rbStp - 0.000001) * rbStp;
    rbV := 0;
    WHILE rbV <= rbTop + rbStp * 0.001 DO
        rbY := 150 - IP(rbV / rbTop * 110 + 0.5);
        LINE_P(G1, 40, rbY, 214, rbY, esGRID);
        TEXTOUT_P(STRING(100 * rbV, 2, MAX(0, -FLOOR(LOG(100 * rbStp)))) + "%", G1, 2, rbY - 5, 1, esGRAY, 36);
        rbV := rbV + rbStp;
    END;
    LINE_P(G1, 40, 38, 40, 150, #000000h);
    LINE_P(G1, 40, 150, 214, 150, #000000h);
    // ---- the two bars, exposed at x 60, unexposed at x 140 ----
    FOR rbI FROM 1 TO 2 DO
        IF rbI == 1 THEN
            rbP := rbRe; rbYl := rbCe(1); rbYh := rbCe(2); rbX := 60; rbNm := "Exposed+";
            rbS := STRING(rbA, 2, 0) + "/" + STRING(rbN1, 2, 0);
            rbXl := rbXe(1); rbXh := rbXe(2);
        ELSE
            rbP := rbRu; rbYl := rbCu(1); rbYh := rbCu(2); rbX := 140; rbNm := "Exposed-";
            rbS := STRING(rbC, 2, 0) + "/" + STRING(rbN0, 2, 0);
            rbXl := rbXu(1); rbXh := rbXu(2);
        END;
        rbY := 150 - IP(rbP / rbTop * 110 + 0.5);
        IF rbY < 150 THEN RECT_P(G1, rbX, rbY, rbX + 50, 150, esBLUE, esMBLU); END;
        // the 95 % Wilson CI as a black whisker left of the bar's
        // middle (v1.7: moved 6 px left to make room) ...
        rbYl := 150 - IP(rbYl / rbTop * 110 + 0.5);
        rbYh := 150 - IP(rbYh / rbTop * 110 + 0.5);
        LINE_P(G1, rbX + 19, rbYl, rbX + 19, rbYh, #000000h);
        LINE_P(G1, rbX + 14, rbYl, rbX + 24, rbYl, #000000h);
        LINE_P(G1, rbX + 14, rbYh, rbX + 24, rbYh, #000000h);
        // ... and v1.7: the exact (Clopper-Pearson) CI in orange to its
        // right -- the longer of the two, since exact never covers less
        // than 95 %
        IF esBinOK == 1 THEN
            rbXl := 150 - IP(rbXl / rbTop * 110 + 0.5);
            rbXh := 150 - IP(rbXh / rbTop * 110 + 0.5);
            LINE_P(G1, rbX + 31, rbXl, rbX + 31, rbXh, esORNG);
            LINE_P(G1, rbX + 32, rbXl, rbX + 32, rbXh, esORNG);
            LINE_P(G1, rbX + 26, rbXl, rbX + 37, rbXl, esORNG);
            LINE_P(G1, rbX + 26, rbXh, rbX + 37, rbXh, esORNG);
            rbYh := MIN(rbYh, rbXh);
        END;
        TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(rbP), G1, rbX + 8, MAX(27, rbYh - 12), 1, esBLUE, 60);
        TEXTOUT_P(rbNm, G1, rbX - 2, 153, 1, esBLUE, 66);
        TEXTOUT_P(rbS, G1, rbX + 4, 165, 1, esGRAY, 60);
        IF rbI == 1 THEN rbYe := rbY; ELSE rbYu := rbY; END;
    END;
    // ---- the risk difference: a bracket joining the two bar tops ----
    FOR rbK FROM 112 TO 222 STEP 3 DO PIXON_P(G1, rbK, rbYe, esMAG); END;
    FOR rbK FROM 192 TO 222 STEP 3 DO PIXON_P(G1, rbK, rbYu, esMAG); END;
    LINE_P(G1, 222, rbYe, 222, rbYu, esMAG);
    LINE_P(G1, 218, rbYe, 222, rbYe, esMAG);
    LINE_P(G1, 218, rbYu, 222, rbYu, esMAG);
    rbY := IP((rbYe + rbYu) / 2);
    TEXTOUT_P("RD =", G1, 228, rbY - 11, 1, esMAG, 88);
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(rbRe - rbRu), G1, 228, rbY + 1, 1, esMAG, 88);
    // v1.7: the legend names both whiskers
    IF esBinOK == 1 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("95% CIs: black = Wilson, orange = exact (C-P)", G1, 6, 180, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    ELSE
