        TEXTOUT_P("Bars = risk, whiskers = 95% Wilson CI.", G1, 6, 180, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    END;
    // ---- OR against RR, computed from these data ----
    IF rbRR == 999 OR rbOR == 999 OR rbRe == 0 OR rbRu == 0 THEN
        rbL1 := "OR vs RR: not compared (a ratio is 0 or";
        rbL2 := "undefined because of a zero count).";
    ELSE
        // name the risk that makes the outcome common: Ru, or Re if Ru is small
        rbRf := "Ru = " + STRING(ROUND(100 * rbRu, 1), 2, 1) + " %";
        IF rbRu < 0.1 AND rbRe >= 0.1 THEN rbRf := "Re = " + STRING(100 * rbRe, 2, 1) + " %"; END;
        IF ABS(rbRR - 1) < 0.0000001 THEN
            rbL1 := "RR = OR = 1: no association, so there is";
            rbL2 := "nothing for the OR to overstate.";
        ELSE
            IF rbRR > 1 THEN
                rbPc := 100 * (rbOR / rbRR - 1);
                rbS := " overstates RR = ";
            ELSE
                rbPc := 100 * (1 - rbOR / rbRR);
                rbS := " exaggerates RR = ";
            END;
            IF MAX(rbRe, rbRu) >= 0.1 THEN
                rbL1 := "OR = " + STRING(ROUND(rbOR, 2), 2, 2) + rbS + STRING(ROUND(rbRR, 2), 2, 2) + " by " + STRING(ROUND(rbPc, 0), 2, 0) + " % here";
                rbL2 := "because the outcome is common (" + rbRf + ").";
            ELSE
                rbL1 := "OR = " + STRING(ROUND(rbOR, 2), 2, 2) + rbS + STRING(ROUND(rbRR, 2), 2, 2) + " by only " + STRING(ROUND(rbPc, 0), 2, 0) + " %:";
                rbL2 := "the outcome is rare here (Ru = " + STRING(ROUND(100 * rbRu, 1), 2, 1) + " %).";
            END;
        END;
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P(rbL1, G1, 6, 193, 1, #000000h, 308);
    TEXTOUT_P(rbL2, G1, 6, 205, 1, #000000h, 308);
    es_DrawFooter(1);
    RETURN 1;
END;

// PAGE 5 of module 2 (v1.6): THE NNT AS 100 PEOPLE.  CEILING(NNT)
// cells (the usual rounding up, as page 3 does) are the group that
// is exposed or treated; one of them is coloured: red for the one
// extra case the exposure causes (RD > 0), green for the one case
// the treatment prevents (RD < 0).  The rest of the 100 are grey, for
// scale.  An NNT above 100 cannot fit: the page says so.
es_NNTIcons(niRD, niNNT)
BEGIN
    LOCAL niM, niI, niR, niC, niX, niY;
    LOCAL niF, niK, niS;
    es_DrawHeader("NNT: ICON ARRAY", "5/5");
    IF niRD == 0 OR niNNT == 999 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("RD = 0: the risks are equal, so the NNT is", G1, 12, 44, 1, #000000h, 296);
        TEXTOUT_P("undefined (it would be 1/0).", G1, 12, 58, 1, #000000h, 296);
        es_DrawFooter(1);
        RETURN 0;
    END;
    // v1.7d (E7): |RD| below 0.000001 gives an NNT above 1,000,000: no
    // icon array can show it, and from 1E12 on STRING printed it in
    // scientific form (1 999999 1 1000000 drew "NNT = 1/< 0.000001 =
    // 1.00E12, round up: 1E12" and "Expose 1E12 (blue)").  The page
    // says so instead and draws no cells, as it does for RD = 0.
    IF ABS(niRD) < 0.000001 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("NNT > 1,000,000 (RD near 0: no meaningful NNT).", G1, 12, 44, 1, #000000h, 296);
        TEXTOUT_P("The two risks differ by less than 0.000001, so", G1, 12, 58, 1, #000000h, 296);
        TEXTOUT_P("no icon array is drawn.", G1, 12, 72, 1, #000000h, 296);
        es_DrawFooter(1);
        RETURN 0;
    END;
    niM := CEILING(niNNT);
    FOR niI FROM 1 TO 100 DO
        niR := IP((niI - 1) / 10);
        niC := niI - 1 - 10 * niR;
        niX := 90 + 14 * niC;
        niY := 32 + 14 * niR;
        niF := esGRID; niK := #C8C8C8h;                       // the rest of 100
        IF niI <= niM THEN niF := esMBLU; niK := esBLUE; END;  // the NNT group
        IF niI == MIN(niM, 100) THEN
            IF niRD > 0 THEN niF := esRED; niK := esRED; ELSE niF := esGRN; niK := esGRN; END;
        END;
        RECT_P(G1, niX, niY, niX + 11, niY + 11, niK, niF);
    END;
    // v1.7b: |RD| below 0.001 would print in scientific form with fixed
    // decimals (emulator, 17 Oct 2026), and 0.000 would read as 1/0, so
    // a small RD is shown to 3 significant figures (es_SigTxt); the
    // line then leaves out "|RD| = " to stay inside the screen
    IF ABS(niRD) < 0.001 THEN
        niS := "NNT = 1/" + es_SigTxt(ABS(niRD));
    ELSE
        niS := "NNT = 1/|RD| = 1/" + STRING(ABS(niRD), 2, 3);
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P(niS + " = " + STRING(niNNT, 2, 2) + ", round up: " + STRING(niM, 2, 0), G1, 6, 176, 1, #000000h, 308);
    IF niRD > 0 THEN
        niS := "Expose " + STRING(niM, 2, 0) + " (blue): about 1 extra case (red).";
        TEXTOUT_P(niS, G1, 6, 189, 1, esRED, 308);
    ELSE
        niS := "Treat " + STRING(niM, 2, 0) + " (blue): 1 case prevented (green).";
        TEXTOUT_P(niS, G1, 6, 189, 1, esGRN, 308);
    END;
    IF niM > 100 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("NNT > 100: only the first 100 are drawn.", G1, 6, 202, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P("Grey = the rest of 100 people, for scale.", G1, 6, 202, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    END;
    es_DrawFooter(1);
    RETURN 1;
END;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MODULE 3: SENSITIVITY & SPECIFICITY
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// v1.5: PPV and NPV at ANY prevalence, from Bayes' theorem.  The
// test's sensitivity and specificity stay fixed; only p (the
// prevalence, the share of the tested group who have the disease)
// changes:
//   PPV = sens·p / (sens·p + (1 - spec)(1 - p))
//   NPV = spec(1 - p) / (spec(1 - p) + (1 - sens)·p)
// At the sample's own prevalence p = (a+c)/N these give back a/(a+b)
// and d/(c+d) exactly.  Each returns -1 when the ratio is 0/0 (for
// example PPV at p = 0 for a test with specificity 100 %: nobody
// tests positive), and -1 is never drawn.
es_PPV(pvSe, pvSp, pvP)
BEGIN
    LOCAL pvD;
    pvD := pvSe * pvP + (1 - pvSp) * (1 - pvP);
    IF pvD <= 0 THEN RETURN -1; END;
    RETURN pvSe * pvP / pvD;
END;

es_NPV(nvSe, nvSp, nvP)
BEGIN
    LOCAL nvD;
    nvD := nvSp * (1 - nvP) + (1 - nvSe) * nvP;
    IF nvD <= 0 THEN RETURN -1; END;
    RETURN nvSp * (1 - nvP) / nvD;
END;

// A value in 0..1 as a pixel row of the prevalence plot (y grows
// DOWN the screen, so 1 is at the top, esPT); -1 stays -1.
es_PixY(pyV)
BEGIN
    IF pyV < 0 THEN RETURN -1; END;
    RETURN esPB - IP(pyV * (esPB - esPT) + 0.5);
END;

// A proportion as "16.1 %", or "undef".
// v1.7b: rounded first (a PPV of 0.04 % read "4.0ᴇ-2 %").
es_PctTxt(ptV)
BEGIN
    IF ptV < 0 THEN RETURN "undef"; END;
    RETURN STRING(ROUND(100 * ptV, 1), 2, 1) + " %";
END;

// One frame of the prevalence page (page 4 of 5), at prevalence
// esPv.  The two curves come from the caches esPPY / esNPY that
// es_PrevPage filled once, so a frame costs two LINE_P per pixel
// column and no arithmetic per column -- cheap enough to redraw on
// every sample of a dragging finger, even on a G1.
//   magenta  PPV: P(disease | test +)
//   blue     NPV: P(no disease | test -)
//   red      the sample's own prevalence ("you are here")
//   grey     the prevalence being looked at (the finger)
es_PrevDraw(pdSe, pdSp, pdP0)
BEGIN
    LOCAL pdI, pdX, pdY, pdPPV, pdNPV, pdXs;
    LOCAL pdXc, pdW, pdS;
    pdW := esPR - esPL;                       // 272 pixel columns
    pdPPV := es_PPV(pdSe, pdSp, esPv);
    pdNPV := es_NPV(pdSe, pdSp, esPv);
    es_DrawHeader("PPV VS PREVALENCE", "4/6");
    // ---- the two numbers, big, in their curves' colours ----
    TEXTOUT_P("PPV = " + es_PctTxt(pdPPV), G1, 6, 27, 5, esMAG, 154);
    TEXTOUT_P("NPV = " + es_PctTxt(pdNPV), G1, 164, 27, 5, esFOOT, 152);
    TEXTOUT_P("sens = " + STRING(ROUND(100 * pdSe, 1), 2, 1) + " %, spec = " + STRING(ROUND(100 * pdSp, 1), 2, 1) + " %", G1, 6, 49, 1, esGRAY, 200);
    TEXTOUT_P("sample: " + STRING(ROUND(100 * pdP0, 1), 2, 1) + " %", G1, 212, 49, 1, esRED, 104);
    // ---- grid, ticks and labels: 0, 25, 50, 75, 100 % both ways ----
    FOR pdI FROM 0 TO 4 DO
        pdY := esPB - IP(pdI * (esPB - esPT) / 4 + 0.5);
        LINE_P(G1, esPL, pdY, esPR, pdY, esGRID);
        TEXTOUT_P(STRING(25 * pdI, 2, 0), G1, 6, pdY - 5, 1, esGRAY, 26);
        pdX := esPL + IP(pdI * pdW / 4 + 0.5);
        LINE_P(G1, pdX, esPT, pdX, esPB, esGRID);
        TEXTOUT_P(STRING(25 * pdI, 2, 0) + "%", G1, MIN(pdX - 8, 290), esPB + 8, 1, esGRAY, 30);
    END;
    // ---- the two curves, pixel column by pixel column, from the cache ----
    FOR pdI FROM 1 TO pdW DO
        pdX := esPL + pdI;
        IF esPPY(pdI) >= 0 AND esPPY(pdI + 1) >= 0 THEN
            LINE_P(G1, pdX - 1, esPPY(pdI), pdX, esPPY(pdI + 1), esMAG);
        END;
        IF esNPY(pdI) >= 0 AND esNPY(pdI + 1) >= 0 THEN
            LINE_P(G1, pdX - 1, esNPY(pdI), pdX, esNPY(pdI + 1), esFOOT);
        END;
    END;
    LINE_P(G1, esPL, esPT, esPL, esPB, #000000h);       // the axes
    LINE_P(G1, esPL, esPB, esPR, esPB, #000000h);
    // ---- the prevalence being looked at: a grey line, a dot on each
    //      curve, and the value riding along at the top ----
    pdXc := esPL + IP(esPv * pdW + 0.5);
    LINE_P(G1, pdXc, esPT, pdXc, esPB, esGRAY);
    pdY := es_PixY(pdPPV);
    IF pdY >= 0 THEN RECT_P(G1, pdXc - 3, pdY - 3, pdXc + 3, pdY + 3, esMAG, esMAG); END;
    pdY := es_PixY(pdNPV);
    IF pdY >= 0 THEN RECT_P(G1, pdXc - 3, pdY - 3, pdXc + 3, pdY + 3, esFOOT, esFOOT); END;
    pdS := "p = " + STRING(ROUND(100 * esPv, 1), 2, 1) + " %";
    IF pdXc > esPR - 76 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P(pdS, G1, pdXc - 74, esPT + 2, 1, #000000h, 72);
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P(pdS, G1, pdXc + 4, esPT + 2, 1, #000000h, 72);
    END;
    // ---- you are here: the sample's own prevalence, in red ----
    pdXs := esPL + IP(pdP0 * pdW + 0.5);
    RECT_P(G1, pdXs - 2, esPB + 1, pdXs + 2, esPB + 6, esRED, esRED);
    pdY := es_PixY(es_PPV(pdSe, pdSp, pdP0));
    IF pdY >= 0 THEN RECT_P(G1, pdXs - 2, pdY - 2, pdXs + 2, pdY + 2, esRED, esRED); END;
    pdY := es_PixY(es_NPV(pdSe, pdSp, pdP0));
    IF pdY >= 0 THEN RECT_P(G1, pdXs - 2, pdY - 2, pdXs + 2, pdY + 2, esRED, esRED); END;
    // ---- the sentence the page exists for ----
    TEXTOUT_P("Same test: at prevalence " + STRING(ROUND(100 * esPv, 1), 2, 1) + " % a positive is", G1, 6, 169, 1, #000000h, 308);
    IF pdPPV >= 0 AND pdNPV >= 0 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("right " + es_PctTxt(pdPPV) + " of the time, a negative " + es_PctTxt(pdNPV) + ".", G1, 6, 181, 1, #000000h, 308);
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P("(PPV or NPV is 0/0 here: undefined)", G1, 6, 181, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    END;
    // ---- the slider: the same x scale as the plot ----
    RECT_P(G1, esPL, esSLT, esPR, esSLB, esGRAY, esLBLU);
    IF pdXc > esPL THEN RECT_P(G1, esPL, esSLT, pdXc, esSLB, esBLUE, esMBLU); END;
    LINE_P(G1, pdXs, esSLT - 4, pdXs, esSLB + 4, esRED);
    RECT_P(G1, pdXc - 3, esSLT - 5, pdXc + 3, esSLB + 5, esBLUE, esBLUE);
    es_Foot("drag or " + CHAR(8592) + CHAR(8594) + " = prevalence, +/- = page, Esc = menu");
END;

// PAGE 4 of module 3 (v1.5): PPV and NPV against prevalence, with a
// live drag.  While a finger is down on the plot or on the slider
// the prevalence follows it and the frame is redrawn for every new
// pixel column it reaches; the arrows step 1 % (Up/Down 10 %), 0 goes
// back to the sample's own prevalence.  The page runs its own loop
// and returns what the page loop should do next, in es_WaitKey's
// codes: 0 Esc, -1 / 1 the - / + keys (Enter = +), -2 / 2 a tap on
// the left / right half outside the plot and the slider.
// ppOK = 0 when the table has an empty column: then sensitivity or
// specificity is undefined and the page only says so.
es_PrevPage(ppSe, ppSp, ppP0, ppOK)
BEGIN
    LOCAL ppK, ppM, ppM1, ppGo, ppAct, ppX;
    LOCAL ppLX, ppI, ppW, ppZn;
    IF ppOK == 0 THEN
        es_DrawHeader("PPV VS PREVALENCE", "4/6");
        TEXTOUT_P("The curves need a sensitivity AND a", G1, 12, 44, 1, #000000h, 296);
        TEXTOUT_P("specificity: type at least one Disease+", G1, 12, 58, 1, #000000h, 296);
        TEXTOUT_P("and one Disease- count in the grid.", G1, 12, 72, 1, #000000h, 296);
        es_DrawFooter(1);
        RETURN es_WaitKey();
    END;
    // ---- the caches: both curves ONCE per visit, one value per pixel
    //      column.  Sens and spec do not change while the finger
    //      drags, so the drag only has to redraw.  MAKELIST with K is
    //      the form NormExplorer and ChiTable use (it writes Home's K).
    ppW := esPR - esPL;
    esPPY := MAKELIST(0, K, 0, ppW, 1);
    esNPY := MAKELIST(0, K, 0, ppW, 1);
    FOR ppI FROM 0 TO ppW DO
        esPPY(ppI + 1) := es_PixY(es_PPV(ppSe, ppSp, ppI / ppW));
        esNPY(ppI + 1) := es_PixY(es_NPV(ppSe, ppSp, ppI / ppW));
    END;
    ppGo := 1; ppAct := 0;
    REPEAT ppK := GETKEY; UNTIL ppK == -1;      // drain the key or tap that
    es_Drain();                                 // brought us here
    WHILE ppGo == 1 DO
        es_PrevDraw(ppSe, ppSp, ppP0);
        REPEAT
            ppK := GETKEY;
            ppM := MOUSE;
            ppM1 := ppM(1);
        UNTIL ppK > -1 OR SIZE(ppM1) > 0;
        IF SIZE(ppM1) > 0 THEN
            // a finger is down (MOUSE list: {x, y, x0, y0, type}).
            // On the plot or the slider it sets the prevalence; anywhere
            // else it is a page turn, as on every other page.
            ppZn := 0;
            IF ppM1(2) >= esPT - 6 AND ppM1(2) <= esPB + 6 THEN ppZn := 1; END;
            IF ppM1(2) >= esSLT - 12 AND ppM1(2) < 218 THEN ppZn := 1; END;
            IF ppZn == 1 THEN
                // the live drag: the first sample is the press, later
                // ones the drag; x maps through the SAME esPL..esPR the
                // drawing uses.  Redraw only when the column changes.
                // The prevalence is rounded to 0.1 %, the precision the
                // page prints (one pixel is about 0.37 %), so a student
                // who checks the digits by hand gets the same answer.
                // Esc ends a drag whose touch reading sticks.
                ppLX := -1;
                REPEAT
                    ppX := MIN(MAX(ppM1(1), esPL), esPR);
                    IF ppX <> ppLX THEN
                        esPv := ROUND((ppX - esPL) / ppW, 3);
                        es_PrevDraw(ppSe, ppSp, ppP0);
                        ppLX := ppX;
                    END;
                    ppM := MOUSE;
                    ppM1 := ppM(1);
                UNTIL SIZE(ppM1) == 0 OR GETKEY == 4;
                es_Drain();
            ELSE
                es_Drain();
                IF ppM1(1) < 160 THEN ppAct := -2; ELSE ppAct := 2; END;
                ppGo := 0;
            END;
        ELSE
            // the keyboard does everything the finger does
            IF ppK == 7 THEN esPv := MAX(0, ROUND(100 * esPv, 0) / 100 - 0.01); END;
            IF ppK == 8 THEN esPv := MIN(1, ROUND(100 * esPv, 0) / 100 + 0.01); END;
            IF ppK == 12 THEN esPv := MAX(0, ROUND(100 * esPv, 0) / 100 - 0.1); END;
            IF ppK == 2 THEN esPv := MIN(1, ROUND(100 * esPv, 0) / 100 + 0.1); END;
            IF ppK == 42 THEN esPv := ppP0; END;                  // 0: your sample
            IF ppK == 4 THEN ppAct := 0; ppGo := 0; END;          // Esc: menu
            IF ppK == 50 OR ppK == 30 THEN ppAct := 1; ppGo := 0; END;   // + or Enter: on
            IF ppK == 45 THEN ppAct := -1; ppGo := 0; END;        // -: back
        END;
    END;
    REPEAT ppK := GETKEY; UNTIL ppK == -1;
    RETURN ppAct;
END;

// PAGE 5 of module 3 (v1.5): the test as ONE point on the ROC square.
// x = 1 - specificity (the false-positive rate), y = sensitivity (the
// true-positive rate).  The dotted diagonal is a test no better than
// chance.  The line from (0,0) through the point has slope
// sens / (1 - spec) = LR+; the line from (1,1) through it has slope
// (1 - sens) / spec = LR-.  A steep LR+ line and a flat LR- line
// mean a test whose results move the odds a lot.
es_ROCPage(rcSe, rcSp, rcLRp, rcLRn, rcOK)
BEGIN
    LOCAL rcX0, rcX1, rcY0, rcY1, rcI, rcFx;
    LOCAL rcPx, rcPy, rcM, rcEx, rcEy, rcLx;
    LOCAL rcLy;
    es_DrawHeader("ROC POINT", "5/6");
    IF rcOK == 0 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("The point needs a sensitivity AND a", G1, 12, 44, 1, #000000h, 296);
        TEXTOUT_P("specificity: type at least one Disease+", G1, 12, 58, 1, #000000h, 296);
        TEXTOUT_P("and one Disease- count in the grid.", G1, 12, 72, 1, #000000h, 296);
        es_DrawFooter(1);
        RETURN 0;
    END;
    // the unit square, 120 px a side: x from rcX0 (0) to rcX1 (1),
    // y from rcY0 (0, the bottom) up to rcY1 (1, the top)
    rcX0 := 40; rcX1 := 160; rcY0 := 154; rcY1 := 34;
    RECT_P(G1, rcX0, rcY1, rcX1, rcY0, #000000h, #FFFFFFh);
    FOR rcI FROM 1 TO 3 DO
        LINE_P(G1, rcX0 + 30 * rcI, rcY1 + 1, rcX0 + 30 * rcI, rcY0 - 1, esGRID);
        LINE_P(G1, rcX0 + 1, rcY0 - 30 * rcI, rcX1 - 1, rcY0 - 30 * rcI, esGRID);
    END;
    // the chance diagonal, dotted (LINE_P has no dash style)
    FOR rcI FROM 0 TO 120 STEP 3 DO
        PIXON_P(G1, rcX0 + rcI, rcY0 - rcI, esGRAY);
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P("0", G1, rcX0 - 3, rcY0 + 3, 1, esGRAY, 10);
    TEXTOUT_P("1", G1, rcX1 - 3, rcY0 + 3, 1, esGRAY, 10);
    TEXTOUT_P("1 - spec (FPR)", G1, rcX0 + 22, rcY0 + 3, 1, esGRAY, 96);
    TEXTOUT_P("1", G1, rcX0 - 10, rcY1 - 4, 1, esGRAY, 10);
    TEXTOUT_P("sens", G1, 4, 82, 1, esGRAY, 34);
    TEXTOUT_P("(TPR)", G1, 2, 95, 1, esGRAY, 36);
    rcFx := 1 - rcSp;
    rcPx := rcX0 + IP(rcFx * 120 + 0.5);
    rcPy := rcY0 - IP(rcSe * 120 + 0.5);
    // ---- LR+: from (0,0) through the point to the edge of the square.
    //      Dividing the point by its larger coordinate puts it on the
    //      edge without changing the slope.
    rcM := MAX(rcFx, rcSe);
    IF rcM > 0 THEN
        rcEx := rcX0 + ROUND(rcFx / rcM * 120, 0);
        rcEy := rcY0 - ROUND(rcSe / rcM * 120, 0);
        LINE_P(G1, rcX0, rcY0, rcEx, rcEy, esFOOT);
        rcLx := rcX0 + ROUND(0.6 * (rcPx - rcX0), 0) + 5;
        rcLy := rcY0 + ROUND(0.6 * (rcPy - rcY0), 0) - 4;
        TEXTOUT_P("LR+", G1, rcLx, rcLy, 1, esFOOT, 24);
    END;
    // ---- LR-: from (1,1) through the point, the same way ----
    rcM := MAX(rcSp, 1 - rcSe);
    IF rcM > 0 THEN
        rcEx := rcX1 + ROUND((rcFx - 1) / rcM * 120, 0);
        rcEy := rcY1 - ROUND((rcSe - 1) / rcM * 120, 0);
        LINE_P(G1, rcX1, rcY1, rcEx, rcEy, esMAG);
        rcLx := rcX1 + ROUND(0.6 * (rcPx - rcX1), 0) - 10;
        rcLy := rcY1 + ROUND(0.6 * (rcPy - rcY1), 0) + 4;
        TEXTOUT_P("LR-", G1, rcLx, rcLy, 1, esMAG, 24);
    END;
    // ---- the test itself: the red dot, drawn last ----
    RECT_P(G1, rcPx - 3, rcPy - 3, rcPx + 3, rcPy + 3, esRED, esRED);
    // ---- the numbers beside the square ----
    TEXTOUT_P("Red dot = this test", G1, 170, 30, 1, esRED, 146);
    TEXTOUT_P("TPR = sens = " + STRING(ROUND(100 * rcSe, 1), 2, 1) + " %", G1, 170, 43, 1, #000000h, 146);
    TEXTOUT_P("FPR = 1-spec = " + STRING(ROUND(100 * rcFx, 1), 2, 1) + " %", G1, 170, 56, 1, #000000h, 146);
    TEXTOUT_P("LR+ = slope from (0,0)", G1, 170, 74, 1, esFOOT, 146);
    TEXTOUT_P(" = sens / (1 - spec)", G1, 170, 87, 1, esFOOT, 146);
    // v1.7c: es_EqTxt -- " = 0.005/0.900 < 0.01", never "= < 0.01"
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt(" = " + STRING(ROUND(rcSe, 3), 2, 3) + "/" + STRING(ROUND(rcFx, 3), 2, 3), es_FmtNum(rcLRp, 2)), G1, 170, 100, 1, esFOOT, 146);
    TEXTOUT_P("LR- = slope from (1,1)", G1, 170, 118, 1, esMAG, 146);
    TEXTOUT_P(" = (1 - sens) / spec", G1, 170, 131, 1, esMAG, 146);
    // v1.7c: es_EqTxt, as above
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt(" = " + STRING(ROUND(1 - rcSe, 3), 2, 3) + "/" + STRING(ROUND(rcSp, 3), 2, 3), es_FmtNum(rcLRn, 3)), G1, 170, 144, 1, esMAG, 146);
    TEXTOUT_P("Dotted line = chance: LR+ = LR- = 1.", G1, 6, 172, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    TEXTOUT_P("Youden J = sens + spec - 1 = " + STRING(ROUND(rcSe + rcSp - 1, 3), 2, 3) + ":", G1, 6, 185, 1, #000000h, 308);
    TEXTOUT_P("the dot's height above the dotted line.", G1, 6, 198, 1, #000000h, 308);
    es_DrawFooter(1);
    RETURN 1;
END;

// v1.7, module 3 page 6: a value 0..1 as a pixel column of the bars
// (x 110 = 0 %, 306 = 100 %), and one interval drawn as a 2-px bar
// with end caps.
es_XPx(xxV)
BEGIN
  RETURN 110 + IP(MAX(0, MIN(1, xxV)) * 196 + 0.5);
END;

es_XBar(xbLo, xbHi, xbY, xbCol)
BEGIN
  LOCAL xbA, xbB;
  xbA := es_XPx(xbLo); xbB := es_XPx(xbHi);
  LINE_P(G1, xbA, xbY, xbB, xbY, xbCol);
  LINE_P(G1, xbA, xbY + 1, xbB, xbY + 1, xbCol);
  LINE_P(G1, xbA, xbY - 2, xbA, xbY + 3, xbCol);
  LINE_P(G1, xbB, xbY - 2, xbB, xbY + 3, xbCol);
END;

// The CAS line for row xcR of page 6 (an event: the page opening or a
// tap on the row), once per row per table.  Only a count of 0 or n has
// a closed form: then the tail is a single term and
//   k = 0:  upper = 1 - (alpha/2)^(1/n) = 1-(1/40)^(1/n) at 95 %
//   k = n:  lower =     (alpha/2)^(1/n) =   (1/40)^(1/n)
// The CAS writes it exactly (and simplifies n = 1 and 2: 39/40, and
// 1-sqrt(1/40) in desktop Giac, which the Prime writes with the root
// sign, 1-√(1/40)).  simplify() is NOT used: in Giac 1.9 it turns many
// of these roots into "Root of ..." or an error.
es_XCas(xcR, xcK, xcN)
BEGIN
  LOCAL xcS;
  IF esXCK(xcR) <> 0 THEN RETURN 0; END;
  IF xcK == 0 THEN
    xcS := "1-(1/40)^(1/" + es_IStr(xcN) + ")";
  ELSE
    xcS := "(1/40)^(1/" + es_IStr(xcN) + ")";
  END;
  esXCT(xcR) := es_CasShow(es_Cas(xcS));
  esXCK(xcR) := 2;
  IF DIM(esXCT(xcR)) > 0 THEN esXCK(xcR) := 1; END;
  RETURN 1;
END;

// PAGE 6 of module 3 (v1.7): EXACT vs WILSON.  Each proportion is a
// row with three bars on one 0-100 % axis: Wilson (black), exact
// Clopper-Pearson (orange) and mid-P (green), and the estimate as a
// red tick through them.  The exact bar is always the longest: it is
// built so that its coverage never falls below 95 % for any true
// value.  A tap on a row (or Up/Down) puts its numbers in the panel;
// a count of 0 or n adds the CAS's closed form of the exact end.
es_ExactPage(xpA, xpB, xpC, xpD)
BEGIN
  LOCAL epI, epY, epK, epN, epNm, epW;
  LOCAL epS, epX, epR, epV, epT, epL;
  epNm := {"Sens", "Spec", "PPV", "NPV"};
  epL := {"Sensitivity a/(a+c)", "Specificity d/(b+d)", "PPV a/(a+b)", "NPV d/(c+d)"};
  epK := {xpA, xpD, xpA, xpD};
  epN := {xpA + xpC, xpB + xpD, xpA + xpB, xpC + xpD};
  // mid-P: once per table, on the first visit (two BINOMIAL_CDFs a pass)
  IF esBinOK == 1 AND esXMP(1) < 0 THEN
    FOR epI FROM 1 TO 4 DO
      epV := {0, 1};
      IF epN(epI) > 0 THEN epV := es_CPInt(epK(epI), epN(epI), 0.025, 1); END;
      esXMP(2 * epI - 1) := epV(1); esXMP(2 * epI) := epV(2);
    END;
  END;
  // the CAS line for the picked row, when its count is 0 or n
  epR := esXSel;
  IF esBinOK == 1 AND epN(epR) > 0 THEN
    IF epK(epR) == 0 OR epK(epR) == epN(epR) THEN es_XCas(epR, epK(epR), epN(epR)); END;
  END;
  es_DrawHeader("EXACT vs WILSON", "6/6");
  // ---- the axis: grid at 0, 25, 50, 75, 100 % ----
  FOR epI FROM 0 TO 4 DO
    epX := es_XPx(epI / 4);
    LINE_P(G1, epX, 26, epX, 154, esGRID);
    epS := STRING(25 * epI, 2, 0);
    IF epI == 4 THEN epS := "100%"; epX := epX - 14; END;    // kept on the screen
    TEXTOUT_P(epS, G1, epX - 6, 156, 1, esGRAY, 30);
  END;
  // ---- the four rows ----
  FOR epI FROM 1 TO 4 DO
    epY := 28 + 32 * (epI - 1);
    IF epI == epR THEN RECT_P(G1, 2, epY - 2, 317, epY + 25, esLORN, esLORN); END;
    es_AddHit(epI, 0, epY - 2, 319, epY + 25);
    TEXTOUT_P(epNm(epI) + " " + STRING(epK(epI), 2, 0) + "/" + STRING(epN(epI), 2, 0), G1, 4, epY, 1, #000000h, 104);
    IF epN(epI) > 0 THEN
      TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(epK(epI) / epN(epI)), G1, 4, epY + 12, 1, esGRAY, 104);
      epW := es_WilsonCI(epK(epI), epN(epI));
      es_XBar(epW(1), epW(2), epY + 5, #000000h);
      IF esBinOK == 1 THEN
        es_XBar(esXCP(2 * epI - 1), esXCP(2 * epI), epY + 12, esORNG);
        es_XBar(esXMP(2 * epI - 1), esXMP(2 * epI), epY + 19, esGRN);
      END;
      epX := es_XPx(epK(epI) / epN(epI));
      LINE_P(G1, epX, epY + 1, epX, epY + 23, esRED);
    ELSE
      TEXTOUT_P("undefined (n = 0)", G1, 4, epY + 12, 1, esGRAY, 104);
    END;
  END;
  // ---- the panel: the picked row's numbers ----
  RECT_P(G1, 2, 167, 317, 216, esORNG, esLORN);
  IF epN(epR) > 0 THEN
    epS := epL(epR) + " = " + STRING(epK(epR), 2, 0) + "/" + STRING(epN(epR), 2, 0) + " = " + es_FmtPct(epK(epR) / epN(epR));
    TEXTOUT_P(epS, G1, 8, 170, 1, #000000h, 304);
    epW := es_WilsonCI(epK(epR), epN(epR));
    TEXTOUT_P("Wilson " + es_FmtCI(epW, 1), G1, 8, 182, 1, #000000h, 150);
    IF esBinOK == 1 THEN
      TEXTOUT_P("exact " + es_FmtCI({esXCP(2 * epR - 1), esXCP(2 * epR)}, 1), G1, 164, 182, 1, esORNG, 150);
      TEXTOUT_P("mid-P " + es_FmtCI({esXMP(2 * epR - 1), esXMP(2 * epR)}, 1), G1, 8, 194, 1, esGRN, 150);
      TEXTOUT_P("coverage >= 95 %", G1, 164, 194, 1, esORNG, 150);
      IF epK(epR) == 0 OR epK(epR) == epN(epR) THEN
        // the closed form: from the CAS when it answered, else the
        // formula in words with the PPL's decimal
        IF epK(epR) == 0 THEN epT := "exact upper = "; epV := esXCP(2 * epR); ELSE epT := "exact lower = "; epV := esXCP(2 * epR - 1); END;
        IF esXCK(epR) == 1 AND DIM(esXCT(epR)) <= 22 THEN
          TEXTOUT_P(epT + esXCT(epR), G1, 8, 206, 1, #000000h, 232);
          TEXTOUT_P("exact (CAS)", G1, 244, 206, 1, esFOOT, 72);
        ELSE
          IF epK(epR) == 0 THEN epS := "1 - (" + CHAR(945) + "/2)^(1/n)"; ELSE epS := "(" + CHAR(945) + "/2)^(1/n)"; END;
          TEXTOUT_P(epT + epS + " = " + STRING(ROUND(epV, 4), 2, 4), G1, 8, 206, 1, #000000h, 232);
          TEXTOUT_P("decimal", G1, 244, 206, 1, esFOOT, 72);
        END;
      ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P("bars: black Wilson, orange exact, green mid-P", G1, 8, 206, 1, esGRAY, 304);
      END;
    ELSE
      TEXTOUT_P("exact, mid-P: unavailable (BINOMIAL check)", G1, 8, 194, 1, esGRAY, 304);
    END;
  ELSE
    TEXTOUT_P(epL(epR) + ": undefined, its n is 0", G1, 8, 170, 1, #000000h, 304);
  END;
  es_Foot("tap a row = its numbers, " + CHAR(8592) + "/" + CHAR(8594) + " = page, Esc = menu");
  RETURN 1;
END;

es_Module_SensSpec()
BEGIN
    LOCAL a, b, c, d;
    LOCAL r1, r2, c1, c2, N;
    LOCAL sens, spec, ppv, npv, prev, acc;
    LOCAL LRpos, LRneg;
    LOCAL oddsR, RR;
    LOCAL kappa, youden, pe, po;
    LOCAL ea, eb, ec, ed;
    LOCAL chisq, fisher_p;
    LOCAL page, action;
    LOCAL inputOK, sOK, xcp, sOK1, sOK2;

    // v1.5: the touch grid replaces v1.4's INPUT form (Values or +
    // still opens it).  Rows = the test result, columns = the truth:
    //              Disease+   Disease-
    //   Test+       a = TP     b = FP
    //   Test-       c = FN     d = TN
    esMod := 3;
    esCel := esM3;
    inputOK := es_Grid("DIAGNOSTIC: COUNTS", {"Test+", "Test-"}, {"Disease+", "Disease-"});
    esM3 := esCel;
    IF inputOK == 0 THEN RETURN; END;
    a := esCel(1); b := esCel(2); c := esCel(3); d := esCel(4);

    // the grid never lets a negative or an empty table through; the
    // v1.4 checks stay as a guard
    IF a < 0 OR b < 0 OR c < 0 OR d < 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: Values must be ≥ 0");
        RETURN;
    END;

    N := a + b + c + d;
    IF N == 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: Table cannot be empty");
        RETURN;
    END;

    EP_table := {a, b, c, d};
    EP_N := N;

    // v1.4: every EP_* result this module sets only inside a guard
    // is reset here first (after the input is accepted, so a
    // cancelled form keeps the previous results, as EP_table does).
    // Before, a zero margin or a zero cell left the previous run's
    // value in place, and the pages below read EP_sens_CI, EP_OR_CI,
    // EP_chisq and EP_chisq_p straight from the globals -- so a stale
    // number could reach the screen.
    EP_sens := 0; EP_spec := 0; EP_ppv := 0; EP_npv := 0;
    EP_sens_CI := {0, 0}; EP_spec_CI := {0, 0};
    EP_ppv_CI := {0, 0}; EP_npv_CI := {0, 0};
    EP_LRpos := 999; EP_LRneg := 999;
    EP_OR := 999; EP_OR_CI := {0, 0}; EP_RR := 999;
    EP_chisq := 0; EP_chisq_p := 1;

    r1 := a + b;
    r2 := c + d;
    c1 := a + c;
    c2 := b + d;
    // v1.4: p1 := r1*r2*c1*c2 was computed here and never used

    IF c1 > 0 THEN
        sens := a / c1;
        EP_sens := sens;
        EP_sens_CI := es_WilsonCI(a, c1);
    ELSE
        sens := 0;
    END;

    IF c2 > 0 THEN
        spec := d / c2;
        EP_spec := spec;
        EP_spec_CI := es_WilsonCI(d, c2);
    ELSE
        spec := 0;
    END;

    IF r1 > 0 THEN
        ppv := a / r1;
        EP_ppv := ppv;
        EP_ppv_CI := es_WilsonCI(a, r1);
    ELSE
        ppv := 0;
    END;

    IF r2 > 0 THEN
        npv := d / r2;
        EP_npv := npv;
        EP_npv_CI := es_WilsonCI(d, r2);
    ELSE
        npv := 0;
    END;

    prev := c1 / N;
    acc := (a + d) / N;
    EP_acc := acc;

    // v1.7: the exact (Clopper-Pearson) CIs of the four proportions,
    // once per table (page 6 shows them beside Wilson's and mid-P's).
    // esXCP keeps the 8 ends in a flat list: sens, spec, PPV, NPV.
    EP_sens_CP := {0, 0}; EP_spec_CP := {0, 0};
    EP_ppv_CP := {0, 0}; EP_npv_CP := {0, 0};
    esXCP := {-1, -1, -1, -1, -1, -1, -1, -1};
    esXMP := {-1, -1, -1, -1, -1, -1, -1, -1};     // mid-P: on page 6's first visit
    esXSel := 1; esXCT := {"", "", "", ""}; esXCK := {0, 0, 0, 0};
    IF esBinOK == 1 THEN
        IF c1 > 0 THEN xcp := es_CPInt(a, c1, 0.025, 0); EP_sens_CP := xcp; esXCP(1) := xcp(1); esXCP(2) := xcp(2); END;
        IF c2 > 0 THEN xcp := es_CPInt(d, c2, 0.025, 0); EP_spec_CP := xcp; esXCP(3) := xcp(1); esXCP(4) := xcp(2); END;
        IF r1 > 0 THEN xcp := es_CPInt(a, r1, 0.025, 0); EP_ppv_CP := xcp; esXCP(5) := xcp(1); esXCP(6) := xcp(2); END;
        IF r2 > 0 THEN xcp := es_CPInt(d, r2, 0.025, 0); EP_npv_CP := xcp; esXCP(7) := xcp(1); esXCP(8) := xcp(2); END;
    END;

    IF spec < 1 THEN
        LRpos := sens / (1 - spec);
        EP_LRpos := LRpos;
    ELSE
        LRpos := 999;
    END;

    IF spec > 0 THEN
        LRneg := (1 - sens) / spec;
        EP_LRneg := LRneg;
    ELSE
        LRneg := 999;
    END;

    youden := sens + spec - 1;
    EP_youden := youden;

    // v1.7d (E3): sensitivity is a/(a+c) over the people WITH the
    // disease, specificity d/(b+d) over those without.  An empty column
    // leaves one of them undefined -- not 0 %, which v1.7c printed (the
    // 0 above is only a placeholder).  sOK1 = 1 when there is anyone
    // with the disease, sOK2 = 1 when there is anyone without.  LR+ =
    // sens/(1-spec), LR- = (1-sens)/spec and Youden's J = sens + spec - 1
    // need BOTH, so they take the 999 sentinel ("undef") when either
    // column is empty, on the pages and in the EP_* results alike.
    sOK1 := 0; IF c1 > 0 THEN sOK1 := 1; END;
    sOK2 := 0; IF c2 > 0 THEN sOK2 := 1; END;
    IF sOK1 == 0 OR sOK2 == 0 THEN
        LRpos := 999; LRneg := 999; youden := 999;
        EP_LRpos := 999; EP_LRneg := 999; EP_youden := 999;
    END;

    pe := (r1/N)*(c1/N) + (r2/N)*(c2/N);
    po := (a + d) / N;
    IF pe < 1 THEN
        kappa := (po - pe) / (1 - pe);
    ELSE
        kappa := 1;
    END;
    EP_kappa := kappa;

    IF b > 0 AND c > 0 THEN
        oddsR := (a * d) / (b * c);
        EP_OR := oddsR;
        EP_OR_CI := es_CalcORCI(a, b, c, d);
    ELSE
        oddsR := 999;
    END;

    IF r1 > 0 AND r2 > 0 AND c > 0 THEN
        RR := (a/r1) / (c/r2);
        EP_RR := RR;
    ELSE
        RR := 999;
    END;

    ea := r1*c1/N; eb := r1*c2/N; ec := r2*c1/N; ed := r2*c2/N;
    IF ea > 0 AND eb > 0 AND ec > 0 AND ed > 0 THEN
        chisq := (a-ea)^2/ea + (b-eb)^2/eb + (c-ec)^2/ec + (d-ed)^2/ed;
        EP_chisq := chisq;
        // v1.7c: the upper tail with 1 df, 2 NORMALD_CDF(-sqrt(x)) (never exactly 0)
        EP_chisq_p := 2 * NORMALD_CDF(0, 1, -(chisq^0.5));
    END;

    fisher_p := es_FisherExact(a, b, c, d);
    EP_fisher_p := fisher_p;

    // v1.5: five pages.  Page 4 (PPV and NPV against prevalence) runs
    // its own touch loop and hands back what the page loop should do;
    // page 5 is the ROC square.  Both need a sensitivity AND a
    // specificity, so both columns must hold someone (sOK).
    sOK := 0;
    IF c1 > 0 AND c2 > 0 THEN sOK := 1; END;
    esPv := prev;                       // page 4 opens at the sample's prevalence
    page := 1;
    REPEAT
        CASE
            IF page == 1 THEN
                // v1.7d (E3): which columns hold anyone, for "undef"
                es_SS_Page1(sens, spec, ppv, npv, LRpos, LRneg, acc, prev, sOK1, sOK2);
            END;
            IF page == 2 THEN
                es_SS_Page2(oddsR, RR, kappa, youden, po, pe);
            END;
            IF page == 3 THEN
                es_SS_Page3(a, b, c, d, r1, r2, c1, c2, N);
            END;
            IF page == 5 THEN
                es_ROCPage(sens, spec, LRpos, LRneg, sOK);
            END;
            // v1.7: exact against Wilson
            IF page == 6 THEN
                es_ExactPage(a, b, c, d);
            END;
        END;
        IF page == 4 THEN
            action := es_PrevPage(sens, spec, prev, sOK);
        ELSE
            action := es_WaitKey();
        END;
        IF action == 0 THEN BREAK; END;
        // v1.7: page 6 keeps a tap on a row and Up/Down for itself (the
        // row whose numbers the panel shows); everything else turns pages
        IF page == 6 AND action >= 3 AND action <= 5 THEN
            IF action == 3 THEN esXSel := MAX(1, MIN(4, esHit)); END;
            IF action == 4 THEN
                esXSel := esXSel - 1;
                IF esXSel < 1 THEN esXSel := 4; END;
            END;
            IF action == 5 THEN
                esXSel := esXSel + 1;
                IF esXSel > 4 THEN esXSel := 1; END;
            END;
        ELSE
            page := es_TurnPage(page, action, 6);   // taps turn pages too; v1.7: six pages
        END;
    UNTIL 0;
END;

es_SS_Page1(sens, spec, ppv, npv, LRpos, LRneg, acc, prev, sOK1, sOK2)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y, s1U, s1S;
    // v1.7d (E3): sOK1 = 0 when no one has the disease (a + c = 0), so
    // sensitivity is undefined; sOK2 = 0 when everyone has it (b + d =
    // 0), so specificity is.  s1U says why a ratio that needs both is
    // undefined (at most one column can be empty: the table is not).
    s1U := "";
    IF sOK2 == 0 THEN s1U := "undef (no Disease-)"; END;
    IF sOK1 == 0 THEN s1U := "undef (no Disease+)"; END;
    es_DrawHeader("DIAGNOSTIC ACCURACY", "1/6");
    y := 30;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Primary Measures ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("Sensitivity:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    // v1.7d (E3): "undef (no Disease+)", not "0.0%"; its CI is blank then
    // (EP_sens_CI stays {0, 0}, which es_FmtCI prints as nothing)
    s1S := es_FmtPct(sens);
    IF sOK1 == 0 THEN s1S := "undef (no Disease+)"; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(s1S, G1, 120, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtCI(EP_sens_CI, 1), G1, 180, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Specificity:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    // v1.7d (E3): the same for specificity, with no Disease- column
    s1S := es_FmtPct(spec);
    IF sOK2 == 0 THEN s1S := "undef (no Disease-)"; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(s1S, G1, 120, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtCI(EP_spec_CI, 1), G1, 180, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("PPV:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(ppv), G1, 120, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtCI(EP_ppv_CI, 1), G1, 180, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("NPV:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(npv), G1, 120, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtCI(EP_npv_CI, 1), G1, 180, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Likelihood Ratios ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("LR+ (positive):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    // v1.7d (E3): an LR needs both sensitivity and specificity; with a
    // column empty it reads why ("undef (no Disease+)"), not 0.00
    s1S := es_FmtNum(LRpos, 2);
    IF sOK1 == 0 OR sOK2 == 0 THEN s1S := s1U; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(s1S, G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("LR- (negative):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    s1S := es_FmtNum(LRneg, 3);
    IF sOK1 == 0 OR sOK2 == 0 THEN s1S := s1U; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(s1S, G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Other ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("Accuracy:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(acc), G1, 100, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P("Prevalence:", G1, 160, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(prev), G1, 250, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("FPR (α):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    // v1.7d (E3): FPR = 1 - spec and FNR = 1 - sens are undefined with
    // their column empty (v1.7c printed 100.0%); "undef" fits the slot
    s1S := es_FmtPct(1-spec);
    IF sOK2 == 0 THEN s1S := "undef"; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(s1S, G1, 100, y, 2, RGB(255,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P("FNR (β):", G1, 160, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    s1S := es_FmtPct(1-sens);
    IF sOK1 == 0 THEN s1S := "undef"; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(s1S, G1, 250, y, 2, RGB(255,0,0));
    // v1.7: where the exact intervals are
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("CIs above: Wilson.  Exact and mid-P: page 6.", G1, 10, y, 1, esORNG, 300);
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

es_SS_Page2(oddsR, RR, kappa, youden, po, pe)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y, kappaInterp;
    es_DrawHeader("AGREEMENT & RISK", "2/6");
    y := 32;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Agreement Measures ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("Cohen's Kappa:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(kappa, 3), G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    IF kappa < 0 THEN kappaInterp := "Poor";
    ELSE IF kappa < 0.2 THEN kappaInterp := "Slight";
    ELSE IF kappa < 0.4 THEN kappaInterp := "Fair";
    ELSE IF kappa < 0.6 THEN kappaInterp := "Moderate";
    ELSE IF kappa < 0.8 THEN kappaInterp := "Substantial";
    ELSE kappaInterp := "Almost Perfect";
    END; END; END; END; END;
    TEXTOUT_P("(" + kappaInterp + ")", G1, 210, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("Observed agreement:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(po), G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Expected agreement:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(pe), G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("Youden's J Index:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(youden, 3), G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 12;
    TEXTOUT_P("(Sens + Spec - 1, range -1 to 1)", G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 22;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Risk Measures ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("Odds Ratio:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(oddsR, 2), G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("95% CI: " + es_FmtCI(EP_OR_CI, 0), G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("Relative Risk:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(RR, 2), G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

es_SS_Page3(a, b, c, d, r1, r2, c1, c2, N)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y, x1, x2, x3;
    es_DrawHeader("TABLE SUMMARY", "3/6");
    x1 := 100; x2 := 170; x3 := 240;
    y := 32;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Observed Values ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Disease+", G1, x1, y, 1, RGB(0,60,200));
    TEXTOUT_P("Disease-", G1, x2, y, 1, RGB(0,60,200));
    TEXTOUT_P("Total", G1, x3, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Test+", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(a,1,0), G1, x1+15, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(b,1,0), G1, x2+15, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(r1,1,0), G1, x3+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Test-", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(c,1,0), G1, x1+15, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(d,1,0), G1, x2+15, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(r2,1,0), G1, x3+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Total", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(c1,1,0), G1, x1+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(c2,1,0), G1, x2+10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(N,1,0), G1, x3+10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 22;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Chi-Square & Fisher's ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("Pearson χ²:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(EP_chisq, 3), G1, 120, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    // v1.7c: the compact "p=" gives way to "p<0.0001" below the floor (never "p=< 0.0001")
    TEXTOUT_P(IFTE(EP_chisq_p < 0.0001, "p<0.0001", "p=" + es_FmtNum(EP_chisq_p, 4)), G1, 200, y, 2,
              IFTE(EP_chisq_p < 0.05, RGB(255,0,0), RGB(0,0,0)));
    y := y + 16;
    IF EP_fisher_p >= 0 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("Fisher's p:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
        TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(EP_fisher_p, 5), G1, 120, y, 2,
                  IFTE(EP_fisher_p < 0.05, RGB(255,0,0), RGB(0,0,0)));
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P("N > 2000: Use χ² approximation", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    END;
    y := y + 20;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Cell Labels ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("a=TP  b=FP  c=FN  d=TN", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MODULE 4: INCIDENCE RATES (Person-Time Analysis)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

es_Module_IncidenceRate()
BEGIN
    LOCAL cexp, u, pte, ptu;
    LOCAL IRe, IRu, IRt, IRR, IRD;
    LOCAL APe, APt, ERR;
    LOCAL WTe, WTu, WTt;
    LOCAL page, action;
    LOCAL inputOK;

    cexp := 0; u := 0; pte := 0; ptu := 0;

    inputOK := INPUT(
        {cexp, u, pte, ptu},
        "Incidence Rate Analysis",
        {"Cases (exposed):", "Cases (unexposed):", "Person-Time (exp):", "Person-Time (unexp):"},
        {"Number of cases among exposed",
         "Number of cases among unexposed",
         "Total person-time for exposed group",
         "Total person-time for unexposed group"},
        {0, 0, 0, 0}
    );

    IF inputOK == 0 THEN RETURN; END;

    IF cexp < 0 OR u < 0 OR pte <= 0 OR ptu <= 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: Cases ≥ 0, Person-time > 0");
        RETURN;
    END;

    // v1.7d (E9): cases are counts.  v1.7c took 2.5 cases as typed and
    // printed "2.5 cases / 1200 person-time"; the entry grid has always
    // rounded a fractional count and said so.  This form now does the
    // same (person-time may keep its decimals).
    IF FP(cexp) <> 0 OR FP(u) <> 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Cases are whole numbers: the values were rounded.");
        cexp := ROUND(cexp, 0);
        u := ROUND(u, 0);
    END;

    IRe := cexp / pte;
    IRu := u / ptu;
    IRt := (cexp + u) / (pte + ptu);

    EP_IR_exp := IRe;
    EP_IR_unexp := IRu;

    IF IRu > 0 THEN
        IRR := IRe / IRu;
        EP_IRR := IRR;
    ELSE
        IRR := 999;
        EP_IRR := 999;       // v1.4: was left from the previous run
    END;

    IRD := IRe - IRu;
    EP_IRD := IRD;

    // v1.4: keep the 999 sentinel so the display prints "undef"
    // (it used to print 998 and 99800%).
    IF IRR == 999 THEN ERR := 999; ELSE ERR := IRR - 1; END;

    IF IRe > 0 THEN
        APe := (IRe - IRu) / IRe * 100;
    ELSE
        APe := 0;
    END;

    IF IRt > 0 THEN
        APt := (IRt - IRu) / IRt * 100;
    ELSE
        APt := 0;
    END;

    IF IRe > 0 THEN WTe := 1/IRe; ELSE WTe := 999; END;
    IF IRu > 0 THEN WTu := 1/IRu; ELSE WTu := 999; END;
    IF IRt > 0 THEN WTt := 1/IRt; ELSE WTt := 999; END;

    page := 1;
    REPEAT
        CASE
            IF page == 1 THEN
                es_IR_Page1(cexp, u, pte, ptu, IRe, IRu, IRt);
            END;
            IF page == 2 THEN
                es_IR_Page2(IRR, IRD, ERR, APe, APt);
            END;
            IF page == 3 THEN
                es_IR_Page3(WTe, WTu, WTt, IRD);
            END;
        END;
        action := es_WaitKey();
        IF action == 0 THEN BREAK; END;
        page := es_TurnPage(page, action, 3);   // v1.5: taps turn pages too
    UNTIL 0;
END;

es_IR_Page1(cexp, u, pte, ptu, IRe, IRu, IRt)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y;
    es_DrawHeader("INCIDENCE RATES", "1/3");
    y := 32;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Input Data ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("Exposed:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(cexp,1,0) + " cases / " + STRING(pte,1,0) + " person-time", G1, 100, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Unexposed:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(STRING(u,1,0) + " cases / " + STRING(ptu,1,0) + " person-time", G1, 100, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 22;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Incidence Rates ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("IR (exposed):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(IRe, 5), G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 14;
    // v1.7c: es_EqV -- "< 0.01 per 1,000 person-time", never "= < 0.01"
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqV(es_FmtNum(IRe*1000, 2)) + " per 1,000 person-time", G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("IR (unexposed):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(IRu, 5), G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 14;
    // v1.7c: es_EqV, as above
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqV(es_FmtNum(IRu*1000, 2)) + " per 1,000 person-time", G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("IR (total):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(IRt, 5), G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    // v1.7c: es_EqV, as above
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqV(es_FmtNum(IRt*1000, 2)) + " per 1,000 person-time", G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

es_IR_Page2(IRR, IRD, ERR, APe, APt)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y;
    es_DrawHeader("RATE COMPARISONS", "2/3");
    y := 32;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Rate Ratio (Relative Rate) ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    // v1.7c: es_EqLb -- no "=" in the label when the value is "< 0.001"
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqLb("IRR = IRe / IRu", es_FmtNum(IRR, 3)), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(IRR, 3), G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 22;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Rate Difference ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    // v1.7c: es_EqLb, as above
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqLb("IRD = IRe - IRu", es_FmtNum(IRD, 5)), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(IRD, 5), G1, 180, y, 2, IFTE(IRD > 0, RGB(255,0,0), RGB(0,140,60)));
    y := y + 14;
    // v1.7c: es_EqV, as above
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqV(es_FmtNum(IRD*1000, 3)) + " per 1,000 PT", G1, 20, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 22;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Excess Relative Rate ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    // v1.7c: es_EqLb, as above
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqLb("ERR = IRR - 1", es_FmtNum(ERR, 3)), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    // v1.4: ERR carries the 999 sentinel when IRR is undefined
    IF ERR == 999 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("undef (IRR undefined)", G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(ERR, 3) + " (" + es_FmtNum(ERR*100, 1) + "%)", G1, 150, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    END;
    y := y + 22;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Attributable Proportions ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("AP (exposed):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(APe, 1) + "%", G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("AP (total/pop):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(APt, 1) + "%", G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

es_IR_Page3(WTe, WTu, WTt, IRD)
BEGIN
    LOCAL y, NNT;
    es_DrawHeader("CLINICAL MEASURES", "3/3");
    y := 32;
    // v1.7d (E6): the unit is named once, in the heading, and each
    // number stands alone at x 200.  v1.7c wrote "1000000.001 time
    // units" from x 200 for 1 case in 1,000,000 person-time, and the
    // line ran off the screen (about 354 px).  The heading says "Mean"
    // (the same thing as "Average") so that it fits too: 39 characters,
    // about 306 px at the checker's widest estimate (7.6 px).
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Mean Waiting Times (time units) ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("Waiting time (exposed):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(WTe, 2), G1, 200, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("Waiting time (unexposed):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(WTu, 2), G1, 200, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 16;
    TEXTOUT_P("Waiting time (overall):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(WTt, 2), G1, 200, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 24;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Person-Time NNT ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    IF IRD <> 0 THEN
        NNT := 1/ABS(IRD);
        IF IRD > 0 THEN
            // v1.7c: es_EqLb, as above (a rate above 10 per unit of person-time)
            TEXTOUT_P(es_EqLb("PT-NNH = 1/IRD", es_FmtNum(NNT, 1)), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
            TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(NNT, 1), G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(255,0,0));
            y := y + 16;
            TEXTOUT_P("Person-time to observe 1 extra case", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
        ELSE
            // v1.7c: es_EqLb, as above
            TEXTOUT_P(es_EqLb("PT-NNT = 1/|IRD|", es_FmtNum(NNT, 1)), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
            TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtNum(NNT, 1), G1, 180, y, 2, RGB(0,140,60));
            y := y + 16;
            TEXTOUT_P("Person-time to prevent 1 case", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
        END;
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P("NNT undefined (IRD = 0)", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(90,90,90));
    END;
    y := y + 24;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Note ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Person-time units depend on your data", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 10;
    TEXTOUT_P("(e.g., person-years, person-months)", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MODULE 5: STRATIFIED ANALYSIS (Mantel-Haenszel Methods)
// v1.6: one engine for all three designs.  The two strata are typed
// in the touch grid (es_Grid), stratum 1 then stratum 2, in the
// module-1 layout, and kept in esSC = {a1, b1, c1, d1, a2, b2, c2, d2}:
//   kind 1  case-control   a = Exp+/Case    b = Exp+/Control
//                          c = Exp-/Case    d = Exp-/Control
//   kind 2  cohort         a = Exp+/Case    b = Exp+/no case
//                          c = Exp-/Case    d = Exp-/no case
//   kind 3  rates          a = Exp+ cases   b = Exp+ person-time
//                          c = Exp- cases   d = Exp- person-time
// Each design gets three pages: the strata, the M-H summary with its
// CI and tests, and a forest plot where a tap drops a stratum.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

es_Module_Stratified()
BEGIN
    LOCAL choice;
    choice := 0;   // v1.2: CHOOSE leaves its index unchanged on cancel
    CHOOSE(choice, "Stratified Analysis",
        "Case-Control (OR_MH)",
        "Cohort Study (RR_MH)",
        "Incidence Rates (IRR_MH)",
        "Back to Main Menu");
    // v1.6: the three designs share es_Strat_Run
    IF choice >= 1 AND choice <= 3 THEN es_Strat_Run(choice); END;
END;

// The two grids, stratum 1 then stratum 2 (v1.6).  The Values soft
// key opens the v1.4 four-field form for the stratum being typed.
// Each design remembers its last two strata in esMS while the
// program runs.  Returns 1 when both strata are ready, 0 on Cancel
// (Cancel on stratum 2 also goes back to the menu; nothing is lost).
es_StratGrids(sgK)
BEGIN
    LOCAL sgOK, sgS, sgT, sgCols, sgJ, sgB;
    esMod := 4 + sgK;                    // 5, 6 or 7: the grid's labels and form
    sgT := {"CASE-CONTROL", "COHORT", "RATES"};
    sgCols := {"Case", "Control"};
    IF sgK == 2 THEN sgCols := {"Outcome+", "Outcome-"}; END;
    IF sgK == 3 THEN sgCols := {"Cases", "Time (PT)"}; END;
    FOR sgS FROM 1 TO 2 DO
        esStr := sgS;
        sgB := (sgK - 1) * 8 + (sgS - 1) * 4;
        esCel := {esMS(sgB + 1), esMS(sgB + 2), esMS(sgB + 3), esMS(sgB + 4)};
        sgOK := es_Grid("STRATUM " + STRING(sgS, 2, 0) + " OF 2: " + sgT(sgK), {"Exposed+", "Exposed-"}, sgCols);
        FOR sgJ FROM 1 TO 4 DO
            esMS(sgB + sgJ) := esCel(sgJ);
            esSC((sgS - 1) * 4 + sgJ) := esCel(sgJ);
        END;
        IF sgOK == 0 THEN RETURN 0; END;
    END;
    RETURN 1;
END;

// One stratum's own estimate (v1.6): {estimate, CI low, CI high,
// M-H weight, flag}.  flag 0 = fine, 1 = 0.5 added to every cell (a
// zero cell; the odds ratio only, as es_CalcORCI does), -1 = no
// log-scale CI (the estimate is 999 = undefined, or 0).
//   OR   ad/bc,             Woolf CI (es_CalcORCI), weight bc/n
//   RR   [a/(a+b)]/[c/(c+d)], log CI (es_CalcRRCI), weight c(a+b)/n
//   IRR  (a/b)/(c/d),       CI exp(ln IRR -/+ 1.96 sqrt(1/a + 1/c)),
//                           weight c b/(b + d)
// The weights are the ones the M-H estimate gives each stratum; they
// size the forest plot's squares.
es_StratEst(seK, xsA, xsB, xsC, xsD)
BEGIN
    LOCAL seN, seE, seCI, seW, seF, seSE;
    seF := 0; seE := 999; seCI := {0, 0}; seW := 0;
    IF seK == 1 THEN
        seN := xsA + xsB + xsC + xsD;
        IF seN > 0 THEN seW := xsB * xsC / seN; END;
        IF xsA + xsB == 0 OR xsC + xsD == 0 OR xsA + xsC == 0 OR xsB + xsD == 0 THEN
            RETURN {999, 0, 0, seW, -1};          // an empty row or column
        END;
        IF xsA * xsB * xsC * xsD == 0 THEN
            seF := 1;
            seE := (xsA + 0.5) * (xsD + 0.5) / ((xsB + 0.5) * (xsC + 0.5));
        ELSE
            seE := xsA * xsD / (xsB * xsC);
        END;
        seCI := es_CalcORCI(xsA, xsB, xsC, xsD);
    END;
    IF seK == 2 THEN
        seN := xsA + xsB + xsC + xsD;
        IF seN > 0 THEN seW := xsC * (xsA + xsB) / seN; END;
        IF xsA + xsB == 0 OR xsC + xsD == 0 OR xsC == 0 THEN RETURN {999, 0, 0, seW, -1}; END;
        IF xsA == 0 THEN RETURN {0, 0, 0, seW, -1}; END;
        seE := (xsA / (xsA + xsB)) / (xsC / (xsC + xsD));
        seCI := es_CalcRRCI(xsA, xsA + xsB, xsC, xsC + xsD);
    END;
    IF seK == 3 THEN
        seN := xsB + xsD;
        IF seN > 0 THEN seW := xsC * xsB / seN; END;
        IF xsB <= 0 OR xsD <= 0 OR xsC == 0 THEN RETURN {999, 0, 0, seW, -1}; END;
        IF xsA == 0 THEN RETURN {0, 0, 0, seW, -1}; END;
        seE := (xsA / xsB) / (xsC / xsD);
        seSE := (1 / xsA + 1 / xsC)^0.5;
        seCI := {EXP(LN(seE) - 1.96 * seSE), EXP(LN(seE) + 1.96 * seSE)};
    END;
    RETURN {seE, seCI(1), seCI(2), seW, seF};
END;

// The expected a under a common odds ratio bfPsi, with the stratum's
// margins fixed (Breslow-Day).  It is the root of
//   (1 - psi) A^2 + (n - r1 - c1 + psi (r1 + c1)) A - psi r1 c1 = 0
// that lies between MAX(0, r1 + c1 - n) and MIN(r1, c1), taken in
// the form that does not lose digits when psi is near 1.
es_BDFit(bfA, bfB, bfC, bfD, bfPsi)
BEGIN
    LOCAL bfN, bfR1, bfC1, bfQa, bfQb, bfQc;
    LOCAL bfSq, bfQ, bfX1, bfLo, bfHi;
    bfN := bfA + bfB + bfC + bfD;
    bfR1 := bfA + bfB; bfC1 := bfA + bfC;
    bfLo := MAX(0, bfR1 + bfC1 - bfN);
    bfHi := MIN(bfR1, bfC1);
    bfQa := 1 - bfPsi;
    bfQb := bfN - bfR1 - bfC1 + bfPsi * (bfR1 + bfC1);
    bfQc := -bfPsi * bfR1 * bfC1;
    IF ABS(bfQa) < 0.000000000001 THEN RETURN -bfQc / bfQb; END;
    bfSq := MAX(0, bfQb^2 - 4 * bfQa * bfQc)^0.5;
    IF bfQb >= 0 THEN bfQ := -0.5 * (bfQb + bfSq); ELSE bfQ := -0.5 * (bfQb - bfSq); END;
    bfX1 := bfQ / bfQa;
    IF bfX1 >= bfLo - 0.000000001 AND bfX1 <= bfHi + 0.000000001 THEN RETURN bfX1; END;
    RETURN bfQc / bfQ;
END;

// THE MANTEL-HAENSZEL ENGINE (v1.6).  mcS = the eight cells (esSC),
// mcM = {drop1, drop2}: a stratum with 1 is left out (the forest
// plot's tap).  Returns a list of 13:
//   1-3   M-H estimate, CI low, CI high   (999 = undefined; CI 0, 0)
//   4-6   crude estimate (the included strata added together) and CI
//   7-8   M-H chi-square (1 df) and p, no continuity correction
//   9-10  the same with the correction            (-1 = undefined)
//   11-12 Breslow-Day chi-square and p, OR only   (-1 = undefined)
//   13    how many strata were included
// Formulas, all checked against statsmodels in verify_EpiStats.py:
//   OR_MH  = sum(ad/n) / sum(bc/n); var ln OR_MH by Robins, Breslow
//            and Greenland (1986): with P = (a+d)/n, Q = (b+c)/n,
//            R = ad/n, S = bc/n,
//            sum(PR)/(2 R+^2) + sum(PS+QR)/(2 R+ S+) + sum(QS)/(2 S+^2)
//   RR_MH  = sum(a n0/n) / sum(c n1/n); var ln RR_MH by Greenland and
//            Robins (1985): sum(m1 n1 n0/n^2 - ac/n) / [sum(a n0/n) sum(c n1/n)]
//   IRR_MH = sum(a T0/T) / sum(c T1/T); var ln IRR_MH (Greenland and
//            Robins 1985): sum(m1 T1 T0/T^2) / [sum(a T0/T) sum(c T1/T)]
//   CI     = exp(ln est -/+ 1.959964 SE)
//   M-H chi-square = (|sum(a - E a)| - 0.5)^2 / sum(Var a), with
//            E a = (a+b)(a+c)/n, Var a = (a+b)(c+d)(a+c)(b+d)/(n^2 (n-1));
//            the 0.5 only when |sum(a - E a)| >= 0.5 (R's rule;
//            statsmodels test_null_odds reports the uncorrected one by
//            default).  The same test of "no association" serves the
//            cohort design.  Not computed for rates.
//   Breslow-Day = sum (a - A)^2 / Var A over the strata, A the
//            expected a under OR_MH (es_BDFit), 1/Var A = 1/A +
//            1/(r1-A) + 1/(c1-A) + 1/(n-r1-c1+A); k - 1 df; no Tarone
//            adjustment (statsmodels test_equal_odds' default).
es_MHCalc(mcK, mcS, mcM)
BEGIN
    LOCAL mcI, mcA, mcB, mcC, mcD, mcN;
    LOCAL mcR, mcSS, mcPR, mcX, mcQS, mcNI;
    LOCAL mcDl, mcVs, mcE, mcLo, mcHi, mcV;
    LOCAL mcCr, mcBD, mcPB, mcX2, mcX2c, mcP;
    LOCAL mcPc, mcAs, mcBs, mcCs, mcDs, mcSe;
    LOCAL mcP1, mcP2, mcFA, mcVa, mcNB, mcR1;
    LOCAL mcC1;
    mcR := 0; mcSS := 0; mcPR := 0; mcX := 0; mcQS := 0; mcNI := 0;
    mcDl := 0; mcVs := 0; mcAs := 0; mcBs := 0; mcCs := 0; mcDs := 0;
    FOR mcI FROM 1 TO 2 DO
        IF mcM(mcI) == 0 THEN
            mcA := mcS(4 * mcI - 3); mcB := mcS(4 * mcI - 2);
            mcC := mcS(4 * mcI - 1); mcD := mcS(4 * mcI);
            mcAs := mcAs + mcA; mcBs := mcBs + mcB;
            mcCs := mcCs + mcC; mcDs := mcDs + mcD;
            mcNI := mcNI + 1;
            IF mcK == 3 THEN
                mcN := mcB + mcD;                   // person-time T = T1 + T0
                IF mcN > 0 THEN
                    mcR := mcR + mcA * mcD / mcN;
                    mcSS := mcSS + mcC * mcB / mcN;
                    mcPR := mcPR + (mcA + mcC) * mcB * mcD / mcN^2;
                END;
            ELSE
                mcN := mcA + mcB + mcC + mcD;
                IF mcN > 0 THEN
                    IF mcK == 1 THEN
                        mcP1 := (mcA + mcD) / mcN; mcP2 := (mcB + mcC) / mcN;
                        mcR := mcR + mcA * mcD / mcN;
                        mcSS := mcSS + mcB * mcC / mcN;
                        mcPR := mcPR + mcP1 * mcA * mcD / mcN;
                        mcX := mcX + mcP1 * mcB * mcC / mcN + mcP2 * mcA * mcD / mcN;
                        mcQS := mcQS + mcP2 * mcB * mcC / mcN;
                    ELSE
                        mcR := mcR + mcA * (mcC + mcD) / mcN;
                        mcSS := mcSS + mcC * (mcA + mcB) / mcN;
                        mcPR := mcPR + (mcA + mcC) * (mcA + mcB) * (mcC + mcD) / mcN^2 - mcA * mcC / mcN;
                    END;
                END;
                IF mcN >= 2 THEN                     // the M-H chi-square sums
                    mcDl := mcDl + mcA - (mcA + mcB) * (mcA + mcC) / mcN;
                    mcVs := mcVs + (mcA + mcB) * (mcC + mcD) * (mcA + mcC) * (mcB + mcD) / (mcN^2 * (mcN - 1));
                END;
            END;
        END;
    END;
    // ---- the M-H estimate and its CI ----
    mcE := 999; mcLo := 0; mcHi := 0;
    IF mcSS > 0 THEN
        mcE := mcR / mcSS;
        IF mcR > 0 THEN
            IF mcK == 1 THEN
                mcV := mcPR / (2 * mcR^2) + mcX / (2 * mcR * mcSS) + mcQS / (2 * mcSS^2);
            ELSE
                mcV := mcPR / (mcR * mcSS);
            END;
            mcSe := MAX(0, mcV)^0.5;
            mcLo := EXP(LN(mcE) - 1.959964 * mcSe);
            mcHi := EXP(LN(mcE) + 1.959964 * mcSe);
        END;
    END;
    // ---- the crude estimate: the included strata added together ----
    mcCr := es_StratEst(mcK, mcAs, mcBs, mcCs, mcDs);
    IF mcCr(5) == -1 THEN mcCr := {mcCr(1), 0, 0, 0, -1}; END;
    // ---- the M-H chi-square ----
    mcX2 := -1; mcP := -1; mcX2c := -1; mcPc := -1;
    IF mcK < 3 AND mcVs > 0 THEN
        mcX2 := mcDl^2 / mcVs;
        // v1.7d (E8): 1 df, so the upper tail is the lower normal tail
        // 2 NORMALD_CDF(0, 1, -sqrt(x)), as for McNemar (es_McNemar):
        // 1 - CHISQUARE_CDF is exactly 0 on the Prime above about 52,
        // and EP_MH_P read 0 where this keeps its digits
        mcP := 2 * NORMALD_CDF(0, 1, -(mcX2^0.5));
        mcX2c := mcX2;
        IF ABS(mcDl) >= 0.5 THEN mcX2c := (ABS(mcDl) - 0.5)^2 / mcVs; END;
        mcPc := 2 * NORMALD_CDF(0, 1, -(mcX2c^0.5));
    END;
    // ---- Breslow-Day: are the strata's odds ratios alike? ----
    mcBD := -1; mcPB := -1;
    IF mcK == 1 AND mcE <> 999 AND mcE > 0 THEN
        mcBD := 0; mcNB := 0;
        FOR mcI FROM 1 TO 2 DO
            IF mcM(mcI) == 0 THEN
                mcA := mcS(4 * mcI - 3); mcB := mcS(4 * mcI - 2);
                mcC := mcS(4 * mcI - 1); mcD := mcS(4 * mcI);
                mcN := mcA + mcB + mcC + mcD;
                mcR1 := mcA + mcB; mcC1 := mcA + mcC;
                // a stratum with an empty row or column carries no information
                IF mcR1 > 0 AND mcC1 > 0 AND mcR1 < mcN AND mcC1 < mcN THEN
                    mcFA := es_BDFit(mcA, mcB, mcC, mcD, mcE);
                    IF MIN(MIN(mcFA, mcR1 - mcFA), MIN(mcC1 - mcFA, mcN - mcR1 - mcC1 + mcFA)) > 0.000000000001 THEN
                        mcVa := 1 / (1 / mcFA + 1 / (mcR1 - mcFA) + 1 / (mcC1 - mcFA) + 1 / (mcN - mcR1 - mcC1 + mcFA));
                        mcBD := mcBD + (mcA - mcFA)^2 / mcVa;
                        mcNB := mcNB + 1;
                    END;
                END;
            END;
        END;
        IF mcNB >= 2 THEN
            // v1.7d (E8): Breslow-Day has K - 1 df for K strata in the
            // pool.  This module takes two strata and skips Breslow-Day
            // when one is dropped, so here K = mcNB = 2 and df = 1: the
            // upper tail is 2 NORMALD_CDF(0, 1, -sqrt(x)), kept to its
            // last digit (1 - CHISQUARE_CDF(1, x) is exactly 0 above
            // about 52 on the Prime, and EP_BD_P read 0).  A future third
            // stratum would need df = mcNB - 1 = 2: the tail is then
            // EXP(-x/2); with more, chi-square's own upper tail.
            mcPB := 2 * NORMALD_CDF(0, 1, -(mcBD^0.5));
        ELSE
            mcBD := -1;
        END;
    END;
    RETURN {mcE, mcLo, mcHi, mcCr(1), mcCr(2), mcCr(3), mcX2, mcP, mcX2c, mcPc, mcBD, mcPB, mcNI};
END;

// An estimate and its CI as text: "3.681  (1.84-7.37)", "undef".
es_SumTxt(stV, stLo, stHi)
BEGIN
    LOCAL stC;
    IF stV == 999 THEN RETURN "undef"; END;
    stC := es_FmtCI({stLo, stHi}, 0);
    IF DIM(stC) == 0 THEN RETURN es_FmtNum(stV, 3) + " (no CI)"; END;
    RETURN es_FmtNum(stV, 3) + "  " + stC;
END;

// One stratum's estimate as text, from es_StratEst's list.
es_EstTxt(etL)
BEGIN
    LOCAL etS;
    IF etL(5) == -1 THEN
        IF etL(1) == 999 THEN RETURN "undef"; END;
        RETURN es_FmtNum(etL(1), 3) + " (no CI: a zero count)";
    END;
    etS := es_FmtNum(etL(1), 3) + "  " + es_FmtCI({etL(2), etL(3)}, 0);
    IF etL(5) == 1 THEN etS := etS + " +0.5"; END;
    RETURN etS;
END;

// PAGE 1: the two strata as typed, each with its own estimate and
// 95 % CI.
es_StratTables(tbK, tbPg)
BEGIN
    LOCAL tbS, tbY, tbA, tbB, tbC, tbD;
    LOCAL tbE, tbH, tbNm, tbT, tbX;
    tbNm := {"OR", "RR", "IRR"};
    tbH := {"STRATIFIED TABLES", "STRATUM RISKS", "STRATUM RATES"};
    es_DrawHeader(tbH(tbK), tbPg);
    FOR tbS FROM 1 TO 2 DO
        tbA := esSC(4 * tbS - 3); tbB := esSC(4 * tbS - 2);
        tbC := esSC(4 * tbS - 1); tbD := esSC(4 * tbS);
        tbY := 30 + 82 * (tbS - 1);
        IF tbK == 3 THEN
            tbT := "PT = " + es_Cnt(tbB + tbD);
        ELSE
            tbT := "n = " + STRING(tbA + tbB + tbC + tbD, 2, 0);
        END;
        TEXTOUT_P("─── Stratum " + STRING(tbS, 2, 0) + " (" + tbT + ") ───", G1, 10, tbY, 2, esBLUE, 306);
        // column heads, then the two rows
        IF tbK == 1 THEN tbX := {"Case", "Control", ""}; END;
        IF tbK == 2 THEN tbX := {"Case", "No case", "risk"}; END;
        IF tbK == 3 THEN tbX := {"Cases", "PT", "rate"}; END;
        TEXTOUT_P(tbX(1), G1, 110, tbY + 16, 1, esGRAY, 60);
        TEXTOUT_P(tbX(2), G1, 175, tbY + 16, 1, esGRAY, 70);
        TEXTOUT_P(tbX(3), G1, 250, tbY + 16, 1, esGRAY, 60);
        TEXTOUT_P("Exp+", G1, 20, tbY + 28, 2, esBLUE, 80);
        TEXTOUT_P(es_Cnt(tbA), G1, 110, tbY + 28, 2, #000000h, 64);
        TEXTOUT_P(es_Cnt(tbB), G1, 175, tbY + 28, 2, #000000h, 72);
        TEXTOUT_P("Exp-", G1, 20, tbY + 42, 2, esBLUE, 80);
        TEXTOUT_P(es_Cnt(tbC), G1, 110, tbY + 42, 2, #000000h, 64);
        TEXTOUT_P(es_Cnt(tbD), G1, 175, tbY + 42, 2, #000000h, 72);
        IF tbK == 2 THEN
            IF tbA + tbB > 0 THEN TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(tbA / (tbA + tbB)), G1, 250, tbY + 28, 2, esGRAY, 66); END;
            IF tbC + tbD > 0 THEN TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(tbC / (tbC + tbD)), G1, 250, tbY + 42, 2, esGRAY, 66); END;
        END;
        IF tbK == 3 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P(es_RateTx(tbA, tbB), G1, 250, tbY + 28, 2, esGRAY, 66);
            TEXTOUT_P(es_RateTx(tbC, tbD), G1, 250, tbY + 42, 2, esGRAY, 66);
        END;
        tbE := es_StratEst(tbK, tbA, tbB, tbC, tbD);
        // v1.7c: es_EqTxt -- "OR₁ < 0.001  (...)", never "= <"
        TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt(tbNm(tbK) + CHAR(8320 + tbS), es_EstTxt(tbE)), G1, 20, tbY + 58, 2, esGRN, 296);
    END;
    IF tbK == 3 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("Total PT = " + es_Cnt(esSC(2) + esSC(4) + esSC(6) + esSC(8)), G1, 10, 196, 2, #000000h, 300);
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P("Total N = " + STRING(esSC(1) + esSC(2) + esSC(3) + esSC(4) + esSC(5) + esSC(6) + esSC(7) + esSC(8), 2, 0), G1, 10, 196, 2, #000000h, 300);
    END;
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

// The reading of the summary (v1.6).  The red "Confounding likely
// present" verdicts of v1.5 are gone: the 10 % change-in-estimate is
// a rule of thumb, so it is stated as one, next to the Breslow-Day p
// that says whether one pooled estimate is a fair summary at all.
// The difference is a share of the M-H (adjusted) estimate.
es_RuleTxt(rtK, rtM, rtY)
BEGIN
    LOCAL rtPc, rtNm, rtL, rtE1, rtE2;
    rtL := {"OR", "RR", "IRR"};
    rtNm := "" + rtL(rtK);
    IF rtM(1) == 999 OR rtM(4) == 999 OR rtM(1) <= 0 OR rtM(4) <= 0 THEN
        rtY := es_ALine("Crude or adjusted " + rtNm + " is undefined (a zero),", rtY, esGRAY);
        rtY := es_ALine("so the 10 % rule cannot be applied.", rtY, esGRAY);
    ELSE
        rtPc := 100 * ABS(rtM(4) - rtM(1)) / rtM(1);
        rtY := es_ALine("Crude and M-H differ by " + STRING(ROUND(rtPc, 0), 2, 0) + " % (of the M-H).", rtY, #000000h);
        IF rtPc >= 10 THEN
            rtY := es_ALine("By the usual 10 % rule of thumb: adjust.", rtY, #000000h);
        ELSE
            rtY := es_ALine("Under the usual 10 % rule of thumb: no need.", rtY, #000000h);
        END;
    END;
    IF rtK == 1 THEN
        IF rtM(12) >= 0 THEN
            IF rtM(12) >= 0.05 THEN
                rtY := es_ALine("Strata ORs homogeneous (Breslow-Day p = " + STRING(rtM(12), 2, 2) + "):", rtY, #000000h);
                rtY := es_ALine("one adjusted OR is a fair summary.", rtY, #000000h);
            ELSE
                rtY := es_ALine("Strata ORs differ (Breslow-Day " + es_PTxt(rtM(12), 3) + "):", rtY, esORNG);
                rtY := es_ALine("report each stratum, not one pooled OR.", rtY, esORNG);
            END;
        ELSE
            rtY := es_ALine("No Breslow-Day test (an empty row or column", rtY, esGRAY);
            rtY := es_ALine("in a stratum): compare the strata on page 3.", rtY, esGRAY);
        END;
    ELSE
        // no homogeneity test here for RR or IRR: the v1.4 heuristic
        // (one stratum more than half again the other), said as one
        rtE1 := es_StratEst(rtK, esSC(1), esSC(2), esSC(3), esSC(4));
        rtE2 := es_StratEst(rtK, esSC(5), esSC(6), esSC(7), esSC(8));
        IF rtE1(5) <> -1 AND rtE2(5) <> -1 THEN
            IF MAX(rtE1(1), rtE2(1)) / MIN(rtE1(1), rtE2(1)) > 1.5 THEN
                rtY := es_ALine(rtNm + CHAR(8321) + " and " + rtNm + CHAR(8322) + " differ by more than half:", rtY, esORNG);
                rtY := es_ALine("look for effect modification (page 3).", rtY, esORNG);
            ELSE
                rtY := es_ALine(rtNm + CHAR(8321) + " and " + rtNm + CHAR(8322) + " are close: one adjusted", rtY, #000000h);
                rtY := es_ALine(rtNm + " is a fair summary (no formal test here).", rtY, #000000h);
            END;
        ELSE
            rtY := es_ALine("A stratum " + rtNm + " has no CI (a zero count):", rtY, esGRAY);
            rtY := es_ALine("compare the strata on page 3.", rtY, esGRAY);
        END;
    END;
    RETURN rtY;
END;

// PAGE 2: crude against M-H, the CI, the tests, and their reading.
es_MHSummary(smK, smM, smPg)
BEGIN
    LOCAL smY, smNm, smS, smL;
    smL := {"OR", "RR", "IRR"};
    smNm := "" + smL(smK);
    es_DrawHeader("MANTEL-HAENSZEL " + smNm, smPg);
    smY := 30;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Crude vs adjusted ───", G1, 10, smY, 2, esBLUE, 306);
    smY := smY + 16;
    // v1.7c: es_EqTxt on the crude and the M-H estimate
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt("Crude " + smNm, es_SumTxt(smM(4), smM(5), smM(6))), G1, 10, smY, 2, #000000h, 306);
    smY := smY + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt("M-H " + smNm, es_SumTxt(smM(1), smM(2), smM(3))), G1, 10, smY, 2, esGRN, 306);
    smY := smY + 14;
    IF smK == 1 THEN
        smS := "95% CI: Robins-Breslow-Greenland variance";
    ELSE
        smS := "95% CI: Greenland-Robins (1985) variance";
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P(smS, G1, 20, smY, 1, esGRAY, 296);
    smY := smY + 16;
    IF smK < 3 THEN
        TEXTOUT_P("─── Tests ───", G1, 10, smY, 2, esBLUE, 306);
        smY := smY + 16;
        IF smM(7) >= 0 THEN
            smY := es_ALine("M-H χ² (1 df) = " + STRING(ROUND(smM(7), 3), 2, 3) + ", " + es_PTxt(smM(8), 4), smY, #000000h);
            smY := es_ALine("  with continuity correction: " + STRING(ROUND(smM(9), 3), 2, 3) + ", " + es_PTxt(smM(10), 4), smY, esGRAY);
        ELSE
            smY := es_ALine("M-H χ²: undefined (no variance)", smY, esGRAY);
        END;
        IF smK == 1 THEN
            IF smM(12) >= 0 THEN
                smY := es_ALine("Breslow-Day χ² (1 df) = " + STRING(ROUND(smM(11), 3), 2, 3) + ", " + es_PTxt(smM(12), 3), smY, #000000h);
            ELSE
                smY := es_ALine("Breslow-Day: undefined (an empty margin)", smY, esGRAY);
            END;
        END;
        smY := smY + 4;
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Reading it ───", G1, 10, smY, 2, esBLUE, 306);
    smY := smY + 16;
    smY := es_RuleTxt(smK, smM, smY);
    es_DrawFooter(1);
END;

// A log10 value as a pixel column of the forest plot (x 100..306).
es_FX(fxL, fxLo, fxHi)
BEGIN
    RETURN MIN(306, MAX(100, 100 + IP((fxL - fxLo) / (fxHi - fxLo) * 206 + 0.5)));
END;

// Drop or restore stratum tgS in the forest plot's pool (v1.6).  One
// stratum must always stay in: a tap that would empty the pool sets
// esFMsg and changes nothing.
es_Toggle(tgS)
BEGIN
    IF tgS < 1 OR tgS > 2 THEN RETURN 0; END;
    IF esDrp(tgS) == 1 THEN
        esDrp(tgS) := 0; esFMsg := 0;
        RETURN 1;
    END;
    IF esDrp(3 - tgS) == 1 THEN esFMsg := 1; RETURN 0; END;
    esDrp(tgS) := 1; esFMsg := 0;
    RETURN 1;
END;

// PAGE 3: THE FOREST PLOT (v1.6), modelled on TrendTest's trDrawOR.
// Rows: stratum 1, stratum 2, the crude estimate, the M-H estimate,
// on a log10 axis with ticks at 1, 2 and 5 in each decade.  Each
// stratum is a blue square whose AREA follows its M-H weight, with
// its own 95 % CI (Woolf for OR) as a bar; the crude estimate is a
// small black square; the M-H estimate is a magenta diamond (LINE_P
// outline) whose tips are its CI; the dotted line is "no
// association" (1).  A tap on a stratum's row (hit boxes 1, 2), or
// Up/Down, drops it from the pool: the M-H estimate and the crude
// row are worked out again from the strata left (es_MHCalc with the
// mask esDrp), the dropped row turns grey.  With two strata, one
// left means the M-H estimate is that stratum's own -- the page says
// so.  The EP_* results keep the full analysis.
es_ForestDraw(fdK, fdPg)
BEGIN
    LOCAL fdL, fdNm, fdM, fdE1, fdE2, fdI;
    LOCAL fdRE, fdRL, fdRH, fdRF, fdLo, fdHi;
    LOCAL fdV, fdD, fdMu, fdTk, fdX, fdLx;
    LOCAL fdY, fdW1, fdW2, fdWm, fdHs, fdCol;
    LOCAL fdS, fdXl, fdXh, fdRow, fdW;
    fdL := {"OR", "RR", "IRR"};
    fdNm := "" + fdL(fdK);
    fdE1 := es_StratEst(fdK, esSC(1), esSC(2), esSC(3), esSC(4));
    fdE2 := es_StratEst(fdK, esSC(5), esSC(6), esSC(7), esSC(8));
    fdM := es_MHCalc(fdK, esSC, esDrp);
    // the four rows: estimate, CI low, CI high, -1 = cannot be drawn
    fdRE := {fdE1(1), fdE2(1), fdM(4), fdM(1)};
    fdRL := {fdE1(2), fdE2(2), fdM(5), fdM(2)};
    fdRH := {fdE1(3), fdE2(3), fdM(6), fdM(3)};
    fdRF := {fdE1(5), fdE2(5), 0, 0};
    FOR fdI FROM 1 TO 4 DO
        IF fdRE(fdI) == 999 OR fdRE(fdI) <= 0 OR fdRL(fdI) <= 0 OR fdRH(fdI) >= 999 THEN fdRF(fdI) := -1; END;
    END;
    // ---- the axis: every CI that can be drawn, and 1; at most
    //      10^-3 .. 10^3; at least 0.6 decades; 8 % to spare ----
    fdLo := 0; fdHi := 0;
    FOR fdI FROM 1 TO 4 DO
        IF fdRF(fdI) <> -1 THEN
            fdLo := MIN(fdLo, LOG(fdRL(fdI)));
            fdHi := MAX(fdHi, LOG(fdRH(fdI)));
        END;
    END;
    fdLo := MAX(fdLo, -3); fdHi := MIN(fdHi, 3);
    fdV := fdHi - fdLo;
    IF fdV < 0.6 THEN
        fdLo := fdLo - (0.6 - fdV) / 2; fdHi := fdHi + (0.6 - fdV) / 2; fdV := 0.6;
    END;
    fdLo := fdLo - 0.08 * fdV; fdHi := fdHi + 0.08 * fdV;
    es_DrawHeader("FOREST PLOT: " + fdNm, fdPg);
    // ---- ticks at 1, 2, 5 in each decade; a label only where there
    //      is room (24 px from the last one) ----
    fdMu := {1, 2, 5}; fdLx := -100;
    FOR fdD FROM FLOOR(fdLo) TO CEILING(fdHi) DO
        FOR fdI FROM 1 TO 3 DO
            fdTk := fdD + LOG(fdMu(fdI));
            IF fdTk >= fdLo AND fdTk <= fdHi THEN
                fdX := es_FX(fdTk, fdLo, fdHi);
                LINE_P(G1, fdX, 34, fdX, 158, esGRID);
                IF fdX - fdLx >= 24 THEN
                    fdV := fdMu(fdI) * 10^fdD;
                    fdW := MAX(0, -fdD);           // decimals: 0.5 has 1, 0.05 has 2
                    fdS := STRING(fdV, 2, fdW);
                    TEXTOUT_P(fdS, G1, fdX - 6, 162, 1, esGRAY, 36);
                    fdLx := fdX;
                END;
            END;
        END;
    END;
    LINE_P(G1, 100, 159, 306, 159, #000000h);
    LINE_P(G1, 4, 96, 315, 96, esGRID);              // strata above, summaries below
    fdX := es_FX(0, fdLo, fdHi);                      // log 1 = 0: no association
    FOR fdY FROM 34 TO 158 STEP 3 DO PIXON_P(G1, fdX, fdY, esGRAY); END;
    TEXTOUT_P(fdNm + ", log scale", G1, 4, 162, 1, esGRAY, 92);
    // ---- the rows ----
    fdW1 := fdE1(4); fdW2 := fdE2(4);
    fdWm := MAX(fdW1, fdW2);
    fdRow := {50, 78, 114, 142};
    FOR fdI FROM 1 TO 4 DO
        fdY := fdRow(fdI);
        // the label column
        IF fdI <= 2 THEN
            IF fdI == 1 THEN fdW := fdW1; ELSE fdW := fdW2; END;
            IF esDrp(fdI) == 1 THEN
                TEXTOUT_P("Stratum " + STRING(fdI, 2, 0), G1, 4, fdY - 10, 1, esGRAY, 92);
                TEXTOUT_P("dropped", G1, 4, fdY + 1, 1, esRED, 92);
            ELSE
                TEXTOUT_P("Stratum " + STRING(fdI, 2, 0), G1, 4, fdY - 10, 1, #000000h, 92);
                fdS := "w = 0";
                IF fdW1 + fdW2 > 0 THEN fdS := "w = " + STRING(ROUND(100 * fdW / (fdW1 + fdW2), 0), 2, 0) + " %"; END;
                TEXTOUT_P(fdS, G1, 4, fdY + 1, 1, esGRAY, 92);
            END;
        END;
        IF fdI == 3 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P("Crude", G1, 4, fdY - 10, 1, #000000h, 92);
            TEXTOUT_P("added up", G1, 4, fdY + 1, 1, esGRAY, 92);
        END;
        IF fdI == 4 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P("M-H", G1, 4, fdY - 10, 1, esMAG, 92);
            TEXTOUT_P("pooled", G1, 4, fdY + 1, 1, esMAG, 92);
        END;
        // the mark and its CI
        IF fdRF(fdI) == -1 THEN
            IF fdRE(fdI) == 999 THEN
                fdS := "undefined (a zero count)";
            ELSE
                fdS := fdNm + " = " + es_FmtNum(fdRE(fdI), 2) + ": no log-scale CI";
            END;
            TEXTOUT_P(fdS, G1, 110, fdY - 5, 1, esGRAY, 196);
        ELSE
            fdX := es_FX(LOG(fdRE(fdI)), fdLo, fdHi);
            fdXl := es_FX(LOG(fdRL(fdI)), fdLo, fdHi);
            fdXh := es_FX(LOG(fdRH(fdI)), fdLo, fdHi);
            IF fdI <= 2 THEN
                fdCol := esBLUE;
                IF esDrp(fdI) == 1 THEN fdCol := #B4B4B4h; END;
                LINE_P(G1, fdXl, fdY, fdXh, fdY, fdCol);
                LINE_P(G1, fdXl, fdY - 3, fdXl, fdY + 3, fdCol);
                LINE_P(G1, fdXh, fdY - 3, fdXh, fdY + 3, fdCol);
                // a square whose area follows the weight
                IF fdI == 1 THEN fdW := fdW1; ELSE fdW := fdW2; END;
                fdHs := 2;
                IF fdWm > 0 THEN fdHs := 2 + IP(5 * (fdW / fdWm)^0.5 + 0.5); END;
                IF esDrp(fdI) == 1 THEN
                    LINE_P(G1, fdX - fdHs, fdY - fdHs, fdX + fdHs, fdY - fdHs, fdCol);
                    LINE_P(G1, fdX + fdHs, fdY - fdHs, fdX + fdHs, fdY + fdHs, fdCol);
                    LINE_P(G1, fdX + fdHs, fdY + fdHs, fdX - fdHs, fdY + fdHs, fdCol);
                    LINE_P(G1, fdX - fdHs, fdY + fdHs, fdX - fdHs, fdY - fdHs, fdCol);
                ELSE
                    RECT_P(G1, fdX - fdHs, fdY - fdHs, fdX + fdHs, fdY + fdHs, fdCol, fdCol);
                END;
            END;
            IF fdI == 3 THEN
                LINE_P(G1, fdXl, fdY, fdXh, fdY, #000000h);
                LINE_P(G1, fdXl, fdY - 3, fdXl, fdY + 3, #000000h);
                LINE_P(G1, fdXh, fdY - 3, fdXh, fdY + 3, #000000h);
                RECT_P(G1, fdX - 3, fdY - 3, fdX + 3, fdY + 3, #000000h, #000000h);
            END;
            IF fdI == 4 THEN
                // the M-H diamond: tips at the CI, 7 px high at the estimate
                LINE_P(G1, fdXl, fdY, fdX, fdY - 7, esMAG);
                LINE_P(G1, fdX, fdY - 7, fdXh, fdY, esMAG);
                LINE_P(G1, fdXh, fdY, fdX, fdY + 7, esMAG);
                LINE_P(G1, fdX, fdY + 7, fdXl, fdY, esMAG);
                LINE_P(G1, fdX, fdY - 6, fdX, fdY + 6, esMAG);
            END;
        END;
    END;
    // ---- the readout ----
    // v1.7c: es_EqTxt on the M-H and the crude estimate
    fdS := es_EqTxt("M-H " + fdNm, es_SumTxt(fdM(1), fdM(2), fdM(3)));
    IF esDrp(1) + esDrp(2) == 0 THEN
        fdS := fdS + ", both";
    ELSE
        IF esDrp(1) == 1 THEN fdS := fdS + ", stratum 2 only"; ELSE fdS := fdS + ", stratum 1 only"; END;
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P(fdS, G1, 6, 170, 1, esMAG, 308);
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt("Crude " + fdNm, es_SumTxt(fdM(4), fdM(5), fdM(6))), G1, 6, 182, 1, #000000h, 308);
    CASE
        IF esFMsg == 1 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P("At least one stratum must stay in the pool.", G1, 6, 194, 1, esRED, 308);
        END;
        IF esDrp(1) + esDrp(2) > 0 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P("One stratum left: M-H = that stratum's " + fdNm + ".", G1, 6, 194, 1, #000000h, 308);
        END;
        IF fdK == 1 AND fdM(12) >= 0 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P("Breslow-Day " + es_PTxt(fdM(12), 2) + ": are the strata's ORs alike?", G1, 6, 194, 1, #000000h, 308);
        END;
        DEFAULT
            TEXTOUT_P("Square size = M-H weight, bar = 95% CI.", G1, 6, 194, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    END;
    IF esDrp(1) + esDrp(2) > 0 THEN
