        TEXTOUT_P("Tap it again (or Up/Down) to put it back.", G1, 6, 206, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    ELSE
        TEXTOUT_P("Tap a stratum (Up = 1, Down = 2) to drop it.", G1, 6, 206, 1, esGRAY, 308);
    END;
    es_AddHit(1, 0, 34, 319, 63);
    es_AddHit(2, 0, 64, 319, 95);
    es_Foot("tap row = drop/restore, " + CHAR(8592) + "/" + CHAR(8594) + " = page, Esc = menu");
END;

// The stratified design srK (1 case-control, 2 cohort, 3 rates),
// v1.6: two grids, then three pages -- the strata, the M-H summary,
// the forest plot.  The EP_* results are set once from the full
// analysis (both strata), before any dropping on the forest plot.
es_Strat_Run(srK)
BEGIN
    LOCAL srM, srPg, srAct, srN1, srN2;
    IF es_StratGrids(srK) == 0 THEN RETURN 0; END;
    IF srK == 3 THEN
        srN1 := esSC(2) + esSC(4); srN2 := esSC(6) + esSC(8);
        IF srN1 <= 0 OR srN2 <= 0 THEN
            MSGBOX("Error: Person-time cannot be zero");
            RETURN 0;
        END;
    ELSE
        srN1 := esSC(1) + esSC(2) + esSC(3) + esSC(4);
        srN2 := esSC(5) + esSC(6) + esSC(7) + esSC(8);
        IF srN1 == 0 OR srN2 == 0 THEN
            MSGBOX("Error: Strata cannot be empty");
            RETURN 0;
        END;
    END;
    srM := es_MHCalc(srK, esSC, {0, 0});
    IF srK == 1 THEN
        EP_OR_MH := srM(1);
        EP_ORMH_CI := {srM(2), srM(3)};
        EP_MH_CHI := srM(7); EP_MH_P := srM(8);
        EP_BD_P := srM(12);
    END;
    IF srK == 2 THEN
        EP_RR_MH := srM(1);
        EP_RRMH_CI := {srM(2), srM(3)};
        EP_MH_CHI := srM(7); EP_MH_P := srM(8);
    END;
    IF srK == 3 THEN
        EP_IRR_MH := srM(1);
        EP_IRRMH_CI := {srM(2), srM(3)};
    END;
    esDrp := {0, 0}; esFMsg := 0;
    srPg := 1;
    REPEAT
        CASE
            IF srPg == 1 THEN es_StratTables(srK, "1/3"); END;
            IF srPg == 2 THEN es_MHSummary(srK, srM, "2/3"); END;
            DEFAULT es_ForestDraw(srK, "3/3");
        END;
        srAct := es_WaitKey();
        IF srAct == 0 THEN BREAK; END;
        IF srPg == 3 AND srAct >= 3 AND srAct <= 5 THEN
            // on the forest plot: a tap on a row, Up (stratum 1) or
            // Down (stratum 2) drops or restores that stratum
            IF srAct == 3 THEN es_Toggle(esHit); END;
            IF srAct == 4 THEN es_Toggle(1); END;
            IF srAct == 5 THEN es_Toggle(2); END;
        ELSE
            esFMsg := 0;
            srPg := es_TurnPage(srPg, srAct, 3);
        END;
    UNTIL 0;
    RETURN 1;
END;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MODULE 6: SAMPLE SIZE CALCULATOR
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

es_Module_SampleSize()
BEGIN
    LOCAL choice;
    choice := 0;   // v1.2: CHOOSE leaves its index unchanged on cancel
    CHOOSE(choice, "Sample Size Calculator",
        "Two Proportions",
        "Two Means",
        "Single Proportion (est.)",
        "Single Mean (est.)",
        "Back to Main Menu");
    CASE
        IF choice == 1 THEN es_SS_TwoProportions(); END;
        IF choice == 2 THEN es_SS_TwoMeans(); END;
        IF choice == 3 THEN es_SS_OneProportion(); END;
        IF choice == 4 THEN es_SS_OneMean(); END;
    END;
END;

es_SS_TwoProportions()
BEGIN
    LOCAL p1, p2, alpha, power;
    LOCAL z_alpha, z_beta, pbar, n;
    LOCAL y;
    LOCAL inputOK;

    p1 := 0.5; p2 := 0.3; alpha := 0.05; power := 0.8;

    inputOK := INPUT(
        {p1, p2, alpha, power},
        "Sample Size: Two Proportions",
        {"Proportion 1:", "Proportion 2:", "Alpha (α):", "Power (1-β):"},
        {"Expected proportion in group 1",
         "Expected proportion in group 2",
         "Significance level (e.g. 0.05)",
         "Desired power (e.g. 0.80)"},
        {0.5, 0.3, 0.05, 0.8}
    );

    IF inputOK == 0 THEN RETURN; END;

    IF p1 <= 0 OR p1 >= 1 OR p2 <= 0 OR p2 >= 1 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: Proportions must be between 0 and 1");
        RETURN;
    END;

    IF p1 == p2 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: Proportions must differ");
        RETURN;
    END;

    // v1.7d (E1): alpha and power are probabilities strictly between 0
    // and 1.  v1.7c never checked them, so alpha = 0 or power = 1 sent
    // NORMALD_ICDF a p of exactly 1 (or 0, or 1.5), which has no finite
    // z; what the Prime does then has never been seen.  Refused first.
    IF alpha <= 0 OR alpha >= 1 THEN
        MSGBOX("α must be between 0 and 1 (0.05 is usual).");
        RETURN;
    END;
    IF power <= 0 OR power >= 1 THEN
        MSGBOX("Power must be between 0 and 1 (0.80 is usual).");
        RETURN;
    END;

    z_alpha := NORMALD_ICDF(0, 1, 1 - alpha/2);
    z_beta := NORMALD_ICDF(0, 1, power);
    pbar := (p1 + p2)/2;

    n := 2 * pbar * (1 - pbar) * (z_alpha + z_beta)^2 / (p1 - p2)^2;
    n := CEILING(n);

    es_DrawHeader("SAMPLE SIZE: 2 PROPORTIONS", "");
    y := 35;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Parameters ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("p₁ = " + STRING(p1,1,0) + "   p₂ = " + STRING(p2,1,0), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("α = " + STRING(alpha,1,0) + "   Power = " + STRING(power,1,0), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    // v1.7c: es_EqTxt -- a power of 0.5001 reads "z_β < 0.001", never "= < 0.001"
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt("z_α/2", es_FmtNum(z_alpha, 3)) + es_EqTxt("   z_β", es_FmtNum(z_beta, 3)), G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 24;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Required Sample Size ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 20;
    TEXTOUT_P("Per group:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P("n = " + STRING(n,1,0), G1, 150, y, 3, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 24;
    TEXTOUT_P("Total (both groups):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P("N = " + STRING(2*n,1,0), G1, 200, y, 3, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 26;
    TEXTOUT_P("Effect size (difference):", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    TEXTOUT_P(es_FmtPct(ABS(p1-p2)), G1, 200, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    es_DrawFooter(0);    // single page: any key or tap goes back
    es_WaitAny();
END;

es_SS_TwoMeans()
BEGIN
    LOCAL delta, sigma, alpha, power;
    LOCAL z_alpha, z_beta, n, effectSize;
    LOCAL y;
    LOCAL inputOK;

    delta := 1; sigma := 2; alpha := 0.05; power := 0.8;

    inputOK := INPUT(
        {delta, sigma, alpha, power},
        "Sample Size: Two Means",
        {"Mean Difference (δ):", "Std Dev (σ):", "Alpha (α):", "Power (1-β):"},
        {"Expected difference in means",
         "Common standard deviation",
         "Significance level (e.g. 0.05)",
         "Desired power (e.g. 0.80)"},
        {1, 2, 0.05, 0.8}
    );

    IF inputOK == 0 THEN RETURN; END;

    IF sigma <= 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: Std Dev must be > 0");
        RETURN;
    END;

    IF delta == 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: Difference must be non-zero");
        RETURN;
    END;

    // v1.7d (E1): alpha and power strictly between 0 and 1, checked
    // before NORMALD_ICDF (see es_SS_TwoProportions)
    IF alpha <= 0 OR alpha >= 1 THEN
        MSGBOX("α must be between 0 and 1 (0.05 is usual).");
        RETURN;
    END;
    IF power <= 0 OR power >= 1 THEN
        MSGBOX("Power must be between 0 and 1 (0.80 is usual).");
        RETURN;
    END;

    z_alpha := NORMALD_ICDF(0, 1, 1 - alpha/2);
    z_beta := NORMALD_ICDF(0, 1, power);
    effectSize := ABS(delta)/sigma;

    n := 2 * sigma^2 * (z_alpha + z_beta)^2 / delta^2;
    n := CEILING(n);

    es_DrawHeader("SAMPLE SIZE: 2 MEANS", "");
    y := 35;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Parameters ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("δ = " + STRING(delta,1,0) + "   σ = " + STRING(sigma,1,0), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("α = " + STRING(alpha,1,0) + "   Power = " + STRING(power,1,0), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    // v1.7c: es_EqTxt -- an effect below 0.001 reads "(Cohen's d) < 0.001"
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt("Effect size (Cohen's d)", es_FmtNum(effectSize, 3)), G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 24;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Required Sample Size ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 20;
    TEXTOUT_P("Per group:", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P("n = " + STRING(n,1,0), G1, 150, y, 3, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 24;
    TEXTOUT_P("Total (both groups):", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    TEXTOUT_P("N = " + STRING(2*n,1,0), G1, 200, y, 3, RGB(0,140,60));
    es_DrawFooter(0);    // single page: any key or tap goes back
    es_WaitAny();
END;

es_SS_OneProportion()
BEGIN
    LOCAL p, moe, alpha;      // v1.4: the margin of error was `E`, the exponent marker / reserved e
    LOCAL z_alpha, n;
    LOCAL y;
    LOCAL inputOK;

    p := 0.5; moe := 0.05; alpha := 0.05;

    inputOK := INPUT(
        {p, moe, alpha},
        "Sample Size: Estimate Proportion",
        {"Expected p:", "Margin of Error (E):", "Alpha (α):"},
        {"Expected proportion (0.5 = most conservative)",
         "Desired margin of error (± half-width)",
         "Significance level (e.g. 0.05)"},
        {0.5, 0.05, 0.05}
    );

    IF inputOK == 0 THEN RETURN; END;

    IF p <= 0 OR p >= 1 OR moe <= 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: 0<p<1 and E>0 required");
        RETURN;
    END;

    // v1.7d (E1): alpha strictly between 0 and 1, checked before
    // NORMALD_ICDF (see es_SS_TwoProportions)
    IF alpha <= 0 OR alpha >= 1 THEN
        MSGBOX("α must be between 0 and 1 (0.05 is usual).");
        RETURN;
    END;

    z_alpha := NORMALD_ICDF(0, 1, 1 - alpha/2);
    n := z_alpha^2 * p * (1-p) / moe^2;
    n := CEILING(n);

    es_DrawHeader("SAMPLE SIZE: 1 PROPORTION", "");
    y := 40;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Parameters ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("p = " + STRING(p,1,0) + "   E = ±" + STRING(moe,1,0), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Confidence = " + es_FmtPct(1-alpha), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    // v1.7c: es_EqTxt, as above
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt("z_α/2", es_FmtNum(z_alpha, 4)), G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 26;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Required Sample Size ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 22;
    TEXTOUT_P("n = " + STRING(n,1,0), G1, 100, y, 4, RGB(0,140,60));
    y := y + 30;
    TEXTOUT_P("(Use p=0.5 for most conservative", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 10;
    TEXTOUT_P(" estimate if p is unknown)", G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    es_DrawFooter(0);    // single page: any key or tap goes back
    es_WaitAny();
END;

es_SS_OneMean()
BEGIN
    LOCAL sigma, moe, alpha;  // v1.4: the margin of error was `E`, the exponent marker / reserved e
    LOCAL z_alpha, n;
    LOCAL y;
    LOCAL inputOK;

    sigma := 10; moe := 2; alpha := 0.05;

    inputOK := INPUT(
        {sigma, moe, alpha},
        "Sample Size: Estimate Mean",
        {"Std Dev (σ):", "Margin of Error (E):", "Alpha (α):"},
        {"Population standard deviation",
         "Desired margin of error (± half-width)",
         "Significance level (e.g. 0.05)"},
        {10, 2, 0.05}
    );

    IF inputOK == 0 THEN RETURN; END;

    IF sigma <= 0 OR moe <= 0 THEN
        MSGBOX("Error: σ>0 and E>0 required");
        RETURN;
    END;

    // v1.7d (E1): alpha strictly between 0 and 1, checked before
    // NORMALD_ICDF (see es_SS_TwoProportions)
    IF alpha <= 0 OR alpha >= 1 THEN
        MSGBOX("α must be between 0 and 1 (0.05 is usual).");
        RETURN;
    END;

    z_alpha := NORMALD_ICDF(0, 1, 1 - alpha/2);
    n := (z_alpha * sigma / moe)^2;
    n := CEILING(n);

    es_DrawHeader("SAMPLE SIZE: 1 MEAN", "");
    y := 40;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Parameters ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 18;
    TEXTOUT_P("σ = " + STRING(sigma,1,0) + "   E = ±" + STRING(moe,1,0), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    TEXTOUT_P("Confidence = " + es_FmtPct(1-alpha), G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,0,0));
    y := y + 14;
    // v1.7c: es_EqTxt, as above
    TEXTOUT_P(es_EqTxt("z_α/2", es_FmtNum(z_alpha, 4)), G1, 10, y, 1, RGB(90,90,90));
    y := y + 26;
    TEXTOUT_P("─── Required Sample Size ───", G1, 10, y, 2, RGB(0,60,200));
    y := y + 22;
    TEXTOUT_P("n = " + STRING(n,1,0), G1, 100, y, 4, RGB(0,140,60));
    es_DrawFooter(0);    // single page: any key or tap goes back
    es_WaitAny();
END;

es_Module_Help()
BEGIN
    LOCAL choice;
    REPEAT
        choice := 0;   // v1.2: CHOOSE leaves its index unchanged on cancel
        // v1.6: fourteen items, the most the simple CHOOSE form takes
        // (Lessons Learned section 2).  v1.7: sixteen, so the menu now
        // uses the LIST form, CHOOSE(var, "title", {...}), which has no
        // such limit (ChiTable's and BayesTree's longer menus use it).
        // The Prime labels items 10 to 16 as A, B, C, D, E, F and G.
        CHOOSE(choice, "Help & Information", {
            "About EpiStats",
            "2×2 Table Guide",
            "Sensitivity/Specificity",
            "Risk & Odds Ratios",
            "Confidence Intervals",
            "Mantel-Haenszel",
            "Kappa Interpretation",
            "Variable Reference",
            "Entry Grid & Soft Keys",
            "Result Pages & Taps",
            "PPV vs Prevalence, ROC",
            "Readings & Fisher Bars",
            "Forest, Risk Bars, NNT",
            "Exact CIs, Exact OR",
            "Where the SE Comes From",
            "Back to Main Menu"});
        CASE
            IF choice == 1 THEN es_Help_About(); END;
            IF choice == 2 THEN es_Help_Table(); END;
            IF choice == 3 THEN es_Help_SensSpec(); END;
            IF choice == 4 THEN es_Help_RiskOdds(); END;
            IF choice == 5 THEN es_Help_CI(); END;
            IF choice == 6 THEN es_Help_MH(); END;
            IF choice == 7 THEN es_Help_Kappa(); END;
            IF choice == 8 THEN es_Help_Variables(); END;
            IF choice == 9 THEN es_Help_Grid(); END;
            IF choice == 10 THEN es_Help_Pages(); END;
            IF choice == 11 THEN es_Help_Prev(); END;
            IF choice == 12 THEN es_Help_Read(); END;
            IF choice == 13 THEN es_Help_Forest(); END;
            IF choice == 14 THEN es_Help_Exact(); END;      // v1.7: E
            IF choice == 15 THEN es_Help_SE(); END;         // v1.7: F
        END;
    UNTIL choice == 0 OR choice == 16;
END;

// shows about five, so the rest -- the Landis-Koch scale among them
// -- was never seen.  Each is now a drawn page (es_HelpPage, one blit
// from G1).  Every sentence of the v1.3 text is kept; the few glyphs
// the fleet has not verified on the device (a sigma with subscript i,
// an em dash, a kappa) are written out in ASCII.

es_Help_SensSpec()
BEGIN
    es_HelpPage("DIAGNOSTIC TEST MEASURES",
        {"Sensitivity = a/(a+c) = TP/(TP+FN)",
         "Specificity = d/(b+d) = TN/(TN+FP)",
         "PPV = a/(a+b)   NPV = d/(c+d)",
         "",
         "LR+ = Sens/(1-Spec)",
         "LR- = (1-Sens)/Spec",
         "Youden's J = Sens + Spec - 1",
         "",
         "Note: PPV and NPV depend on prevalence."});
END;

es_Help_RiskOdds()
BEGIN
    es_HelpPage("RISK & ODDS RATIOS",
        {"Risk Ratio (RR) = Re/Ru",
         "  Use for cohort studies.",
         "",
         "Odds Ratio (OR) = (a×d)/(b×c)",
         "  Use for case-control studies.",
         "",
         "RR = 1 or OR = 1: no association",
         "> 1: positive association",
         "< 1: protective effect",
         "",
         "OR approximates RR when disease is rare."});
END;

es_Help_CI()

         "coverage than Wald, esp. for small n).",
         "Exact (Clopper-Pearson) beside it: Help E.",
         "",
         "OR/RR: log method - compute CI on ln(OR),",
         "then exponentiate.  Its SE: Help F.",
         "",
         "If a 95% CI for OR/RR excludes 1, the",
         "association is significant at α = 0.05.",
         "For RD, the CI excludes 0 when significant."});

         "95% CI: Robins-Breslow-Greenland for the OR,",
         "Greenland-Robins (1985) for RR and IRR.",
         "M-H χ² (1 df): is the common OR 1?",

// v1.7: help E, the exact intervals and the exact odds ratio.
es_Help_Exact()
BEGIN
    es_HelpPage("EXACT CIs, EXACT OR",
        {"Exact (Clopper-Pearson) CI of a proportion:",
         "every p under which your count is in neither",
         "2.5 % tail of Binomial(n, p). No normal curve,",
         "and it never covers less than 95 % for any p,",
         "so it is wider than Wilson (conservative).",
         "mid-P counts half of P(X = k): nearer 95 %.",
         "Where: 2×2 Diagnostic tab, risk bars (orange),",
         "diagnostic module page 6 (tap a row).",
         "Conditional MLE OR (2×2, page 7): the OR that",
         "puts the mean of a at your a, all four totals",
         "fixed. R's fisher.test reports it; it sits",
         "nearer 1 than ad/bc when counts are small."});
END;

// v1.7: a help page with a colour for each line (es_HelpPage draws
// them all black): up to 13 lines, 13 px apart from y 30 (the 13th
// ends near y 196, above the footer).  Composed on G1, one blit.
es_CasPage(cgT, cgL, cgC)
BEGIN
    LOCAL cgI;
    DIMGROB_P(G1, 320, 240, #FFFFFFh);
    RECT_P(G1, 0, 0, 319, 239, RGB(245,245,255), RGB(245,245,255));
    RECT_P(G1, 0, 0, 319, 24, RGB(0,60,200), RGB(0,60,200));
    TEXTOUT_P(cgT, G1, 8, 4, 3, RGB(255,255,255));
    FOR cgI FROM 1 TO SIZE(cgL) DO
        IF DIM(cgL(cgI)) > 0 THEN
            TEXTOUT_P(cgL(cgI), G1, 12, 30 + 13 * (cgI - 1), 1, cgC(cgI), 300);
        END;
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P("any key or tap = back", G1, 8, 224, 1, esFOOT);
    BLIT_P(G0, 0, 0, G1);
    RETURN es_WaitAny();
END;

// v1.7: one term of the variance as CAS text: "1/12", or "2/25" for
// 1/(12 + 0.5) when a zero cell made every cell get 0.5 (whole
// numbers only inside CAS text, fence rule 1).
es_SETerm(stV, stH)
BEGIN
    IF stH == 0 THEN RETURN "1/" + es_IStr(stV); END;
    RETURN "2/" + es_IStr(2 * stV + 1);
END;

// v1.7: help F, WHERE THE SE COMES FROM (the delta method), with the
// CAS doing the calculus live:
//   the log odds of a row is g(p) = ln(p/(1-p)); the CAS differentiates
//   it (diff -> 1/p+1/(1-p)) and checks that this is 1/(p(1-p));
//   Var g(p-hat) is about g'(p)^2 Var(p-hat) = 1/(n p (1-p))
//   = 1/(cases) + 1/(non-cases), so row 1 gives 1/a + 1/b, row 2
//   1/c + 1/d, and the rows are independent, so the four add up;
//   the CAS adds the four fractions for the student's table exactly.
// The derivative and its check are the same for every table, so they
// are asked once per run; the fraction once per table (esSEK).  If the
// CAS does not answer, the page shows the textbook form and the
// decimal, with the "decimal" badge.  The table: the last one any
// module analysed (EP_table), or an example if there is none.
es_Help_SE()
BEGIN
    LOCAL hsA, hsB, hsC, hsD, hsH, hsT;
    LOCAL hsS, hsV, hsL, hsF, hsSE, hsOK;
    LOCAL hsC1, hsC2, hsD1, hsN, hsLs, hsB;
    hsL := EP_table;
    hsT := "Your last table";
    IF SIZE(hsL) <> 4 THEN hsL := {0, 0, 0, 0}; END;
    IF MIN(MIN(hsL(1), hsL(2)), MIN(hsL(3), hsL(4))) < 0 OR hsL(1) + hsL(2) + hsL(3) + hsL(4) <= 0 THEN
        hsL := {12, 5, 8, 30}; hsT := "Example table";
    END;
    hsA := ROUND(hsL(1), 0); hsB := ROUND(hsL(2), 0); hsC := ROUND(hsL(3), 0); hsD := ROUND(hsL(4), 0);
    // ---- the calculus: once per run ----
    IF esDvOK == -1 THEN
        esDvT := es_Cas("diff(ln(esq/(1-esq)),esq)");
        esDvC := "";
        IF DIM(esDvT) > 0 THEN esDvC := es_Cas("simplify(diff(ln(esq/(1-esq)),esq)-1/(esq*(1-esq)))"); END;
        esDvOK := 0;
        IF DIM(esDvT) > 0 THEN esDvOK := 1; END;
    END;
    // ---- the four terms, with 0.5 added to every cell if one is 0 ----
    hsH := 0;
    IF hsA * hsB * hsC * hsD == 0 THEN hsH := 1; END;
    hsS := es_SETerm(hsA, hsH) + "+" + es_SETerm(hsB, hsH) + "+" + es_SETerm(hsC, hsH) + "+" + es_SETerm(hsD, hsH);
    hsV := 1 / (hsA + hsH / 2) + 1 / (hsB + hsH / 2) + 1 / (hsC + hsH / 2) + 1 / (hsD + hsH / 2);
    hsSE := hsV^0.5;                     // the number: PPL's (rule 8)
    // ---- the exact fraction: once per table ----
    IF esSEK(1) <> hsA OR esSEK(2) <> hsB OR esSEK(3) <> hsC OR esSEK(4) <> hsD THEN
        esSET := es_Cas(hsS);
        esSEK := {hsA, hsB, hsC, hsD};
    END;
    hsOK := 0;
    IF DIM(esSET) > 0 THEN hsOK := 1; END;
    // ---- the lines ----
    IF esDvOK == 1 THEN
        hsC1 := "CAS: g'(p) = " + es_CasShow(esDvT);
        IF DIM(hsC1) > 46 THEN hsC1 := "CAS: g'(p) has a long form; it equals"; END;
        hsC2 := "  = 1/(p(1-p))";
        IF esDvC == "0" THEN hsC2 := hsC2 + "   (CAS: the difference is 0)"; END;
    ELSE
        hsC1 := "g'(p) = 1/p + 1/(1-p)  (textbook form)";
        hsC2 := "  = 1/(p(1-p))";
    END;
    hsD1 := hsT + ": a = " + STRING(hsA, 2, 0) + ", b = " + STRING(hsB, 2, 0) + ", c = " + STRING(hsC, 2, 0) + ", d = " + STRING(hsD, 2, 0);
    IF hsH == 1 THEN hsD1 := hsD1 + " (+0.5)"; END;
    hsN := "Var = " + hsS;
    IF DIM(hsN) > 46 THEN hsN := "Var = 1/a+1/b+1/c+1/d with these counts"; END;
    IF hsOK == 1 AND DIM(esSET) <= 40 THEN
        hsF := "  = " + esSET;
        hsS := "SE = sqrt(" + esSET + ") = " + STRING(ROUND(hsSE, 4), 2, 4);
        IF DIM(hsS) > 46 THEN hsS := "SE = its square root = " + STRING(ROUND(hsSE, 4), 2, 4); END;
    ELSE
        IF hsOK == 1 THEN
            hsF := "  = an exact fraction with " + STRING(DIM(esSET) - 1, 2, 0) + " digits";
        ELSE
            hsF := "  = " + STRING(ROUND(hsV, 6), 2, 6);
        END;
        hsS := "SE = sqrt(" + STRING(ROUND(hsV, 6), 2, 6) + ") = " + STRING(ROUND(hsSE, 4), 2, 4);
    END;
    // the badge: "exact (CAS)" only when both the calculus and the fraction came
    // from the CAS
    hsB := "decimal";
    IF hsOK == 1 AND esDvOK == 1 THEN hsB := "exact (CAS)"; END;
    hsLs := {"Why is SE(ln OR) = sqrt(1/a+1/b+1/c+1/d)?",
            "A row's log odds: g(p) = ln(p/(1-p)).",
            hsC1, hsC2,
            "Delta method: Var g(" + CHAR(57345) + ") is about g'(p)" + CHAR(178) + " Var(" + CHAR(57345) + ")",
            "  = 1/(n p(1-p)) = 1/(np) + 1/(n(1-p))",
            "  = 1/cases + 1/non-cases: 1/a + 1/b in row 1.",
            "The rows are independent: the four terms add.",
            hsD1, hsN, hsF, hsS, hsB};
    es_CasPage("WHERE THE SE COMES FROM", hsLs,
        {#000000h, #000000h, esMAG, esMAG, esBLUE, esBLUE, esBLUE, esBLUE,
         #000000h, #000000h, esGRN, esGRN, esFOOT});
END;

// v1.6: the two new help pages (items C and D in the Help menu).
es_Help_Read()
BEGIN
    es_HelpPage("READINGS & FISHER BARS",
        {"2×2 module, page 1: the same four counts read",
         "three ways.  Tap a tab (or Up/Down):",
         "Cohort: rows = exposure; RR, OR, RD, AF.",
         "Diagnostic: rows = test, columns = disease;",
         "sens, spec, PPV, NPV, LR, Youden, error rate.",
         "Paired: rater A by rater B (or before/after);",
         "kappa, McNemar (b vs c only), Yule's Q and Y.",
         "Page 4: every table with your margins, one bar",
         "each.  Red = yours; magenta = no more likely",
         "than yours: their sum is Fisher's p.  Grey =",
         "more likely.  Tap a bar (or Up/Down): its table.",
         "From Home: EpiStats2x2(a,b,c,d) -> Fisher p."});
END;

es_Help_Forest()
BEGIN
    es_HelpPage("FOREST, RISK BARS, NNT",
        {"Stratified, page 3: one row per stratum, then",
         "the crude and the M-H estimate, log scale.",
         "Square size = M-H weight; bar = 95% CI;",
         "magenta diamond = M-H and its CI; dotted = 1.",
         "Tap a stratum (Up = 1, Down = 2): drop/restore.",
         "Risk module, page 4: each group's risk with a",
         "95% Wilson CI; the magenta bracket is the RD.",
         "OR/RR = (1-Ru)/(1-Re): the OR overstates the",
         "RR when the outcome is common.",
         "Page 5: NNT = 1/|RD| rounded up: that many",
         "people (blue); red = the one extra case,",
         "green = the one case prevented."});
END;
